# Supamario MSC

`supamario.c`: Supamario's decompiled MSC (first commit: the v5 package's `supamario.mscsb` as
decompiled; a copy of Mario's, without the universal mechanics until `supamario_universal_v1` below). Built by
`tools/supamario_kame_build.py`, which replaces the `KAME_*` names and the Super God Fist's `GF_*` (`--msc-only OUT.mscsb`: only this file, without
the XV2 package, the effect values from `tools/supamario_kame_values.py` `SUPAMARIO_FX`).

## Neutral special: Kamehameha (status 0x152)

`kame_n_*` (end of the file), called from the vanilla status functions: `func_2256` (main: init),
`func_2257` (per frame: `kame_n_loop`), `func_2258` (situation: re-arms the air stall), `func_2279`
(end: the charge effects off). Hold special to charge (the throw frozen at frame 10), release (or
100 frames) to fire article 5 at frame 14 in status 0 / 1 / 2 = the charge level; the firing pose
(frame 16) is held while the beam exists. In the air he floats down while charging and floats while
the beam is out. The fireball
article (0) is unchanged, not generated by this move. Details: `docs/custom-effects.md`, "The move".

## weapons/

- `kamehameha.c`: the Kamehameha projectile (weapon kind 0x18B): fly / hold / finish statuses.

## Kamehameha fix 85 (`build/supamario_kame_fix85.zip`)

User: move the beam a very small amount forward after the first hit (moving opponents still get hit), let it
multi-hit a shield instead of getting stuck where it was shielded, make it unaffected by staling, and make its
hitboxes linger until it begins to disappear. Install over fighters_latest (the Kamehameha files of test 30).

- **Creep:** the hold moves forward `KAME_CREEP` (0.2) a frame (was 0).
- **Shields:** the hit status (3) checks the fighters around the head (`kame_near`: 10 behind to 20 ahead of it,
  their feet 8 above to 28 below it; Supamario stays behind it): only shielding ones (status 0x1B-0x1E) means a
  shield: it flies on (`kame_shielded`, its fly motion again from its frame: the status change takes its
  hitboxes), multi-hitting the shield as it passes; a later connection (`AttackModule(0x2727)`, reset with
  `0x2724` while it's only shields) with a fighter not shielding starts the hold there.
- **Lingering hitbox:** the body capsule (ID 1) covers the whole beam: from the head back over all the ground it
  covered, every frame of the flight (`supamario_kame_build.py fly_game`; it was the last 4 frames' worth). The
  hold plays the fly motion from the frame it hit at, frozen (`kame_hold_begin`), so the capsule stays the beam's
  length (not proven in game: that starting a motion part-way runs its script up to there); the finish / the
  dissolve removes it.
- **Staling:** neutral special's status data (`func_2250`) had the attack log `0x11192` (the fireball's special
  N); now 0 (the note sheet's "stale move negation": 0 = off). Not proven that the beam's hits take their staling
  from it.
- Built without the XV2 package: the MSC from `kamehameha.c` with test 30's KAME_* values
  (`tools/supamario_kame_values.py`, read back from fighters_latest's file: the unchanged source compiles to it
  byte for byte) + `KAME_CREEP`; the weapon game.bin = fighters_latest's with fly1-3 from `fly_game` (its first 4
  capsules checked equal to the installed ones); `supamario.mscsb` = fighters_latest's with that one word 0.

## Kamehameha fix 86 (`build/supamario_kame_fix86.zip`)

Install over fix 85. User: it deleted itself on a shield; the lingering hitbox for the whole flight was too much
(24 frames, a bit more fully charged); the multi-hits should force the opponent into the tip, where the finish
hits (held by the lingering body, they never got it).

- **Shield / hold restarts:** fix 85 restarted the fly motion at the frame it had reached; that most likely ran
  none of the script's earlier commands (the head's effects and its hitboxes at frames 0-1: a status change ends
  them), so on a shield it flew on with no head and no hitboxes and looked deleted. Now `kame_play_to` plays it
  from frame 0 at rate f, which reaches frame f in one game frame running every command on the way, then sets
  the rate wanted (`kame_catch_update`: 0 for the hold, frozen; 1 for flying on through a shield).
- **Linger:** the body capsule covers the head's last 24 / 24 / 30 frames (`supamario_kame_build.py LINGERS`,
  `KAME_LINGER_n`): 120 / 132 / 180 units.
- **Into the tip:** in the hold every fighter not shielding in the body (behind the head, up to the capsule's
  length + 8) is moved toward the head along the beam, half the way a frame (1.5-12 units: `kame_pull`); at the
  finish all the way, so the launcher at the head hits them. Through the slot position variables; Supamario stays
  behind the beam's start.
- Changed: `kamehameha.mscsb`, the weapon `game.bin` (fly1-3); `supamario.mscsb` as fix 85.

## Kamehameha fix 87 (`build/supamario_kame_fix87.zip`)

Install over fix 86. User: on a shield it only flew on at medium / full charge; the pull took Supamario into the
tip, not the opponent; double the beam's shield damage.

- **The owner:** `kame_find_owner` at the beam's spawn (status 0): the fighter at or behind its start (along its
  facing, up to 2 ahead) nearest it: Supamario. Left out of `kame_near` (the shield check: he counted as "a
  fighter not shielding" when near, so the beam held instead of flying on) and `kame_pull`.
- **Shield check window:** 32 ahead of the head (was 20: a big shield meets the head further out).
- **Pull:** every frame of the hold all the way to the tip (was half the way), so the finish's launcher hits them.
- **Shield damage x2:** each hitbox's added shield damage = its damage, rounded (`supamario_kame_build.py
  shield_add`): ticks +1 / +1 / +2, the finish +7 / +10 / +13.
- Changed: `kamehameha.mscsb`, the weapon `game.bin` (fly1-3, finish); `supamario.mscsb` as fix 85.

## Kamehameha fix 88 (`build/supamario_kame_fix88.zip`)

Install over fix 87. User: too much shield damage fully charged (x1.5 there); it still only multi-hit fully
charged; push the opponent into the beam only vertically (no teleport to the tip) and let the body's hits be the
final hit, so the opponent needn't be moved far.

- **Shield damage:** x2 / x2 / x1.5 by level (`SHIELD_MUL`): ticks +1 / +1 / +1, the finish +7 / +10 / +7.
- **Shield, every level:** the shield check looked around the head only (10 behind it); the lingering body often
  meets the shield first (behind the head), which counted as "no shield" and held the beam. `kame_near` now looks
  along the whole body (min(frames flown, linger) x speed + 10 behind the head), at the first contact and on the
  later ones while it flies on. (Whether this was the "multi-hits only fully charged" seen: not proven.)
- **Vertical push:** `kame_push` (replaces `kame_pull`): a fighter in hitstun along the body (not Supamario, not
  shielding) whose feet aren't within 2 of 7 below the head (a standing fighter's middle at the beam) is put
  there, its X kept. Every frame of the hold and at the finish.
- **Finish along the body:** `supamario_kame_build.py finish_game`: the launcher is a capsule from the head back
  over min(frames flown, linger) x the speed (one block per level and length; the MSC writes (level + 1) x 64 +
  frames into the level variable at the finish), so it hits the opponent wherever the body holds them.
- Changed: `kamehameha.mscsb`, the weapon `game.bin` (fly1-3, finish); `supamario.mscsb` as fix 85.

## Kamehameha fix 89 (`build/supamario_kame_fix89.zip`)

Install over fix 88. User (clip): it still only multi-hits a shield fully charged.

- **Cause:** native, not the MSC. A weapon's hit result reaches its singleton's hit method (+0xAC) as bits; the
  base fireball's (0x0320EE2C) removes the weapon on 0x13 and on 0x4 (unless the weapon's byte +0x46 is set),
  and 0x4 is the shield-block result. The beam's own method (`weapon_expand.py` on_hit) caught only 0x13, so a
  shield block went to the base method and the beam was removed before its MSC saw it. Fully charged, the hit
  presumably came in as 0x13 (its damage; its status 3 and the fly-through on the shield ran), hence "only fully charged".
- **Fix:** `extra_kinds.json` kamehameha `"on_hit_bits": "0x17"` (`weapon_expand.py`'s new per-article option):
  a shield block asks for status 3 too, which (fix 85-88) flies on, multi-hitting the shield. One word in the
  RPX: 0x04BB43FC `andi. r0, r5, 0x13` -> `0x17` (`weapon_expand_test.py` checks the method on bit 0x4).
- Changed: `code/cross_f.rpx` only (fix 79's plus that word); the scripts are fix 88's.


## Kamehameha fix 90 (`build/supamario_kame_fix90.zip`)

Install over fix 89. User: multi-hits shields at every charge now; shield damage slightly higher again; Kirby
should be able to take Supamario's copy ability.

- **Shield damage:** `SHIELD_MUL` x2.25 / x2.25 / x1.75 (was 2 / 2 / 1.5). The added shield damage is a whole
  number a hit: ticks +1 / +2 / +1 (the medium ticks up by 1), finishes +9 / +13 / +10 (were +7 / +10 / +7).
- **Kirby's copy:** Kirby copies Supamario (12th star missile hit) as Mario's copy with Supamario's Kamehameha
  as its neutral B: `fighters/kirby/specials.md` "Supamario copy" (Kirby MSC, `kirby_supa_copy_acmd.py`,
  `weapon_expand.py` "kirby_copy").
- Changed: `code/cross_f.rpx` (fix 89's plus Kirby's Mario copy article list, its header and two reserve
  entries), the weapon `game.bin`, Kirby's `kirby.mscsb`, `effect.bin`, `sound.bin`.

## Kirby's Supamario copy fix 91 (`build/supamario_kame_fix91.zip`)

Install over fix 90. User: Kirby T-poses in the Kamehameha and has no hat. Kirby's copy hats and motions load
for the fighters in the match by kind, and the loader skipped kinds `>= 0x3A`: without a Mario in the match,
Mario's copy hat and motions (Kirby's fireball throw, which the Kamehameha plays) weren't there. The loader now
takes Supamario as Mario (`fighters/kirby/specials.md`, "Supamario copy"). Changed: `code/cross_f.rpx` only (fix
90's plus 18 words). With the throw playing, the charge effects (Supamario's own, on Kirby's hand) show as his.

## Universal mechanics v1 (`supamario_universal_v1.zip`)

Install over fix 91. User: give Supamario all the universal changes (CLAUDE.md, `docs/universal-mechanics.md`).
He had none: the v5 MSC was a plain copy of Mario's (no reverse tilts, no tilts / down smash out of run, dash or
turns, no roll-off), and his body `game.bin`'s EscapeF had no speed (the animation's movement; no EscapeB script).

- **MSC:** `tools/universal_mechanics.py` on `supamario.c` (its patch mode, as every patched fighter: the reverse
  tilts in `func_247` / `func_248`; the run commands in `func_716`; `run_attack_update` / `dash_turn_attack_update`
  in the Dash, Run, RunBrake, Turn, TurnDash, TurnRun and TurnRunBrake loops; roll-off in the roll pre / loop /
  end scripts `func_362` / `363`, `370` / `372`, `381` / `382`, Fall's main `func_501`, and Hero's momentum,
  `rolloff_hold_update`, from the per-frame hook `func_82`), roll-off speed 2.2 / air multiplier 1.0 (Mario's in
  `universal_mechanics_all`: the defaults). Its three new globals are named here (`rolloff_shield`,
  `rolloff_slid`, `rolloff_cap`) and declared after the Kamehameha's, so those keep their numbers (62-66).
- **Checks:** the unchanged source, compiled with `SUPAMARIO_FX`, does what fix 85-91's `supamario.mscsb` does in
  every function (`msc_fingerprint`: 0 differ); the patched one differs only in those 18 functions, by additions
  (and the rolls' kinetic type `0xf` -> `0x6`), `--added 12`; the Kamehameha's functions are the same.
  `msc_stack_check`: 0 problems. `rolloff_hold_test.py`: as Hero's.
- **ACMD:** fighters_latest's body `game.bin` (test 24's, the last shipped) through `tools/rolloff_acmd.py --plain
  --add EscapeF EscapeB` (2.2): EscapeF +2.2 / EscapeB -2.2 at frame 5, cleared first, 0 at 27 (EscapeB created);
  both the same as Mario's in `universal_mechanics_all`; every other script unchanged.
- Kirby's Supamario copy: nothing to change (a copy only changes Kirby's neutral special; his roll-off is his own).
- Changed: `script/msc/supamario.mscsb`, `script/animcmd/body/game.bin`. Not tested in game.

## Ported sound ids v1 (`$S/ported_sound_ids_v1.zip`): his own sound bank ids

nus3 loads one bank an id (docs/native-notes.md "Fighter sound banks: one bank an id"); a port keeping its base's bank
ids silenced the later player with the base (or another port on those ids) in the match. Every bank of a new kind
now has its own id (`tools/sound_bank_ids.own_id`, `reference/port_sound_ids.json`), its rows keep the base's ids
and the executable (`tools/sound_alias_cave.py` v2) gives his banks those ids as aliases; nothing else changes
(soundlabeltable, scripts, MSC, sequences: the same).
- **Banks:** `snd_vc_Supamario` 46001 / `_ouen` 46002 (vanilla Mario's, which his are: tools/fighter_own_bank.py
  left them on Mario's ids 6001 / 6086); `snd_se_Supamario` keeps 2144 (already his own; alias 2144). Aliases: vc
  6001, cheer 6086. Check: Supamario vs Mario vs Squirtle.

## Super God Fist (dash attack, `supamario_god_fist_v1.zip`)

User: put Xenoverse 2's Super God Fist (Super Attack `030_GOD_CSA`) on his dash attack: a hitgrab (a hit catches the
opponent and the move goes on into the God Fist), the opponent spun around and launched a set distance (not by
percent), kept on the ground in a downed state it can't act out of until its stun ends; a hit on it then that
doesn't launch it stands it up. Install over `supamario_universal_v1` + `ported_sound_ids_v1` (either exe: no exe
change). Not tested in game.

### The XV2 skill (`tools/xv2bac.py`: BAC / BCM / BAS reader)

The files given (`030_GOD_CSA.zip`) were only the **BAC, BAS and BCM**: no EAN (the animations), BDM (damage /
reactions), EEPK / EMP / EMB (effects) or ACB / AWB (sounds). So the timing below is the skill's own; the damage,
the animations, the effects and the sounds aren't (see "Approximated"). `python3 -I tools/xv2bac.py 030_GOD_CSA.bac
030_GOD_CSA_PLAYER.bcm 030_GOD_CSA.bas` prints it all. XV2 runs at 60 frames a second, as Smash 4.

- **BCM:** four variants (activator conditions `0xC01` / `0xC02` / `0xC20` / `0xC40`): BAC entry 0 (on connect: the user
  plays entry 1, the victim entry 2), entry 5 / 10 / 11 (user 6, victim 7). Entry 4 is empty.
- **Entry 0 (the dash punch, 70 frames):** the skill EAN's animation #1; tracking (homing) on the target 0-10; the
  character's aura effects off at 0; skill effects 0 at 2 and 1 at 10 (with common effect 220); **hitbox 14-17**
  (BDM #160, two boxes: flags 8 = the connect one, and one dealing 9 guard damage / 90 stamina); skill effect 2 at
  40 (1 off), common effect 180 at 41; the skill voice at 0.
- **Entry 1 (the user on connect, 111 frames):** skill animation #0; **invulnerable 0-40, time x0.8 0-40** (the slow
  hold of the fist in the victim: 50 real frames); **ThrowHandler 0-40** (flags `0x2001`: the victim held at the
  user's bone 22 by its bone 17, no victim animation); skill SE cue 81 at 0; skill effect 2 at 17; common effect
  120 at 60; a BCM callback window 80-111 (the next move may chain from 80).
- **Entry 2 (the victim on connect, 58 frames):** common CMN.ean #179 (a held / struck pose), invulnerable and x0.8
  0-40 (in step with the user), ThrowHandler `0x200` to entry 3 at its end; screen effect BPE 45 at 2; effects at 3
  and 12 (skill 11); **pushed back 0.3 a frame 40-58** (Movement -Z: launched at 40); functions 2 (90) at 0-4 and
  0x50 (90, 5, 1) at 47 (unknown).
- **Entry 3 (the victim, launched, 77 frames):** CMN.ean #96 (the long tumble), a size-2 body hitbox 0-79 (it hits
  others on the way), falling (-0.11 a frame) 57-77, common effects 160 at 20 / 42 and 120 at 62. Entries 7 -> 8 ->
  9 are the other variant's victim chain (8: CMN #71 19 frames moving +0.25, then 9: CMN #96).
- **The clip** (`ffmpeg` frames): the punch connects ~frame 19 (0.32 s), the close-up holds the fist in the victim
  with a gold burst until ~frame 61, then the victim streaks off out of shot and is seen far away tumbling (legs over
  head, about two turns in ~0.3 s), then hanging in XV2's yellow-ring stun for the rest of the clip; the attacker
  spins in the air (a flip) meanwhile. "2 hits": the punch and the God Fist.

### The move

| Frames | What |
|---|---|
| 0-6 | dash in: TransN 0 -> 16 (the animation moves him) |
| 7-10 | **the dash punch**: 4%, angle 0, 20 set knockback (a flinch), a fist box on LHandN + one at his chest height |
| 11 | caught if it hit a fighter's body (`Hit_Check` kinds 2: not a shield): `gf` = 1, the victim's slot kept, Supamario invincible 45 frames (XV2: 0-40), the victim put 11 in front of him |
| 11 -> 40 | the MSC jumps the motion there in one frame (`gf_update`: the rate that reaches it, every script command on the way still run, as the Kamehameha's `kame_play_to`) |
| whiff | the punch's follow-through and back to Wait1, ends at 38 (vanilla Mario's dash attack acts from 39) |
| 40-49 | the hold: the victim 11 in front of him on the ground, 20 frames of hitstun every frame, no stage collision (an aerial victim is pulled down to him); the fist pulled back (the charge, a glow on it) |
| 50 | **the God Fist**: 11%, both frozen 24 frames (XV2's slowed hold), angle 0 / 25 set knockback (the launch is scripted), no DI / SDI; fire burst + rings + flash (stand-ins) |
| 51 + 30 | **the flight** (a thread, from the end of the freeze): the victim moved 55 along his facing (ease-out: it lands 66 in front of him) on an arc 14 high, turned -720 degrees about its side axis (two backward somersaults, ease-out, around its middle 5 above its feet), frozen in hitstop each frame (its own status can't move or turn it) and its controller ignored. Whatever its percent or weight |
| after | it drops the last unit; once on the ground: DownWait (0x50), held down 60 frames with the controller off (the auto get-up is 220) |
| downed | a hit that launches it (its status leaves DownBound - DownWait / DownDamage): controller back at once. A hit that doesn't (DownDamage 0x57): when the down damage is over it stands up (DownStand 0x54), controller back. Else after the 60 frames the controller comes back (it gets up, rolls or attacks from the down as usual). If it never lands (off the stage): controller back after 20 frames |
| 60-80 | Supamario's follow-through, then a back flip in place (XV2's attacker spins away), landing into Wait1 at 93; acting from 89 |

Totals: 15% (the BDM isn't there to say XV2's split). Supamario's own frames: whiff 38, hit 89 (+ the two hit
freezes). After the strike's freeze the victim flies 30 frames, lands in ~2 and is held down 60: ~92 frames, while
Supamario can act 38 frames after the freeze (frame 89): ~54 frames to reach it 66 away and hit it (XV2's stun
follow-up).

### How (files)

- `tools/supamario_godfist_anim.py`: the AttackDash animation (94 frames: 0-38 the punch / whiff, 40-93 the God
  Fist), posed from Mario's own animations (Run, his forward smash's thrust, JumpB's back flip, LandingLight, Wait1:
  played or cross-faded between keys), TransN for the dash, RotN for the flip's hop; fists (Attack11's hands);
  main / lhand / rhand / mariopt1, node order kept (vbn order). `tools/supamario_godfist_render.py`: stick-figure
  renders (side and front) for checking.
- `tools/supamario_godfist_build.py SUPAMARIO_DIR OUT_DIR`: the package (motion, the four attackdash scripts,
  `supamario.mscsb`). The game script's variables are `New_Var`s in slots 0x30-0x37 (`gf`, `gf_slot`, `gf_st`,
  `gf_sit`, `gf_phase`, `gf_cnt`, `gf_one`, `gf_lock`); the victim is target 2 (the last fighter hit) while the move
  lasts, then its player slot (targets 3-10, `gf_slot`), so a downed victim is still found after the move; `gf_lock`
  remembers whose controller is off (a second God Fist gives the first victim its controller back first).
- `supamario.c`: `gf_init` (AttackDash's init `func_272`) and `gf_update` (its loop `func_273`, first): the whiff's
  end, the jump, no cancel before `GF_IASA`. `GF_*` from `supamario_godfist_build.msc_values()` (also in
  `supamario_kame_build.py --msc-only`). One new global (`gf_jump`): 73 of 128.
- `tools/xv2ean.py`: an EAN reader + retargeting onto a Smash 4 skeleton (XV2_TO_MARIO bone map, world-space turns
  from the rest poses; Mario's vbn rest Euler order checked: X, Y, Z) and OMO writing, for when the skill's EAN is
  sent; **not checked against a real EAN** (none given): its self-test only proves reader / writer / retarget agree.

### Tests (offline)

- `tools/supamario_godfist_test.py OUT/fighter/supamario`: the compiled MSC in `rolloff_hold_test`'s interpreter
  (whiff ends at 38 in Wait / Run; the jump's rates 29 then 1; no acting before 89 even with the vl cancel window
  open) and the game script in a small ACMD interpreter with a simulated victim (the hold, the flight's offsets and
  spin every frame, controller off from the launch, DownWait, held 60 frames, a weak hit -> stands, a launching hit ->
  released, never lands -> released, a second God Fist releases the first victim): 25 checks.
- `msc_stack_check`: 0 problems; `msc_fingerprint` against the user's `supamario.mscsb`: only `func_272` /
  `func_273` differ, +2 functions. Every other ACMD script and every other animation byte for byte the user's.
- Both exes of `ported_sound_ids_v1` (A v45h, B v46g) have every command used (Thread_Start, Hit_Check, Status_* /
  Input_* / Hitstun_Set / Hitstop_Set / Rotation_Set / Target_Slot_Get / Body_State_Set, Extra_Special_Hitbox,
  New_Var, Hit_Reset).
- Animation: `supamario_godfist_render.py` sheets (no point of the head / hands / feet below the floor).

### Approximated / not done

- **Animation:** XV2's own (the skill EAN's #0 / #1, CMN #96 / #179) weren't in the files: posed from Mario's
  animations after the clip. Send `030_GOD_CSA.ean` (and the character's / CMN.ean if the skill uses them) and
  `tools/xv2ean.py omo` retargets them.
- **Effects:** no EEPK / EMP / EMB in the files: Smash 4's own stand in (the dash smoke, an orange flash at the fist,
  an orange glow while it's held, his forward smash's fire burst `0x341000E`, rings `0x100003A`, a big dash cloud).
  Send the skill's `.eepk` and its EMP / ETR / EMB / EMO and they go through `xv2_to_ptcl` / `xv2_to_nud` as the
  Kamehameha's did.
- **Sounds:** no ACB / AWB: his own (the dash attack swish and voice; the Kamehameha's start / burst from his bank
  2144, his forward smash's fire sound, the common heavy hit). The skill's ACB / AWB can be built into his bank with
  the next free ids then.
- **Damage:** the BDM wasn't there: 4% + 11% chosen (a dash attack with a guaranteed follow-up).
- **In game, unproven:** the motion jump (rate 29 for one frame) running the script's commands on the way; a
  rotation (`Rotation_Set`) staying on a fighter frozen in hitstop, and the spin's pivot (its feet assumed, kept
  around its middle; if it turns about its middle it wobbles 5 units); the victim facing him when hit (the spin's
  direction); CPUs obeying "controller ignored"; `Status_Set` DownWait from a damage status (its DownWaitU / D pick
  reads a flag the earlier status sets); the flight ignores walls (it passes through the stage while flying, lands
  on whatever is under its end point, or falls if nothing is). The CPU's attack data (ATKD) still has the vanilla
  dash attack's frames.

## Super God Fist v2 (`supamario_god_fist_v2.zip`): XV2's CMN files

Install over v1 (no exe change; either exe). The user sent XV2's `chara/CMN` folder (CMN.ean, CMN.bdm, CMN.bac,
CMN_M*.bac, the lobby / mission .ean / .bac; **no .eepk, no .acb / .awb, no ESK of its own**: CMN.ean carries its
skeleton). Not tested in game.

### What the BAC's animations really are
The BAC's animation items carry the EAN type (`xv2bac.py`): **0xFFFE = the skill's own EAN** (030_GOD_CSA.ean),
0 = CMN.ean, 10 / 11 = face, 9 = (entry 9's extra, unknown).
- **The attacker** (entry 0 dash punch: skill #1; entry 1 the God Fist: skill #0; the other variant's #3 / #2): all
  in the **skill's own EAN, still missing**. CMN.ean has no attacker animation for it (searched every .ean in the
  folder for CSG / GOD). The attacker stays posed from Mario's animations (v1's, timing adjusted below).
- **The victim**: CMN.ean **#179 GOK_SPA_CSG_D** (59 frames; the God Fist's own victim animation, from Goku's "CSG":
  held 0-40 doubled over and leaning back, then launched in a fast backward tumble 40-58), **#96
  CMN_DMG_SUBERI_AOMUKE** (78 frames: a tumbling slide along the ground ending on its back, face up), and the other
  variant's #180 (the _S of #179) and #71 CMN_DMG_BLOW_F.

`tools/xv2ean.py` reads CMN.ean as it is (its layout is now checked: names, frame counts, natural poses retargeted
onto Mario: previews in the package).

### The victim's motion, from #179 and #96 (`tools/supamario_godfist_xv2.py` -> `reference/xv2/godfist_victim_cmn.json`)
Per frame, from the pelvis: the travel away from the attacker (#179 -Z plus entry 2's BAC Movement 0.3 a frame
40-58; #96 +Z, played with the victim turned away as its travel goes on), the pelvis height, and the body's tilt
toward the travel (unwrapped). XV2: launched leaning back 57 degrees, **20.0 m** in 95 frames (10.6 m in the 18 launch
frames, 9.7 m sliding), **935 degrees** of backward tumble (1.4 turns in the launch at ~27 a frame, ~1.2 more sliding,
then flat on its back), a hop of only 0.4 m: it stays low and ends on the ground (the "downed" the user asked for).

v2's scripted motion follows it (`supamario_godfist_build.py` `xv2_victim` / `flight_frame`):
- **hold** (40-49): leaning back 29 -> 57 degrees (#179 0-40), frozen in place.
- **launch** (18 frames, #179 40-58 at XV2's speed) then **slide** (26 frames: #96's 77 at ~3x): 44 frames (v1: 30);
  travel scaled to 60 units (3 a metre: it ends 71 in front of him, its feet at 66); the pelvis height x 5 as its
  middle (kept off the floor); the tilt scaled 0.8 to end lying on its back (810 = 2.25 turns, face up as Smash's
  DownWaitU; XV2 ends 993 = on its back with its head toward the attacker, which the pure tilt can't give face up).
  #96 starts 1.1 m lower and 75 degrees back from #179's end: the height eased in over its first 6 frames, the tilt
  offset by the 75 (so the tumble never turns back).
- it lands lying, kept turned until DownWait takes over (then upright: the down animation lies it down).
- **timing**: Supamario's God Fist part is 6 frames longer (motion 100 frames, acting from 95, v1 89): the flight is
  over before he can move (the victim is placed relative to him, as the move's last hit: if he could run or attack
  during it, it would follow him or another fighter). Downed 50 frames (v1 60): ~52 frames for him to reach it.
- `tools/supamario_godfist_flight_render.py`: the victim's path as panels (a standing figure placed and turned).

### CMN.bdm
199 entries of 10 x 0x80 bytes (`+0` u32 id). **#160** exists: type 10, "damage" 100 (every CMN entry has 100: a
ratio, not the damage: the skill's damage isn't in these files), **stun 9 / 9** (u16 +0x30 / +0x32: the attacker's /
victim's hitstop), pushback 0.25 / 0.7 (f32 +0x28 / +0x2C), 0.1 / 0.04 (+0x40 / +0x44). The BAC hitbox's u16 +0x12 is
1 in the God Fist (0 in most CMN.bac hitboxes): probably the BDM file (common / character / skill), so #160 may be
the character's or the skill's BDM, not CMN's. Used: the punch's freeze 9 / 9 (`Extra_Special_Hitbox` HitstopFrames).
Damage stays 4% + 11%.

### Still missing
- **The attacker's animations**: `030_GOD_CSA.ean` (skill EAN #0-#3). With it: `python3 -I tools/xv2ean.py omo
  030_GOD_CSA.ean 1 model.vbn AttackDash.omo OUT.omo` (then fitted into the AttackDash timeline).
- **Effects**: `030_GOD_CSA.eepk` (+ EMP / ETR / EMB / EMO) for the skill's effects 0, 1, 2, 11 and CMN.eepk (CMN
  effects 120, 160, 180, 220, 230) — none in CMN/.
- **Sounds**: the skill's ACB / AWB (cue 81, voice cue 0) and CMN SE (cues 35, 53, 74) — none in CMN/.
- **Damage**: the skill's / character's BDM with #160 (or the CUS skill power).

### Tests
`supamario_godfist_test.py`: 28 checks (v1's, the flight now against `flight_frame`'s XV2 curves, the hold's lean, the
lying-until-DownWait and the rotation back to 0). `msc_stack_check` 0; `msc_fingerprint` vs the user's: `func_272` /
`func_273` + 2 (the MSC code is v1's; only `GF_IASA` 89 -> 95). Every other script / animation byte for byte the
user's. `xv2ean.py --selftest`.

## Super God Fist v3 (`supamario_god_fist_v3.zip`): XV2's own animation, CMN effects, the BDM

Install over v2 (no exe change; either exe). Not tested in game. New files from the user: the skill's
`030_GOD_CSA.ean` (+ `_F` female, `_MAM` heavy male), `030_GOD_CSA.bas` (only 24 bytes of zero padding more than
the first one: a newer BAS layout), `030_GOD_CSA_PLAYER.bcm` (byte for byte the first one), XV2's `vfx/cmn`,
`GOK_PLAYER.bdm / .bac / .bcm` (Goku's character files) and `CAR_BTL_GOK_SE.acb`.

### The animation (`tools/supamario_godfist_anim.py`, now from 030_GOD_CSA.ean)
- The EAN: #0 GOD_SPA_CSA_C (112 frames: the follow-up), #1 GOD_SPA_CSA_MISS_C (71: the dash punch, which goes on
  past the target), #2 / #3 their `_S` variants (the other BCM variant). #0 frame 0 is #1 frame 15's pose. The three
  bodies share the skeleton; `_F` differs from the default by up to 0.3 m (posture), `_MAM` up to 1 m (wide arms,
  low stance): the default (C) is used.
- XV2 is left-handed, characters face -Z: `xv2ean.Ean(smash_axes=True)` mirrors z -> -z (facing +Z, the right hand
  still the right; the skill punches with the RIGHT fist: BAC entry 1 holds the victim on b_R_Arm2). Retargeted bone
  by bone (world-space turns from the T-pose rests, fingers too: `XV2_TO_MARIO`), 5.04 units a metre (Mario's hip
  to foot / XV2's pelvis to foot).
- Timeline (BAC timings, 60 frames a second both): **0-18 = #1 0-18** (the hitbox 14-17, the BAC's: the fist is
  7.3-7.5 in front of him, 5 up, from 14), the whiff #1 18-70 twice as fast, **ending at 44**; the hit check at 18
  jumps to **46 = #0 frame 0**; **46-96 = #0 0-40 at x0.8** (the BAC's TimeScale: 50 frames, the fist held in);
  **96 = #0 40, the launch** (the God Fist hit); 96-167 = #0 40-111 (the flip away 40-60, the stance by 111).
- Root: #1's dash x the body scale to the contact (18.6), x0.3 after (a whiff slides 28 in all, vanilla Mario's 28);
  #0's own 2.5 m x0.5 (6 units, mostly in the flip). RotN: the pelvis' height changes, then the lower foot put on
  the floor on every frame (XV2 fights in the air: its dash leaps and its hold hangs) except over the flip (#0
  40-62, ramped over 4 frames: only lifted where the head (3), hands (1), knees (1.5) or feet would go under the
  floor). Checked: feet at the floor (1.56, the foot joint standing) everywhere but the flip; head / hands / knees
  never below 1.5.
- Previews: `supamario_godfist_render.py` sheets, and the clip beside ours (`preview/v3_vs_clip.png`): the clip's
  hold looks ~6 frames shorter than the BAC's 50 (its launch ~44 frames after the contact); the BAC's kept.

### Gameplay timings (v3)
| | v2 | v3 |
|---|---|---|
| punch hitbox / check | 7-10 / 11 | 14-17 / 18 (BAC: 14-17) |
| whiff ends | 38 | 44 |
| God Fist part from | 40 | 46 |
| hold | 10 frames + 24 freeze | 50 frames (the animation's x0.8) + 8 freeze |
| launch | 50 | 96 (#0 40) |
| hold distance | 11 | 11.5 (his fist 7.3-7.5 ahead + 4) |
| acting | 95 | 141 (the flight's end) |
| invincible from the catch | 45 | 75 |
The punch is on RHandN now (bone 0x14). The flight is v2's (XV2 #179 / #96 curves) less his own travel during it
(`flight_offset`: he moves 5 forward in the flip; the victim is placed relative to him).

### Effects (vfx/cmn)
The BAC's EEPK type 1 is **BTL_CMN.eepk**: its 220 = Btl_MHit (hit sparks), 120 = Btl_MHitSmk, 160 = Btl_PHitSmk
(hit smoke), 230 = Btl_RHit; 0 / 11 = Btl_FocusLineW (camera focus lines). Converted (`xv2_to_ptcl`, 64-pixel
textures, sets `PtcSupamarioGfBtl_MHit / _MHitSmk / _PHitSmk`, efc entries 0x2C-0x2E: ids 0x0341002C-E, +94 KB in
the 2.9 MB ptcl) and played at the BAC's frames: 220 at the dash's frame 10 (entry 0, replacing v2's orange
flash), 120 at #0 60 (the flip's landing, 116, replacing v2's dust), and on the victim's slide (entry 3: 160 at #96
20 / 42, 120 at 62: flight frames 25 / 32 / 39, at its position, from the game script's thread). Size x0.59 (the
converter's 8.5 units a metre against his 5.04). `xv2vfx.load_package(strict=False)` now skips the 15 vfx/cmn EMPs
its reader can't name (a Sphere emitter's parameter 3), instead of failing.
**Still missing**: the skill's own effects (030_GOD_CSA.eepk: skill effects 0 at 2, 1 at 10, 2 at #0 17 / #1 40,
11 on the victim at 12: the gold God Fist ones) and CMN 180 (#1 41: not in BTL_CMN.eepk) and the focus lines (not
converted: a camera effect). The gold stand-ins (glow, fire burst, rings) stay.

### The BDM
`GOK_PLAYER.bdm` has entries 0-16 and 142: **no #160**. #160 is CMN.bdm's (v2: stun 9 / 9, pushback 0.25 / 0.7,
the "damage" field 100 in every entry: a ratio). So the BAC hitbox's +0x12 = 1 doesn't name the character's BDM;
CMN.bdm it is. The punch keeps its 9-frame freeze; the damage (in neither file: the skill's power) stays 4% + 11%.
`GOK_PLAYER.bac / .bcm`: Goku's own moveset (913 BAC entries); the skill doesn't use them.

### Sounds
`CAR_BTL_GOK_SE.acb` holds 6 cues, all in memory (an AWB inside: no separate .awb needed): 0 nage_DashBasa,
1 nage_CatchDashi, 2 nage_Hit_Blow, 3 nage_GOK_Jump, 4 nage_GOK_CatchBashi, 5 nage_GOK_GiantSwing (Goku's throws).
It is Goku's **character SE** (ACB type 2: GOK_PLAYER.bac's type-2 sounds use exactly cues 0-5). The God Fist's BAC
plays none of them: skill SE / voice cue 0 (types 10 / 11: the skill's own .acb), **character voice** cues 81 / 35
(type 3: the attacker's battle voice bank), common SE cues 53 / 74 (type 0: XV2's common battle SE). Not added
(not this move's sounds); the stand-ins stay.

## Super God Fist v4 (`supamario_god_fist_v4.zip`): the launch and the rotation fixed, the skill's effects

Install over v3 (no exe change; either exe). User, v3 in game (clip `MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261114045029851`):
"the opponent isn't launched by dash attack after the initial hit", "their rotation is permanently stuck".

### What the clip shows
The punch (4%), the catch, the motion jump and the hold all work: the opponent (Marth) is put in front of him
and leans back. The strike connects: the training display goes Damage 11, Combo 2, Total 15 (4% + 11%). Then
nothing moves the opponent: it stays where it was held, leaning back ~57 degrees (the hold's last
`Rotation_Set`, XV2 #179's lean), recovers into its idle pose still tilted, and stays tilted. Supamario plays his
flip and recovers as normal.

### Causes
1. **The flight thread never started.** v3 started it at frame 97 (the first frame after the strike's 8-frame
   freeze) only if `Hit_Check(0, 0, 2)` reported the hit. The clip shows the strike dealt its 11%, so that check
   was false although the hit happened. Most likely timing: the kinds a hit adds to AttackModule `+0xC8` come from
   the attack's per-frame record pass (`0x02C09C0C`: it ORs `1 << kind` of the new records into `+0xC4` /
   `+0xC8`), and on the first frame after the attacker's freeze the script runs before that pass has handled the
   strike's record (the punch's check is 4 frames after its hit, so it always worked). The diag build logs the
   kinds at 97-100 (k 4) to confirm; the fix doesn't depend on it. With no thread, nothing moved the victim, turned
   it, put it down or turned it back.
2. **Nothing else zeroes the rotation.** `Rotation_Set` is `PostureModule(4, ...)`; no status change resets it (the
   clip: the victim back in its idle pose, still tilted). v3 zeroed it only inside the flight thread (on the
   down's first frame and some releases), so any way the hold ended without the flight (this one; Supamario hit or
   grabbed mid-hold; a new dash attack; a second God Fist on another fighter while the first lay down) left the
   last lean on the victim, and the hold's stage collision 0 too (it could fall through the stage).
3. (Not the cause, checked:) threads survive status changes and the motion jump (acmd_ext_cave.py: only the
   object's set-up, `WM_RESET 0x02BF5864`, drops them; the jump only changes the motion rate); `Thread_Start`
   with Flags 1 runs during the attacker's hitlag. My first guess, the strike blocked by the punch's Part 0 hit
   record (rehit 0: `0x02C10BC8` never frees such a record), is ruled out by the clip's 11%; the strike is on
   Part 1 now anyway (a record of its own, `Hit_Log_Clear(1)` before it), which costs nothing.

### Fixes (tools/supamario_godfist_build.py)
- **The launch no longer depends on Hit_Check:** at frame 97, still holding (`gf` 1), it always launches (`gf` 2,
  the flight thread). The hold is a throw: XV2's ThrowHandler doesn't wait on the hit either. The flight is v3's
  (target 2, as the hold, which works in game).
- **A watchdog thread** (`GF_WATCH` 0x46, Flags 1) from the catch: every frame it reads his status
  (`Status_Get(0)`) and `gf`: his status no longer AttackDash (hit, grabbed, KO'd: the script that holds the
  victim is gone), `gf` back to 0 (a new dash attack), still 1 past frame 98, or its 160 frames over -> it lets go:
  `Rotation_Set` 0 and `Stage_Collision_Set` 5 on the victim's slot (`gf_slot`, targets 3-10: not target 2).
  `gf` 2 (the flight thread has it) -> off duty. A thread is the only place: the MSC can't reach another fighter's
  PostureModule (no rotation variable for other targets), and a thread outlives his status.
- **Every release turns the victim upright** (`unspin`: by slot, `gf_spin` 0): the flight thread's releases
  (time, a launching hit while down, the downed stand-up, never landing) and its end, unconditionally (v3 skipped
  it when `gf_rot` said it had been done).
- **A second God Fist** stops the first one's flight thread (`Thread_Stop(0x47)`) and turns the first victim
  upright before it takes the new one.
- MSC: unchanged (the compiled `supamario.mscsb` is byte for byte v3's: only comments were added).

### Diagnostic variant (`diag/` in the package)
If anything still misbehaves, the diag build writes log lines: `S4GF k=<k> v=<value> c=<value x 100> n=<serial>`
in Cemu's log.txt (Debug > Logging > "Coreinit Logging"). Its game script and MSC write diag variables
`0x0F0003F0 + k`; the diag executables (`tools/diag_godfist_cave.py` over exe A v45h / B v46g: acmd_ext_cave's
`hvv_zero` at `0x04B97920` aimed at a stub at `0x04BAC000` that OSReports writes of `0x3F0-0x3FF`, then goes on as
before; the normal exes ignore these writes). k: 0 the kinds hit at the catch check (2 = a hurtbox), 1 the victim's
slot, 2 his motion frame at the hold's first / last frame (46 / 95: the jump landed), 3 target 2's rotation X at
the hold's first / last frame and at flight frames 1 / 22 (the thread reaches it), 4 the kinds since the Hit_Reset
at 97-100 (when the strike shows), 5 the flight thread started (the slot), 6 a hold release (1 his status changed,
2 a new dash attack, 3 still holding after 98, 4 time), 7 the victim's rotation X read back after each reset (0),
9 / 10 / 11 MSC: the jump asked / the rate back to 1 / the status left (the frame), 12 the flight's phase (2
downed; at a release, the phase it was in), 13 his status when the watchdog let go. `tools/diag_godfist_test.py`
(unicorn): through the real fighter `set_float` (`0x02F1DC04`) a diag write makes one OSReport call with the line's
values and keeps every register; reads, `0x0F0003D0`, a New_Var slot: no call. Both exes.

### The skill's effects (Goku's skill folder `002_GOK_CSG`)
The user's `002_GOK_CSG.eepk` (Goku's own God Fist, "the same effects") has exactly the effect ids the God Fist's
BAC plays as skill effects (EEPK type 5 / 3, skill 30): 0, 1, 2, 11.

| EEPK effect | parts (bone, scale) | BAC | Supamario |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `Csg_Crg.emp` (b_R_Hand, 0.6, -0.1 along it) | entry 0 at 2 | frame 2, on RHandN (follows) |
| 1 | `Csg_Bas.emo` model + `.emm/.emb/.mat.ema/.obj.ema` (b_R_Hand, 1.5), `Csg_Circle.emp` (0.6), `Csg_WindS.etr` | entry 0 at 10, off at 40 | frame 10: the model following him where the fist strikes (7.3 ahead, 5.2 up; turned 180 about Y: XV2's front is -Z), the circle on RHandN; off at 29 on a whiff (#1 40), at 64 on a hit (its 37 frames) |
| 2 | `Csg_Bas.emp` (0.6), `Csg_drop_ptcl.emp` (1.0) (b_R_Hand) | entry 1 at #0 17; entry 0 at 40 | 67 (46 + 17 / 0.8) on a hit; 29 on a whiff |
| 11 | `Csg_HitPt.emp` (the victim's b_C_Head, 0.4) | entry 2 (the victim) at 12 | 61 (46 + 12 / 0.8), at its head (bone 0: HOLD_X + 10 sin(lean), 10 cos(lean)) |

- Particles: `xv2_to_ptcl` at his 5.04 units a metre (the ACMD size is the EEPK scale), 128-pixel textures: sets
  `PtcSupamarioGfCsg_Crg / _Bas / _Circle / _drop_ptcl / _HitPt` (efc 0x2F-0x33, ids 0x0341002F-33); the ptcl
  +99 KB (3.09 MB). Skipped by the converter (no Smash 4 equivalent): the 4 `ConeExtrude` stripes (Crg's two
  `stripe_4ptcl`, Circle's two `stripe_ring00`).
- The model: `xv2_to_nud` (`--plain 1 --scale 5.04 --tex-base 0x15141B00`): `effect/supamario/model/EffSupamarioGfCsgBas`
  (efc 0x34, id 0x03410034; the red-orange spiral cone BasC + two wind swirls, 37 frames of obj / mat EMA; preview

- Not converted: `Csg_WindS.etr` (three thin swirling streaks; Smash 4 has no trace emitter and
  `xv2_trace_to_nud` makes straight beams). CMN 180 and the focus lines as before.
- `tools/xv2vfx.py`: an EMP's 8 header bytes at +0x18 may hold junk (Csg_Crg / Bas / Circle: XV2-Tools never reads
  them): noted instead of an error.
- Replaced: v1-v3's stand-ins (the orange glow on the fist while held, the charge flash, the fire burst / rings /
  flash at the launch). XV2 plays nothing at the launch on the attacker; the Smash hit effect and the big dust
  cloud at 97 stay.
- Approximated: the model doesn't follow the hand (it follows him at the strike point; XV2's follows b_R_Hand,
  whose axes aren't RHandN's); the victim's head is placed for a Mario-sized fighter; the Circle's ring emitters
  are turned with RHandN's axes.

### Sounds
`sound.zip` (the user's) holds only voices: `VOX/Battle/Chara/CAR_BTL_GOK_VOX.awb` (no .acb: no cue table) and 16
skill voice banks `VOX/Battle/Skill/CAR_BTL_S000_KMH_GOD_VOX.acb` ... `U801_SKMV` (Kamehameha, Kaioken, ... ; no

### Tests
- `supamario_godfist_test.py` now models the game's open rules (`Acmd.DEFER` the hit's kinds reaching Hit_Check

## Super God Fist v5 (`supamario_god_fist_v5.zip`): the flight without threads, effects on the fist, XV2's CMN sounds

Install over v4, **exe B** (`B_exe_v46g`; the user uses B only: no A files are built any more). User, v4 in game
(clips `MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261114112625883` hit, `...112819636` whiff, `...112939121` facing): "the effect
seems faint", "move it closer to the tip of the fist", "the release still isn't working", "some effects only
appear facing left". Then XV2's `CAR_BTL_CMN.acb` / `.awb`.

### The release: what the v4 clip shows, and the cause
Donkey Kong takes the punch (4%) and the strike (15%); after the strike he stays where he was held, leaning back
~57 degrees (the hold's last `Rotation_Set`), and goes back to his idle tilted (it reads as sitting) for the rest of
the clip (110 frames): no movement, no spin, no down, never upright. Exactly v3's picture, although v4 started the
flight thread without any condition and had a watchdog thread. So nothing either thread did reached him, while
everything the game script itself did (the hold's placement and lean, through the same target 2 and commands)
worked. The threads (`acmd_ext_cave.py`'s Thread_Start / thread_run) were only ever run in unicorn on a weapon's step
entry (`acmd_ext_test.py`), never confirmed in game; the fighters' step entry the cave hooks (`0x02E408B8`) is only
called from `0x02E40B90` when the global at `[0x102F6C04]` allows it (byte `+8` 0, or mode `+0` 2), otherwise
`0x02E40B24` runs instead, so a fighter's threads may well never run. Not proven which; v5 doesn't depend on it
(**found later, exe_v46m**: the gate only skips whole fighter frames; the real cause was the thread runner's check on
its r4, a stack slot outside the heap range in Cemu, which skipped every object's threads; fixed in exe_v46m,
docs/acmd-extra-commands.md "Thread_Start"):
- **The flight is in the game script** (frames 97-140 = flight frames 1-44, his motion running 1:1 after the
  strike's freeze), through target 2 as the hold. DownWait is asked at flight frame 42 (no hitstop / hitstun from
  then, so the status change isn't held up), the victim upright from 43 (DownWait's own animation lies it down: a
  rotation is only ever zeroed by a script), at 141 the drop (stage collision back) and the hand-off. He acts
  from 143 (v4 141).
- **The downed part is in the MSC**: `gf_tick()` (supamario.c), called by the per-frame hook `func_82` (as
  `rolloff_hold_update`, every frame in every status), through the position / input variables (MSC reaches them
  as ACMD does): gf_phase 1 (flying; his status no longer AttackDash -> controller back), 2 (DownWait, controller
  off 50 frames; DownDamage -> 3; out of the down statuses -> controller back), 3 (a hit that didn't launch it:
  back in DownWait -> DownStand, controller back).
- If the hold or flight is cut short (he's hit or grabbed: the script is gone), the controller comes back from the
  MSC at once; the rotation (MSC can't turn another fighter) is zeroed by the watchdog thread if threads run, and
  in any case at his next dash attack's first frame (gf_spin). A second God Fist turns the earlier victim upright and
  gives its controller back at the catch.
- The test's model of the game was wrong: it ran Thread_Start blocks. `supamario_godfist_test.py` now has
  `Acmd.THREADS` (False by default: the v4 clip) and runs the compiled `gf_tick` every frame (`MscTick`); on v4's
  build it reproduces the clip (no movement, -57.2 degrees left, 19 failures); v5 passes with and without threads,
  every `--all-models` combination.

### Diagnostic variant (`diag/`, exe B only)
`S4GF` lines as v4's (`tools/diag_godfist_cave.py` on exe B), now covering the whole chain: k 0 the catch's kinds,
1 the victim's slot (at the catch, then target 2's slot at flight frames 1 / 22 / 43: still found), 2 his motion
frame at the hold's first / last frame, 3 target 2's rotation X at the hold's first / last frame and at flight
frames 1 / 22 / 43 (spun, spun, 0), 4 the kinds at 97-100 (when the strike's hit shows), 5 the launch (slot),
6 a release by the watchdog (reason), 7 the rotation read back after a reset, **8 every frame the watchdog thread
runs (does a thread run in game at all?)**, 9 / 10 / 11 MSC jump / rate 1 / status left, 12 the hand-off (2) and
gf_tick's phase 3 / release (99), 13 the status that made gf_tick let go.

### Effects
- **On the fist** (user): effects 0, 1 (the model and Csg_Circle) and 2 follow RHandN (External_Graphic on bone
  0x14, XV2's follow b_R_Hand) 1.2 along its +X (at the punch +X points forward, the fingers 0.74 along it; the
  charge 0.7); the model turned Y -90 (its front, XV2's -Z, onto the hand's +X). v4 put the model on bone 0 where the
  fist strikes and the particles at the hand's origin.
- **Brighter** (user: "faint"): XV2 draws these with glare (the EMMs' GlareCol 1.5-2.0, bloom Smash 4 doesn't have):
  every converted skill emitter's `color_scale` x2 and `alpha_init` x1.5 (capped at 1), the model's colour gain x2
  (`--tint`). The emitters' colours themselves are XV2's (orange / gold: Csg_Crg's 1.0, 0.8, 0.2 ring, 1.0, 0.5,
  0.37 haze, ...).
- **Facing**: new test check (`facing_tests`): every effect of the move placed alike facing left and right (bone 0:
  no X (depth) offset, turns only about Y; the fist: offsets only along its +X). v4 passes it too (Mario's own
  forward smash fire is `Bone 0, Y 7, Z 15`: Z forward), so the placement can't make an effect show one way only.
  In the facing clip the teal clouds that pass near him are the stage's background (they fly across before he
  moves and far from him); to compare the two facings, a plain stage. Not added to port_check (not shown to be a
  general trap).
- Whiff clip: the dash, punch and slide play as the frame table says (the whiff ends 44 frames in); the effects
  were barely visible there (the faintness above).

### Sounds (XV2's `CAR_BTL_CMN.acb` / `.awb`)
`tools/xv2acb.py` (new: CRI @UTF tables, cue -> sequence -> track -> noteOn -> synth -> waveform, AFS2) lists /
extracts the cues; `tools/xv2acb_decode.mjs` decodes the HCA with hca.js (`npm i --ignore-scripts hca.js`; the CMN
HCAs carry `ciph` type 0: no key). 234 cues, 183 waveforms (181 in the ACB's own AWB, 2 streamed), all decoded
but the placeholder the 44 empty cues share (`m143`). Which cues the move plays:
- **053 Hit_LL**: BAC entry 2 (the victim, on the catch) `common SE cue 53` at 0. Ours at frame 46 (the God Fist
  part's first frame) on the hit path. 1.92 s.
- **106 Down**: not in the God Fist's BAC; CMN.bac entry 78 (the generic reaction playing CMN.ean #96
  CMN_DMG_SUBERI_AOMUKE, the slide our victim's entry 3 plays) plays it at its frame 1 (entries 76-80, 750-753 too,
  for the other knockdown slides). Ours when the slide starts: frame 115 (flight frame 19 = #96 frame 1). Inferred.
- Not CMN: entry 0's skill SE cue 0 (the skill's own SE bank: not in the files), the voices (entry 0 skill voice
  cue 0, entry 1 character voice cue 81: left out, the user). CMN.bdm #160 (the punch) has no hit sound (its cue
  field +0x0E is -1; +0x0C the ACB type 0, +0x10 the hit effect); #0 (the victim's body box) neither.
- `74 kidan_hit01` (entry 6) is the other BCM variant's (not this one).
- The XV2 clip's audio, cross-correlated with all 182 decoded waveforms: nothing above 0.23 (the clip's mix of
  music / voices hides them), so it neither confirms nor adds a cue.

Added to `snd_se_Supamario` (BINF 2144, unchanged) as one-shot tones 90 / 91 (`snd_se_Supamario_gf_hit_ll`,
`_gf_down`: IDSP 48 kHz mono, -0.5 dBFS peak, volume 0 dB: RMS over the first 0.5 s -11.9 / -12.5 dB, in the
Kamehameha tones' -10.6 to -15), labels 143 / 144 of soundlabeltable row 3249: sound ids **`0x0CB1008F`** Hit_LL,
**`0x0CB10090`** Down. The table: the user's latest (`kirby_hero_copy_fix_v1` B), only row 3249 grown. The sound
script: Hit_LL at 46 in place of the Kamehameha start stand-in, the heavy hit at the strike kept (Smash's), the
Kamehameha fire / burst stand-ins at the strike gone (XV2 plays nothing there), Down at 115.

### Tests
`supamario_godfist_test.py OUT/fighter/supamario --all-models`: 0 problems (normal and diag builds; all 8
combinations of the open game rules: kinds deferred or not, Part records blocking or not, threads running or not); on v4's build 19 failures (its clip). `msc_stack_check`: 0; `msc_fingerprint`
v4 vs v5 (`--added 2`: gf_release, gf_tick): `func_82` (the call) and `func_2320` (GF_IASA 143) differ.

## Super God Fist v6 (`supamario_god_fist_v6.zip`): a real knockdown, the effects on bone 0, bigger

Install over v5, exe B (no exe change). User, v5 in game (clips `MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261114115719714` hit,
`...115708933` whiff): "The release works. The opponent doesn't actually go get forced to lay down since they never
go into tumble. The effect is too close to Supamario when it should be further from the tip of his fist. The effect
seems mirrored vertically depending on facing direction. Also the effect is too small by comparison to Supamario."

### The knockdown
The v5 hit clip: Donkey Kong flies and spins away, lands on his feet, stands, and (the CPU) jumps ~1.4 s after the
strike. v5 asked for DownWait (0x50) at flight frame 42, with the victim still in the air (its stage collision is
off while it's flown): DownWait's own loop (`func_963`, the common status every fighter's MSC carries) starts with
`if (global18 == 0x2) func_16(0xe)`: in the air -> Fall. So it fell, landed on its feet, and gf_tick, seeing it out
of the down statuses, gave the controller back. Asking for the down statuses directly isn't safe either: DownBound
(0x4D) and DownSpot (0x4E) share their main (`func_921` -> `func_960`), which plays the motion in int `0x11000010`,
and no MSC sets that int (the native damage code does). The way the game itself knocks a fighter down: a tumbling
fighter (DamageFly 0x47 / DamageFall 0x4C, whose loop `func_816` goes to DownBound when it touches the ground with
the down term `0x1e00000d` on; DownBound's loop `func_922` / `func_961` then ends in DownWait) that doesn't tech.

v6 ends the flight with a real launch: at frame 141 the victim 3 above the ground, its stage collision back (air),
upright, and a hitbox on it (Part 2, a hit record of its own; 1%, the strike now 10%: 15 in all as before; angle 20,
set knockback WBKB 60 / BKB 30 / KBG 100: ~90 knockback units for weight 100, 86 for Donkey Kong's 127, over Smash
4's tumble threshold of 80; no SDI, DI x0.001) puts it in DamageFly; it lands within a few frames and the game knocks
it down its own way (DownBound, then DownWait, face up / down its own pick). gf_tick waits for the down (gf_phase 4:
damage / tumble statuses 0x45-0x4C keep it waiting, up to 60 frames; anything else, a tech or landing on its feet,
releases), then holds it down 50 frames (phase 2) as before; a non-launching hit still stands it up.
- The controller is off from the launch (Input_Set 21), so a player can't tech; a CPU's AI may not go through the
  controller (unconfirmed): a CPU tech is released at once (the clip's CPU jumped only after v5's release).

### Effects
- **Facing**: v5 put them on RHandN with an offset along its local +X and the model turned Y -90 about that bone. At
  the punch RHandN's +Y points down (-0.96 world up), so the model was upside down relative to his body, and a body
  bone's frame doesn't come out the same on screen for both facings (the user: "mirrored vertically depending on
  facing"). v6 puts every skill effect on bone 0 (TopN: Z forward along his facing, Y up in both facings, the axes
  Mario's own forward smash fire uses, `Bone 0, Y 7, Z 15`) where his fist is (from the built animation) and turns
  only about Y (0 / 180: the model's front, XV2's -Z, onto his facing). They follow him (External_Graphic), not the
  hand. The test's facing check is stricter: bone 0 effects may only sit at a height / ahead and turn about Y by 0 /
  180; any other bone gets no offset and no turn. v5 fails it (7 effects).
- **Further out**: the model's origin 5 past the fist tip (its front 1.6 x size beyond that, its swirl and wind
  trailing back over the fist), the rings 2, the charge / burst 1.2 / 1.5 past the tip (v5: 1.2 along the hand,
  inside the cone).
- **Bigger**: x1.5 on every skill effect's size: XV2's sizes are in metres of Goku (head bone 1.55 m up), Supamario's
  head bone is 9.67 units up, 6.24 units a metre (x1.24 on the 5.04 they were converted at, his hip-to-foot scale),
  with XV2's bloom halo (none in Smash 4) on top.

### Tests
`supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems (normal and diag builds). The model of the game: a DownWait
asked in the air falls (func_963), a tumbling fighter landing goes DownBound -> DownWait, the end's hit launches
(Part 2). On v5's build: 12 failures (no knockdown: the clip; the effects' facing). `msc_stack_check` 0;
`msc_fingerprint` v5 vs v6: only `func_2322` (gf_tick, phase 4). Unchanged from v5: the motion, sound / expression
scripts, ptcl / efc / model, sound bank, soundlabeltable.

## Super God Fist v7 (`supamario_god_fist_v7.zip`): the down without a hit, facing-safe emitters, bigger and brighter

Install over v6, exe B (the user's current one, `exe_v46i`; no exe change). User, v6 in game (a diag `log.txt`, two
screenshots): "still too small, not far enough down Supamario's arm, vertical effects are mirrored based on facing
direction and not vibrant enough. The move didn't hit correctly unless I used the diagnostic build. ... it shouldn't
allow them to tech during the hitstun of the move and shouldn't knock them back much further than where they were
when they spun. The additional hit shouldn't do damage but force them into their laying down state once they land
... if they are still in hitstun"; then "make them carry a small amount of momentum into their knocked down state so
it gives the impression of sliding slightly".

### The diag log (5 God Fists)
Every run the same chain: `k=0 v=2` (the catch: a hurtbox), `k=1 v=2` (slot 2), `k=9 v=17` (the MSC's jump asked at
17), `k=2 46` / `k=3 -29` (the hold's first frame and lean), `k=10 46` (rate 1), `k=2 94` / `k=3 -57` (the hold's
end), `k=4 v=2` (the strike's kinds at 97: seen; v4's "not yet seen" guess is ruled out), `k=5` (the launch),
`k=3 -64 / -415 / -810` with `k=1 2` (the flight spinning the victim through target 2, found all along), then at
the hand-off `k=12 v=4` and at once `k=13 v=14`, `k=12 v=99`: gf_tick let it go because the victim was already in
**Fall** (0xE): once the flight stopped freezing it (v6: no hitstop from flight frame 42), its ground Damage status
ran in the air and fell. The 1% end hit then launched a free fighter: that is the "knocked much further". In the
4th run (`k=11 v=146`, `k=13 v=76`) he acted at frame 146 and the victim, launched, was still tumbling (DamageFall
0x4C) after gf_tick's 60 frames: released by time. **No `k=8` line in any run**: the watchdog thread never ran
once, a fighter's ACMD threads don't run in game (v4's finding confirmed); v7 drops the watchdog (each God Fist
left a never-running thread registered: 32 slots in all). **Cause found and fixed in exe_v46m** (no thread of any
object ran up to exe_v46l: the runner's check on its stack-slot argument failed in Cemu; docs/acmd-extra-commands.md
"Thread_Start"); the God Fist keeps its thread-free design, which works with or without threads.

### "Didn't hit correctly unless the diagnostic build"
Not found in the files: the normal and diag game scripts differ only by the log commands (SET_FLOAT / SUB_FLOAT_2
to 0x3F0-0x3FF, Hit_Get / Rotation_Get / Target_Slot_Get / Motion_Get reads, the watchdog's log line), the MSC only
by its `//GFD` writes. The executables: v6's diag was `exe_v46g` (+ ids) + the diag stub; the user's normal one is
`exe_v46h` / `exe_v46i`. `exe_v46g_ids` -> `exe_v46h` changes 48 code words, all Kirby's (the copy-held article's
`0x02B02184`, the sword cave `0x04BAF200-0x04BAF244`, zero before, and its `0x04BAF680-0x04BAF6F8` rewritten);
`exe_v46h` -> `exe_v46i` 8 data words (`0x103868C4-0x103868E4`, zero before, Kirby's motion table). None is in or
near acmd_ext_cave (`0x04B90000`-`0x04B9xxxx`, threads `0x04B98000`), position_vars_cave (`0x04B78000`-`0x04B7C000`)
or the diag stub (`0x04BAC000`, zero in all three). So nothing in the exes or the scripts makes the normal build
behave differently; what the user saw is most likely v6's Fall / free launch above, which varies with timing. The
v7 diag executable is built on `exe_v46i` (the user's), so diag and normal now differ only by the stub.

### The down (v7)
- No hit at the end (v6's 1% launch gone): the strike is 11% again, 15% in all.
- The flight keeps the victim frozen (hitstop 2 each frame, hitstun 40) through flight frame 42, where it's asked
  into **DamageFall** (0x4C, the game's tumble: its main `func_815` turns the down term `0x1e00000d` on), so its
  ground Damage status never runs in the air. The strike freezes the victim 2 frames longer than Supamario (10 / 8)
  so it's still frozen when the flight's first frame freezes it again.
- At 141 it's 1 above the ground, stage collision back (air), upright (DamageFall's own animation), carrying 0.6
  a frame along his facing (the flight's end speed: XV2 #96's slide ends slowing from 0.5-0.7 a frame) through
  its PUSH variable (its energy 4 speed: the game decays it and stops it at a ledge). DamageFall's loop
  `func_816` knocks it down when it touches the ground (DownBound 0x4D -> DownWait 0x50, the path every
  un-teched tumble landing takes: the game's own down motion), with that momentum as a short slide.
- No tech: the tech (passive 0x64 / 0x65 in `func_816`) needs a shield press in its window, and the victim's
  controller is off (Input_Set 21) from the launch to the end of the down. (A CPU's AI going around the controller
  is still unconfirmed.)
- gf_tick: waits for the down (phase 4: the damage / tumble statuses 0x45-0x4C, 30 frames), then holds it 50 frames
  (unchanged from v6: the MSC is byte for byte v6's).

### Effects (v7)
- **Mirrored vertically**: the screenshots show it's the Csg_Circle line / ray emitters (eft directional lines
  along their velocity): they shoot up with him facing left, down into the floor facing right. XV2's emitters there
  are turned X +-90 and emit along their Y; every converted skill emitter is now at the effect's origin, unturned,
  with any spread / emitter speed a full sphere (dispersion 180: the lines radiate from the fist every way), so it
  looks the same whatever the game does to an effect's axes with his facing. The test checks every
  `PtcSupamarioGfCsg_*` emitter for it.
- **Down the arm, past the fist**: the model unturned (its swirl and wind, +Z in the model, stream forward past the
  fist), its origin at his fist tip: it spans from about his elbow (apex 4.8 behind the tip at size 3) to ~19 past
  the fist. v6 had it turned 180 with the trail back over his arm and body (the screenshot: at his shoulder).
- **Bigger**: x2 (v6 1.5): XV2's aura spans ~0.9 of the character's height (1.5 m on ~1.75 m), Supamario is ~12 tall.
- **More vibrant**: alpha x2 (capped at 1; v6 1.5), the model's colour gain x2.5 / x2 / x1.5 (R / G / B, keeping its
  orange; v6 x2 flat).

### Tests
`supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems (normal and diag). The model of the game now also: the
victim frozen by a hit's own hitstop and by Hitstop_Set, a ground Damage status unfrozen in the air falls (the v6
log's Fall), DamageFall landing -> DownBound -> DownWait, the PUSH sign by his facing (a left-facing run). On v6's
build: 13 failures (the log's Fall at the hand-off, the end hit, no down, the emitters). `msc_fingerprint` v6 vs v7:
0 differ. `diag_godfist_test.py` on `exe_v46i`: 0 failed.

## Super God Fist v8 (`supamario_god_fist_v8.zip`): spin into the down, XV2's slide, the aura XV2's way round

Install over v7, exe B (`exe_v46i`, no change). User, v7 (clip `MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261114125824287`,
screenshots): "The effect is slightly too large now, and is inverted. The facing direction problem is fixed. Can you
make the opponent's movements look more natural between spinning and landing? Also landing does not slide far
enough."

### Spin to landing (the clip, frame by frame)
At the flight's end (flight frame 44, the victim lying, its tilt -810) it popped upright at once (the hand-off's
Rotation 0 under DamageFall's curled tumble), then hung ~30 frames 1 above the ground (the PUSH, written while it was
in the air, made its energy 4 a ground push: no fall), then landed hard (the game's down-bound effects), bounced and
lay down. v8:
- **The flight is XV2's launch (#179 40-58: the spin, 18 frames) and 10 frames easing down**: the spin running on from
  the launch's own turn rate (a cubic, ~24 to ~40 degrees a frame) to the next whole backward turn (-720: the same
  pose as upright, so no pop when it's set to 0), its height eased (cosine) to the ground, its travel going on at 1
  a frame. DamageFall is asked 2 frames before the end (it's frozen to the end), and at the hand-off (frame 125) it's
  on the ground with its stage collision back: it lands at once, the game knocks it down. No PUSH in the air.
- **The slide is XV2's #96 face-up slide, after the knockdown**: from its first down frame gf_tick drives its speed
  along his facing every frame through the PUSH variable (its energy 4: the game moves it, its ground correction stops
  it at a ledge), 2.27 a frame decaying x0.92 a frame to a stop: **28.3 units** in all = #96's 9.44 m at the flight's 3
  units a metre (v7: the flight's last 0.6 a frame once, left to the game's decay). The flight plus the slide end
  about where v7's flight did plus ~10 (XV2's whole #179 + #96 travel).
- XV2's CMN slide smoke (160 at #96 20) at the slide's planned place.
- He acts from 136 (XV2's BCM callback window from #0 80; v7 143): the victim is on its own from 125.

### The aura
- **Inverted**: v7 turned the model to stream forward past the fist (its trail, +Z in the model, along his facing);
  XV2's apex leads and its swirl / wind trail back over the punching arm. v8 turns it back (Y 180: still facing-safe)
  with its origin 6 past the fist tip, so the bright cone (its apex 4.2 beyond that) sits on the fist and forearm and
  the wind trails back toward the shoulder, not over his body (v6's complaint).
- **Size** x1.75 (v7 2, v6 1.5).

### Tests
`supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems (normal and diag): new checks: the spin never turns back or
jumps and ends at -720, the height eases to the ground, the hand-off on the ground upright with no PUSH in the air,
the slide's PUSH series 28.3 units decaying to 0 along his facing (and the other way facing left). On v7's build: 5
failures. `msc_fingerprint` v7 vs v8: `func_2320` (GF_IASA) and `func_2322` (gf_tick: the slide).
Approximated: whether the game moves a downed fighter by its energy 4 every frame as it does a shielding one (the
PUSH's test 154); the slide's real length if a ledge stops it.

## Super God Fist v9 (`supamario_god_fist_v9.zip`): 1.6x travel, a shorter hold, acting after the flip, no hitting downed fighters, the orb on the fist

Install over v8, exe B (`exe_v46i`, no change). User: "please make Super God Fist move Supamario about 1.6x
further", "and make it actionable after the flip Supamario does", then (replacing a spin speed-up asked in
between, which is not in v9) "not the spinning part, the hold on the fist should be reduced"; then "make it so
Super God Fist can't hit an opponent who is knocked down" and "move the effect back very slightly so that the orb
overlaps Supamario's fist".

### Travel x1.6
All of his own travel (TransN, `TRAVEL` in `supamario_godfist_anim.py`) is x1.6: the whiff now ends 44.4 forward
(v8 27.8), the God Fist 39.5 (v8 24.7), the catch at 29.8 (v8 18.6). The punch's hitboxes are on his bones, so they
go with it; the hold keeps the victim 11.5 in front of the fist; the victim's flight is written relative to him less
his own travel (`ROOT_Z`), so its world path, the hand-off and the slide (and the slide smoke) are where they were.
Ledges: AttackDash's ground correction stops him at a ledge as it does vanilla dash attacks (not tried in game with
the longer travel).

### The hold: 20 frames (v8 50)
The XV2 reference clip: the punch connects at its frame ~19 and the victim flies at ~61, 42 frames contact to launch.
Ours adds the punch's freeze (9), the punch's last 4 frames to the hit check, the jump's frame and the strike's freeze
(8): 22 frames, so the hold is **20 frames** (XV2 #0 0-40 at x2; v3-v8 50 at the BAC's x0.8: contact to launch now
42 frames, v8 72). Everything after it is 30 frames earlier: the strike at 66 (v8 96), the flight 67-90, the hand-off
at 91, the knockdown sound at 92, the move 138 frames (v8 168). The hold's two effects in step with the victim's
reaction (its hit spark at #0 12, the aura's burst at #0 17) are placed at JUMP_TO + those / 2. The spin is as v8's.
Invincibility runs to 45 (75 less the 30).

### Acting after the flip: frame 92
His flip (#0 40-62) lifts him off the floor from the strike; floor_fix eases the feet back down over 4 frames, so in
the built motion both feet are back on the floor (left 2.06 / right 1.56, the landing stance) at 66 + 22 + 4 = **92**:
GF_IASA 92 (v8 136), and the build asserts the feet first touch down at that frame. The victim's flight is in his
game script, so it is handed off the frame before (91): the flight is XV2 #179's launch for 14 frames (v8 18: its
last frames only drift back) then the same 10-frame ease to the next whole backward turn (-720) on the ground. The
MSC acts from GF_IASA as before (`func_39` in the AttackDash loop; the knockdown, slide and getup are gf_tick's from
the hand-off on, whatever he does).

### No hitting a fighter lying down
User: "make it so Super God Fist can't hit an opponent who is knocked down". The punch's two hitboxes have
Ground/Air `0x80000003` (v8: 3): bit 31 is Smash 4's own "misses a fighter lying down" flag (vanilla Diddy Kong's
dash attack uses it; the community sheet's Hitbox tab documents it). Checked in the exe (`docs/native-notes.md`,
"Ground/Air bit 31"): both the ACMD Hitbox handler and the MSC builder that Extra_Special_Hitbox goes through turn
bit 31 into the attack data's `+0x56` byte, and the collision set-up then drops the lying class from the hitbox's
target mask. So a fighter lying down (DownBound / DownWait / DownDamage ..., and a God Fist victim the MSC holds down)
takes no damage and isn't caught: the move is a whiff. As a backup, the catch (frame 18) also checks the fighter it
hit (LASTHIT_STATUS): a down status (0x4D DownBound .. 0x57 DownDamage, 0x5B DownFuraFura) means no catch. The strike
keeps Ground/Air 3 (it only ever hits the caught fighter). Not traced: which statuses the game counts as lying
(the hurt side); getups are invincible for most of their frames anyway.

### The aura back on the fist
User, a v8 screenshot: "please move the effect back very slightly so that the orb overlaps Supamario's fist". The
orb is the model's BasC mesh. Its bright core is at the front of the cone (model z -0.75 to -1), and it is 1.6 to
4.5 units across over its 37 frames; the Wind meshes are the faint swirl. In the screenshot (facing left) its centre
is about a glove's width (~1.8 units) ahead of the glove's centre. The model goes back 2.5 units (its origin 3.5 past
the fist tip, v8 6), and so do the rings spawned with it (Csg_Circle: 1.5 -> -1.0, so they stay round the orb, on
the fist). Size (x1.75), the Y 180 turn and the facing-safe emitters are as v8's. The charge (Crg, before the punch)
and the burst (Bas / Drop) stay where they were. Approximated: the 1.8 units are read off one screenshot, and the
camera's angle foreshortens it. The effect follows bone 0 at the fist's spawn-frame place, not the hand, so the
overlap changes a little as the fist moves.

### Tests
`supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems (normal and diag). New checks: the punch's Ground/Air; a fighter
lying in DownWait / DownBound / DownStand / DownDamage isn't hit or caught (a whiff); the same with the game ignoring
the flag: hit, but not caught (the status backup); a God Fist on its own downed victim misses. `msc_stack_check`:
0 of 2323. `msc_fingerprint` v8 vs v9: only `func_2320` (GF_IASA 136 -> 92). The sound bank is byte for byte v8's.
Approximated: the 42 frames from contact to launch are read off the clip by eye (+-2 frames); the hold's lean
(XV2 #179 0-40) plays twice as fast to match the shorter hold.

## Super God Fist v10 (`supamario_god_fist_v10.zip`): 1.4x travel, Leave_Ground_Cancel, his height, a startup sound, no hitting through invulnerability

Install over v9, exe B (`exe_v46i`, no change). User: "make Super God Fist move Supamario a bit less far than it does
now, but still farther than before, and give it Leave_Ground_Cancel. Also, it's still missing its startup sound
effects"; v9's clip: "make Supamario's distance from the ground consistent", then "The initial hit of Super God
Fist doesn't make Supamario move high enough. That is the problem"; "make Super God Fist not hit through
invulnerability".

### Travel x1.4
`TRAVEL` is 1.4 (v8 1.0, v9 1.6). The whiff now ends 38.9 forward (v9 44.4, v8 27.8) and the full God Fist 34.6
(v9 39.5, v8 24.7). As in v9, the victim's path is written relative to him minus his own travel.

### Leave_Ground_Cancel
The move uses the command as Diddy Kong's dash attack does (`docs/acmd-extra-commands.md`). The game script turns it
off (`Enabled=0`) at frame 0, so the dash runs off a ledge and the punch goes on in the air.
- **Whiff:** turned back on at WHIFF_END - 1. In the air, the MSC's Fall then goes through, so the move ends in Fall
  by its end: at 39 if the vl cancel window opens there, else at 43.
- **Catch:** `Enabled=2` from the catch. That is still off, but the hold, the strike and the flip stop at a ledge as
  in v9, so the victim's choreography stays on the stage.
- **Caught in the air** (he ran off before the punch): the God Fist goes on in the air to the hand-off.
- **Hand-off:** turned back on at the hand-off (91).
- **MSC:** gf_update's hit path asks for Fall in the air only from GF_IASA (v9: every frame, which the cave dropped
  until the vl cancel window opened, so it would have cut the hold short). In the air the move therefore ends in Fall
  at 92, after the victim has been handed off and knocked down, and the usual paths release it.
- **No Gravity_Set:** the fall uses his own gravity, unlike Diddy's floaty version.

### His height
v3-v9 put his lower foot on the floor on every frame except the flip's. So he stood on the floor through the punch
and the hold, then jumped up to XV2's height (the hold's hang, about 3.4 over the floor) at frame 63, three frames
before the strike, where the flip's ramp began. XV2 rises with the punch (#1 frames 10-14) and stays up through the
hold, the strike and the flip.
- **Raised:** from the punch's rise (eased over frames 10-14) to the flip's landing, he is at XV2's own height
  (`anim.raise_weight`). The punch (frames 14-18), the hold (46-65) and the strike are at one height (3.3-3.6).
- **Whiff:** comes back down with #1's own descent (frames 18-23).
- **Flip:** lands at 92 as before.
- **What goes up with him:**
  - the held victim (`LH_OFFY` = his height each frame);
  - the strike's body hitbox (Y 6.5 + 3.5);
  - the launch (the flight's first 14 frames 3.5 higher, eased away by the end of the flight);
  - the hit spark at the victim's head.
  
  The punch's fist hitbox follows the fist. Its body hitbox (ID 1) stays where it was, so short fighters are still
  hit.
- **Build check:** feet on the floor (0 to 0.15) on every ground frame; no jump over 1.3 a frame outside the MSC's
  jump and the flip; the punch, the hold and the strike within 0.2 of one height.

### Startup sound
The God Fist's BAC has one startup sound: entry 0's skill SE cue 0 at frame 0, along with skill voice cue 0, which is
a voice and is left out. Skill SE is the skill's own bank, named by its CUS entry (the 030_GOD skill's SE ACB and AWB,
`CAR_BTL_S030_GOD...SE`), and it isn't among the uploads:
- `CAR_BTL_GOK_SE` (Goku's character SE) has six throw cues (`BRD_BTL_nage_*`);
- `CAR_BTL_CMN` has no God Fist cue.

As a stand-in, CMN cue 138 `CAR_BTL_CMN_BurstDash_Atk` (XV2's burst-dash strike, 1.0 s, loudest 10 frames in) is
added to his bank (`gf_start`, sound id 0x0CB10091) and plays at frame 1. Mario's own dash sound at 10 stays. The
bank and the soundlabeltable change: row 3249 gains one label.

### Not through invulnerability
- **Invincible:** the punch's hitboxes don't ignore invincibility, but an invincible fighter's hurtbox still collides.
  The attack's hit record counts as Hit_Check's "hurtbox" (2) with no damage, so v3-v9 caught it. Now the catch also
  needs the fighter it hit to have body state 0 (`0x0F00015B`, its INTANGIBLE: 1 invincible, 2-4 intangible, the
  stronger of its status's own and its timed one).
- **Intangible** (spot dodge, rolls): it doesn't collide, so it isn't caught.
- **Shield:** a shielded punch is Hit_Check's 4, so it isn't caught either. The punch does its shield damage and the
  move whiffs. Super armor is unchanged.
- **Not confirmed in the exe:** that an invincible collision makes a hit record of the hurtbox kind (taken from the
  user's report).

### Tests
`supamario_godfist_test.py --vbn MODEL_VBN --all-models` (the test now reads his height from the built motion):
0 problems on the normal and diag builds. New checks:
- the Leave_Ground_Cancel schedule on both paths;
- the MSC off a ledge on the whiff (with and without the vl cancel window);
- the MSC in the air from frame 14, 50 and 71 on the hit path (no Fall before GF_IASA; v9's MSC fails them);
- Fall at GF_IASA: the victim still downed and released;
- the hold at his height;
- invincible / intangible / shielding at the punch (no damage, no catch).

`msc_stack_check`: 0 of 2323. `msc_fingerprint` v9 vs v10: only `func_2320` (the air check).

## Super God Fist v11 (`supamario_god_fist_v11.zip`): the skill's own startup sound

v11 is installed over v10 and still uses exe B (`exe_v46i`).

The user uploaded the skill's own SE bank, `CAR_BTL_S030_CSA_SE.acb`. It has no separate .awb: the waveform is in the
ACB's in-memory AFS2, which `tools/xv2acb.py` already reads. The bank holds one cue, 0 `CAR_BTL_S030_CSA_Dash`. That
is the God Fist BAC's entry 0 sound: skill SE (ACB type 10), cue id 0, at frame 0.

The cue is a 2.15 s one-shot:
- Its cue Length is 2153 ms.
- Its HCA has no loop chunk, so the waveform table's LoopFlag 1 has nothing to loop.
- It is loud from its first frame for about 100 frames (the dash, the punch and the hold), then fades out.

The BAC's sound item has flags 0, so XV2 doesn't stop it when the entry ends, and here it also plays out.

Changes:
- It replaces v10's stand-in (CMN `CAR_BTL_CMN_BurstDash_Atk`) under the same label `gf_start`, in the same bank slot
  (sound id 0x0CB10091, peak -0.5 dBFS as the others). The soundlabeltable is unchanged from v10.
- It plays at frame 0, the BAC's frame (the dash punch runs 1:1 with XV2 there).
- It covers the dash, so Mario's own dash sound at frame 10 is dropped. It is kept only when the build has no XV2 sound
  effects (without `--cmn-se`).

The package holds only `sound.bin` and `snd_se_Supamario.nus3bank`. The diag files are unchanged from v10.

## Super God Fist v12 (`supamario_god_fist_v12.zip`): a shorter, quieter startup sound, an off-stage catch held in place, XV2's tracking, a faster startup on a combo

v12 is installed over v11 and still uses exe B (`exe_v46i`). The user asked:
- "make Super God Fist's startup sound less loud and end much sooner";
- "if it hits an opponent off stage then it halts Supamario's momentum and stops him from falling until the animation
  is finished";
- partial tracking of an opponent in hitstun, the initial burst only: not behind him, at most 60 degrees up or down,
  horizontally at most 1.5x faster;
- "when the move is done with an opponent in hitstun who is in range (40 units), the startup of Super God Fist should
  be cut in half".

### Startup sound
- **Quieter:** 6 dB down (the tone's volume, -6.0).
- **Shorter:** the waveform is cut at 20 frames, with a linear fade over its last 6 frames (frames 14-20), so it ends
  with the punch rather than at ~130. This is `se_pcm` in the build, and it applies to the label's tone in the bank.
- **Why trimmed rather than stopped:** I did it in the waveform, not with a stop from the sound script. Smash 4's ACMD
  has no command to stop one sound, and a trimmed tone ends the same way whatever happens to the move.
- **What changes:** same label and sound id (0x0CB10091); the soundlabeltable is unchanged.

### An off-stage catch held in place
- **When it applies:** a catch made in the air (situation 2 on the catch's frame, e.g. after running off a ledge or
  tracking up).
- **The MSC pins him:** gf_update stores his X / Y and writes them back every frame up to GF_IASA (92).
- **The game script stops his fall:** at the catch, if Input_Get's SITUATION is 2, it sets
  `Gravity_Set(-1, 0, 2)`. That keeps his own gravity, so a rise slows; sets max fall to 0; and cuts a fall at once.
