- **Afterwards:** the hold, the strike, the flight and the flip play in place, and the victim's offsets are relative
  to him. At 92 the move ends in Fall (v10's air path) and he falls normally.
- **Restoring gravity:** Gravity_Set ends by itself on the status change. It also ends on a hit, a ledge grab or a KO,
  and the pin stops with the status. The launched victim goes into DamageFall with its stage collision back and falls
  as before (gf_tick lets it go if it doesn't land within 30 frames).

### Tracking (XV2's), the burst only
- **The target:** the nearest fighter, over player slots 1-8, that is:
  - in hitstun (hitstun left > 0 and a damage status 0x45-0x4C);
  - in front of him (along his facing);
  - within 60 degrees of level (dy^2 <= 3 dx^2).
  
  It is read through the slot position / hitstun / status variables and picked again every frame. He is never in
  front of himself, so he is never the target.
- **When:** only over the dash's burst, motion frames 9-15, where his TransN covers 4.53 a frame (`BURST_SPEED`,
  checked against the motion). Before and after the burst, no tracking.
- **How far:** his travel is aimed at the target. Its horizontal part is scaled by k = (the distance to it less the
  catch's 11.5) / (the burst's way left), kept between 1 and 1.5. So the burst covers at most 1.5x its way (27.2 ->
  40.8 units).
- **On the ground:** the animation moves him, and the MSC adds (k - 1) of it forward through `0x0F000004`. Up, it
  moves him up through `0x0F000005` and sets SITUATION 2, so with Leave_Ground_Cancel off the move goes on in the air.
  Down, it never moves him, so he can't go into the floor.
- **In the air:** his kinetics move him. The MSC makes up the difference to the aimed path, measured from the frame
  before, and moves him down at most 3 a frame.
- **Opponents lower than him** (the user: "don't track opponents in hitstun lower than default. If they're off an
  edge then it can happen"). This applies when he starts the move on the ground:
  - **Over the stage:** an opponent below him is never tracked down, on the ground or after he leaves it (his own
    path, no dip).
  - **Past the edge of his ground:** downward tracking is allowed. He runs off the edge (Leave_Ground_Cancel is off),
    then the air path aims down, at most 60 degrees and 3 a frame. An off-stage catch is then held as above.
  - **How "past the edge" is decided:** `GroundModule(0x2726, distance)`, asked every grounded burst frame with the
    opponent's distance in front (kept once he is in the air). This is the game's own teeter check: Wait / Walk call it
    with 0.86 and go into Ottotto when it is 1, i.e. his ground doesn't go on that far in front of him.
  - **Not confirmed:** that it answers the same at long distances is known only from its vanilla use. The diag build
    logs it (k 14: 1000 x past the edge + the distance).
  - **Upward tracking** is unchanged.
- **No target:** exactly v11.

### A faster startup on a combo
At the move's start (gf_init), if an opponent in hitstun is within 40 units, using gf_pick's rules (in front, within
60 degrees), the motion plays at 2x up to the punch (motion frame 14) and at 1x from then on.
- **The punch:** comes out on game frame 7 (normal: 14). The hit check / catch is at game frame 11 (normal: 18).
- **The rest:** everything is tied to motion frames, so it shifts with the punch.
- **Tracking:** still works, on the burst frames the motion plays at 2x (10, 12, 14), with twice the speed a frame.

### Tests
`supamario_godfist_test.py --vbn MODEL_VBN --all-models`: 0 problems on the normal and diag builds. New checks:
- **Tracking:**
  - none with the opponent behind him, above or below 60 degrees, not in hitstun, or with him facing away;
  - extras only on the burst's frames, capped at 1.5x;
  - an opponent just out of reach is reached;
  - up leaves the ground, and down on the ground does nothing;
  - grounded vs an opponent below over the stage (a slope, 20 below with him off a ledge mid-burst, 20 below inside
    the edge): never down;
  - grounded vs an opponent below past the edge: he runs off it and aims down (at most 3 a frame);
  - of two opponents, the nearest is tracked;
  - with the startup at 2x, tracking on the burst frames it plays.
- **Startup:** at 2x within 40 (the punch on game frame 7); normal at 45, behind him, or not in hitstun.
- **Off-stage catch:** held at the catch's place to GF_IASA, then not; a ground catch isn't held. Gravity_Set only
  for an air catch.
- **Startup sound:** the cut / fade / volume.

`msc_stack_check`: 0 of 2325 (gf_pick and gf_track added).

**Approximated, to check in game:**
- that writing SITUATION 2 and moving him up lifts him off the ground mid-dash, rather than the ground snapping him
  back;
- what speed his kinetics carry in the air after the burst;
- that set_pos-based tracking doesn't pass through walls;
- that the pinned speed left at 92 is negligible (air friction runs while he is held).

## Super God Fist v13 (`supamario_god_fist_v13.zip`): tracking on the burst's first frames within 20, an off-stage God Fist plays to its end

v13 installs over v12 and still uses exe B (`exe_v46i`). The user's requests:
- "make the tracking only happen in the first few frames";
- "make it so the animation finishes in the air instead of cutting to fall when hitting an opponent";
- "reduce the homing range to 20 units".

### Tracking: the first 3 burst frames, within 20 units
- **When:** only motion frames 9-11, the burst's first three (`TRACK_END` 12), not 9-14. At the 2x startup, the one of
  them it plays is frame 10, which covers motion frames 10 to 12.
- **Who:** an opponent within 20 units (`TRACK_RANGE`; from his position to its, in front, within 60 degrees, in
  hitstun).
- **Speed:** the x1.5 cap, the 60-degree limit and the off-edge rule are as in v12.
- **How much extra:** k now spreads, over the tracking frames left, the extra way the burst needs to reach the
  opponent at the contact (frame 15). That is the opponent's distance, plus its speed away from him (measured from the
  frame before) over the burst's frames left, less the catch's 11.5 and the burst's own way left.
  - **At most:** 3 x 0.5 x 4.53 = **6.8 units** more (v12: 13.6). At the 2x startup it is one frame: 0.5 x 9.07 =
    **4.5**.
  - **A still opponent within 20** is already in reach: the burst covers 27 and the catch is 11.5 further. So the
    horizontal boost only comes in for an opponent flying away from him, at about 3.2+ a frame. Aiming up (and down
    past an edge) still applies.
- **The 2x startup** keeps its own 40-unit range (`COMBO_RANGE`; the user hasn't changed it). An opponent within 20 at
  the start is within 40 too, so a tracked combo usually gets the 2x startup and its one tracking frame.
- **The edge check** (GroundModule 0x2726) is now asked on every grounded frame from the move's start, not only on
  tracking frames. So the 2x startup's single tracking frame knows it even when he's already off the ground by then.

### An off-stage God Fist plays to its end
- **Hit path in the air** (the v12 off-stage catch): the move no longer goes to Fall at GF_IASA (92). It plays the
  animation to its end (the flip, the stance) held in place: the MSC pin runs until the motion ends, and the game
  script's Gravity_Set stays. Then Fall, with his gravity back (the status change).
- **Acting from 92:** he can still act from 92 in the air. gf_update calls func_41 (the air interrupts: aerials,
  specials, air dodge, jumps). Their transition terms (STRANS ON, WorkModule 0x17) are turned on at 92, since a ground
  status has them off:
  - 0x1e000061 attack air, 0x1e00008a item shoot air;
  - 0x1e000062-65 specials, 0x1e000094 final;
  - 0x1e00003d air dodge;
  - 0x1e00003e / 3f / 43 / 44 / 45 jumps.
  
  Acting changes the status, which ends the pin and the Gravity_Set.
- **Leave_Ground_Cancel:** turned back on at the hand-off only on the ground (Input_Get SITUATION != 2), so nothing
  asks for Fall early in the air. The v10 MSC air Fall at GF_IASA is gone.
- **Grounded hits** are unchanged (act from 92 via func_39, Wait at the end).

### Tests
`--all-models`: 0 problems.

New or updated checks:
- tracking: none behind, above / below 60 degrees, not in hitstun, facing away, or out of range (25, flying away);
- tracking: a still opponent within 20 gets no extra way;
- tracking: an opponent flying away 6 a frame gets extras on frames 9-11 only, 0.5x each, 6.8 in all;
- tracking: one flying away 4.2 a frame gets part of it;
- tracking up at 50 / 59 degrees: off the ground on frame 10 (2x) and never steeper than 60;
- tracking down: never over the stage, and down past an edge;
- the 2x startup's single tracking frame;
- off-stage catch: held to the animation's end, then Fall; the air terms are turned on at 92; acting at 100 ends it
  there; a ground catch is unchanged;
- Leave_Ground_Cancel is not turned on at an off-stage hand-off;
- the v10 air test now expects Fall at the animation's end.

`msc_stack_check`: 0 of 2325.

## Super God Fist v14 (`supamario_god_fist_v14.zip`): actionable in the air at 92, tracking range 35

v14 is installed over v13 and still uses exe B (`exe_v46i`). User requests:
- "make Supamario actionable at the same time in the air as on the ground after hitting an opponent";
- "increase the tracking range to 35 units".

### Acting in the air at 92
**Why v13 didn't work.** v13 turned the air actions' transition terms on (WorkModule 0x17) and called func_41 from the
AttackDash status, and in game he still couldn't act at 92 in the air. The game reads a term only while its transition
group is on, and AttackDash, a ground status, has the air groups off. The game's own air statuses turn them on with
`func_40` (WorkModule 0x19 on these groups, from `reference/msc-note-sheet/STransGroup_ID.csv`):

| Group | Actions |
|---|---|
| 0x1f000008 | landing |
| 0x0a | air specials |
| 0x0b | light throw |
| 0x0c | air catch |
| 0x0d | air dodge |
| 0x0e | aerials |
| 0x0f | footstool |
| 0x10 | wall jump |
| 0x11 | air jump |

For example, `func_175` calls `func_40` and then `func_41` from its loop.

**What v14 does (`gf_air_act`).** From GF_IASA (92) in the air:
1. It calls `func_40` and turns the terms on, once.
2. It then calls `func_41`, the game's air interrupts with all their own rules.
3. If `func_41` doesn't act, it does the same actions straight from the frame's commands (`global24`), in `func_41`'s
   order, keeping its special-move hooks (`func_72`):
   - up / down / side / neutral special (0x154 / 0x155 / 0x153 / 0x152);
   - air dodge (0x22);
   - aerial (0x34; AttackAir picks the direction from the stick);
   - jump (0xC for Mario's kind, or 0xD), with the button, or with tap jump if it's on, and only with a jump left.

Acting ends the hold (the status change stops the pin and Gravity_Set). With nothing pressed, the animation plays to its
end in the air, then Fall (v13). Grounded hits are unchanged: `func_39` from 92.

### Tracking range 35
`TRACK_RANGE` is 35 (v13 20). The 2x combo startup keeps its own 40.
- **A still opponent:** within 35, it is still in reach. At 35 the burst (27) plus the catch (11.5) just covers it,
  so it gets no extra way.
- **An opponent moving away:** it gets the extra way (up to 6.8, 4.5 at the 2x startup) once it flies away faster than
  about (38.7 - its distance) / 6 a frame: about 0.6 a frame at 35, 3.1 at 20.
- **Overlap with the 2x startup:** an opponent within 35 at the start is within 40 too, so a tracked combo usually
  gets the 2x startup and its one tracking frame. The opponents tracked over all three frames are those that come
  into range after the start.

### Tests
`--all-models`: 0 problems. New checks:
- in the air, with an aerial, air dodge, each special or a jump pressed from 89, he acts on 92 exactly, into that
  status, held until then;
- a jump with no jump left plays out, then Fall;
- the air groups are turned on at 92;
- nothing pressed: held to the end, then Fall;
- on the ground: unchanged (`func_39` from 92, no air groups);
- the tracking range: out of range beyond 35 on the tracking frames.

Not confirmed in game:
- that `func_40`'s groups make `func_41` act from AttackDash (the direct fallback acts if they don't);
- that the command flags are there on the frame (`global24`, the game's buffer).

## Super God Fist v15 (`supamario_god_fist_v15.zip`): one range of 50 for the tracking and the 2x startup

v15 goes over v14 and still uses exe B (`exe_v46i`). The user asked: "make the tracking and double startup range the
same and increase them to 50".

### One constant for both
`HOMING_RANGE` = 50 is used for both the tracking and the 2x startup. Before, the tracking range was 35 (v14) and the
startup's 40.
- `TRACK_RANGE` and `COMBO_RANGE` are now both `HOMING_RANGE`.
- The MSC's 2x check in `gf_init` now uses the tracking's own `gf_pick(GF_TRACK_D2)`, so the two can't drift apart.
  `GF_COMBO_D2` is gone. Both use the same rules: in hitstun, in front, within 60 degrees.

### What 50 means
Without tracking, he catches an opponent about 37.5 from where he started: his way at the contact (26.0) plus the
catch's 11.5.
- **Still opponents beyond that (about 38-50):** the burst is now stretched toward them too, capped at 1.5x. A still
  opponent within 50 at the start always gets the 2x startup, so it has one tracking frame (motion 10 -> 12). That is
  +4.5 at most, a reach of about 42.
- **Opponents that come into range after the start:** the startup stays 1x and they get the three frames, +6.8 at
  most, a reach of about 44.3.
- **Fleeing opponents:** they get the extra way sooner (their speed away from him counts, as in v13).
- **Between about 44 and 50:** a still opponent is tracked (and aimed at) but not reached. The punch's own reach past
  the catch point isn't counted here.

### Tests
`--all-models`: 0 problems.
- **Out of range:** beyond 50 on the tracking frames.
- **A still opponent at 45:** stretched on frame 10 by 4.53 (the cap).
- **The 2x startup:** within 50 (48: 2x), and not at 52.

## Super God Fist v16 (`supamario_god_fist_v16.zip`): his travel from the MSC (same in the air), the edge miss, falling from 92 after an air hit

v16 goes over v15 and still uses exe B (`exe_v46i`). The user, with v15 clips (`aa89bf6c`, `3fdb9f20`):
- "When done near an edge, the speed isn't the same as on the ground when going into the air and for some reason this
  isn't hitting the opponent (first clip)."
- "Sometimes when landing from doing the quick, tracking Super God Fist, Supamario will teleport forward."
- "Please make Supamario transition into his normal gravity and falling after being actionable from hitting Super God
  Fist", specifically after hitting it in the air.

### The cause (clip 1, frames 94-110)
DK was in the air in hitstun, ahead of Supamario and above him, near the edge. Supamario dashed with the 2x startup.
The tracking's one 2x frame (motion 10) aimed up and set SITUATION 2, so he left the ground. From then on
Leave_Ground_Cancel's FALL kinetics ran instead of MOTION, and the game no longer applied the motion's TransN. The
burst's remaining way (about 14 units, motion 10-15) was lost: in the clip he hardly moves from frame 96 to 106, and
the punch fell short of DK.

The same happens when the dash runs off an edge: in the air the TransN stops ("the speed isn't the same").

On landing, MOTION kinetics come back, and the game catches up with the TransN that went by in the air in one frame
("teleport forward"). The game's own catch-up can't be run offline. It is the likely cause, and with no TransN it
can't happen.

### The fix: his travel is the MSC's
- **The motion:** `anim.ROOT_IN_MOTION = False` sets TransN to 0 on every frame (the build checks this).
- **The MSC:** every frame, `gf_travel` moves him forward by `gf_root`'s change since the frame before (forward
  writes through `0x0F000004`), on the ground and in the air alike. Its change follows the motion's rate, hitstop and
  the jump to the God Fist; at the jump there's no way, where v3-v15's TransN stepped him back about 2.4.
  - `gf_root` is the build's `ROOT_TABLE` (`anim.root_z`, a constant checked against the animation) as 75 straight
    pieces within 0.01 of every frame, generated into the MSC (`GF_ROOT_CODE`).
- **Tracking:** its extra rides on the same write (`gf_tvx`), so only the extra is the tracking's. The air branch
  takes `(k - 1)` of the way, as on the ground.
- **On the ground on the God Fist** (Leave_Ground_Cancel 2): the travel stops at the edge of his ground (the teeter
  check `GroundModule(0x2726, way)`), as the dash attack's cliff stop did.
- **The whiff:** runs off the edge as before (Leave_Ground_Cancel 0) and keeps its per-frame way in the air.

### Falling from 92 after an air hit
For a catch made in the air:
- **The hold:** the MSC holds him only to GF_IASA - 1 (91). At 92 the game script turns its Gravity_Set off (`(-1,
  -1, 0)`: his own gravity and max fall).
- **After 92:** he falls with his own gravity while the animation plays on, and his travel goes on. He can act from
  92 (v14's `gf_air_act`).
- **No action:** Fall at the animation's end.
- **Landing first:** a normal landing (the game's `func_55` with the landing group on, else LandingHeavy `0x16`).
- **Ground hits and whiffs:** unchanged.

### Tests
`--all-models`: 0 problems. New or updated checks:
- the motion's TransN is 0 on every frame;
- travel the same on the ground and in the air:
  - a whiff off a ledge at frame 11, or off an edge at 2;
  - 2x tracked up (clip 1's case), landing mid-move;
  - 1x tracked up, landing mid-move;
  - in each, every frame's move is his travel plus the tracking's capped extra, never more than a frame's way, landing
    included;
- the God Fist on the ground stops at an edge;
- tracking's extras measured over the travel;
- the off-edge down aim on a 1x run;
- an air hit: held to 91, then falling with the travel on and Fall at the end; landing at 100 is a normal landing;
  every action at 92;
- Gravity_Set on at the catch and off at 92.

**Not confirmed in game:**
- that set_pos-based travel on the ground follows slopes as the TransN did;
- the game's landing catch-up mechanism itself.

## Super God Fist v17 (`supamario_god_fist_v17.zip`): no tech on the landing, a shorter off-stage hitstun

v17 goes over v16 and still uses exe B (`exe_v46i`). The user's reports:
- "Opponents are able to tech Super God Fist".
- "The hitstun when hitting an opponent offstage is 10 frames too long".

### Why opponents could tech (the common MSC every fighter's `.mscsb` carries)
v7-v16 put the victim in DamageFall at the flight's end and let it land on its own. DamageFall's loop (`func_816`)
on landing, with the down term (`0x1e00000d`) on, goes to:
- **EscapeAir (`0x22`)** when the fighter asks to tech (`BattleObject(0x86, 0)`: the CPUs' tech request, which the
  controller-off doesn't stop) and its air dodge term (`0x1e00003d`) is on;
- **DownBound** otherwise.

The CPU's landing air dodge is the "tech" in the user's clips (training mode, a CPU).

DamageFall's own Passive (`0x64` / `0x65`) needs the flag `0x20000054`, which only Ganondorf's ground Flame Choke sets
(the end of status `0xBC`, `func_1630`). The tech paths of Damage / DamageFly (`func_810` / `func_811`, the buffered
`0x2100001a`) and the wall / ceiling techs (`func_806` / `func_807`) need the passive terms (`0x1e000013`-`18`, on in
those statuses' mains unless `0x21000018`) and a shield press or the CPU request.

The transition-term ops: WorkModule `0x16` turns a term on and `0x17` turns it off. The evidence:
- `func_272` / `func_271` (the dash attack's catch window);
- `func_797`'s passive set-up by `0x21000018`;
- DamageFall's main turning the down term on with `0x16`.

v13 / v14 had them the other way round: v14's `gf_air_act` turned its air terms with `0x17`, which turns them off,
so in game only its direct fallback acted. v17 turns them on with `0x16`, so `func_41` (the game's own air actions,
every rule of theirs) acts first; the fallback stays.

None of these can be turned off on another fighter from Supamario's scripts. The ACMD / position variables reach a
target's status, hitstun, hitstop, input, stage collision, position and so on, not its work flags or terms.

### The fix: no DamageFall landing on the stage
- **At the hand-off (91):** the game script keeps the victim's stage collision off and sets gf_phase 5.
- **On the stage:** gf_tick decides. When he is on the ground and his ground goes on to where the victim is (the
  teeter check `GroundModule(0x2726, its distance)`), it puts the victim down itself:
  - offset Y 0 (his height: the floor);
  - SITUATION 0;
  - stage collision 2 (ground);
  - status DownWait (`0x50`; DownWaitU / D from its own flag);
  - then the held-down phase and the slide as before.
  
  DownWait's loop (`func_934` / `func_963`) has no tech of any kind, and the victim never lands in DamageFall. The
  difference you can see: no DownBound bounce, it lies down at once.
- **Off the stage** (no ground there, or he is in the air): its stage collision comes back (air) and it falls in
  DamageFall as before (gf_phase 4). DamageFall in the air has no wall / ceiling tech.
- **Diag log:** the diag build logs the decision (k 15: 1000 + its status on the stage, else its status).

### The off-stage hitstun: 10 frames shorter
An off-stage victim falls in DamageFall. It can act once its controller is back (gf_tick's wait for a down that never
comes) and its hitstun is over (`Hitstun_Set` on every flight frame to DOWNFALL_K, motion 88):

| | Hitstun | Wait | Hitstun ends | Controller back | Acts at |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| v16 | 40 | 30 | 128 | 121 | the strike + 62 |
| v17 | 30 (`FLIGHT_HITSTUN`) | 27 (`GF_FALL_WAIT`) | 118 | 118 | the strike + 52 |

The "acts at" frames are motion frames; add the strike's 8-frame freeze for game frames. The on-stage knockdown, the
flight and Supamario's own timing are unchanged.

### Tests
`--all-models`: 0 problems. New checks:
- **Knockdown:** DamageFall, then put down at the hand-off (DownWait on the ground).
- **No tech:** a CPU asking to tech and a human pressing shield both stay down (DownWait), never EscapeAir or Passive.
- **Air actions:** the 13 air actions' terms are turned on with `0x16` at 92.
- **The model reproduces the report:** v16's hand-off (a DamageFall landing) with a CPU asking to tech gives EscapeAir.
  On v16's build the new checks fail with exactly that.
- **Off stage:** not put down, it falls (stage collision 5), controller back, upright; it acts at the strike + 52
  (v16 + 62).

**Not confirmed in game:** that SITUATION 0 with stage collision 2 holds a victim put at floor height through
DownWait's first frame.

## Super God Fist glow test (`supamario_god_fist_glow_test.zip`): XV2's glowing parts brighter than v17

### Correcting the bloom statement
v5 said "XV2 draws these with glare ... bloom Smash 4 doesn't have", and v6 said "none in Smash 4". Both are wrong:
Smash 4 blooms colours drawn past 1. The God Fist's effects have been past 1 since v5.
- **Particles:** every converted skill emitter is drawn at its colour keys x `SKILL_GAIN` 2, so it peaks at 2.0 (keys
  at most 1; a few dim lines at 0.66 / 1.14), whether XV2 glares that part or not. The drawn colour is keys x colour
  scale x the texture's R. The vanilla template programs don't clamp it; only Hero's own pixel programs do (fix 27).
- **The aura model:** gain `NU_effColorGain` = MatCol x `MODEL_TINT` (2.5, 2.0, 1.5), giving (2.5, 0.99, 0.73) on
  BasC and (2.5, 0.24, 0) on the wind (additive).

The user still doesn't see XV2's glow at those values. So the test goes upward to find where Smash 4's bloom shows.

### Where the risk starts: Hero's haze (fighters/brave/README.md)
- **Fix 21:** the Frizz test showed purple at the brightest spot of each part (glows, sparks, fire).
  - Frizz's spark1 peaked at 4.4 (colour x `color_scale` 4.0).
  - 86 of Hero's 840 emitters were over 2.5; vanilla's 99th percentile is 2.0.
  - `hdr_scale` then compressed peaks over 1.2 to a quarter (4.4 -> 2.0).
- **Fix 22:** the purple crescent seen on every Frizz level was in fact Robin's own Thunder orb (base entries 1-3),
  not bloom. So fix 21's particle evidence is mixed.
- **Fix 23:** the whirlwind's opaque pale-blue meshes glowed purple. `HDR_START` / `HDR_KNEE` 1.0 / 0.05 then held
  colour keys near 1, as vanilla fire keeps its keys at 1.0-1.05.
- **Fix 27:** Ultimate's colours went back once Hero's own programs clamped to 0-1.

So the clearest haze evidence is on opaque meshes just past 1. For additive particles, 4.4 is the one reported case,
and part of it was the Thunder orb.

### XV2's glow per material (`002_GOK_CSG.ptcl.emm`)
`tools/xv2_to_ptcl.py` notes each emitter's material and glare (`glare G material NAME`).

| Material | GlareCol | Emitters |
|---|---|---|
| `add_LR2_GY_BF` | 2.0 | Crg `sprite_ring00` (the charge ring) |
| `nml_GY_BF` | 1.5 | Circle `sprite_tsubu00` x2, drop `sprite` x2 (the sparkles / burst drops) |
| `nml_LR2_G` | 1.1 | Circle `line_line01` x2 |
| everything else | none | |

The model's material has GlareCol (1, 1, 1) and no flag.

### The variants (`--glow PEAK`; the default build is v17)
Everything is v17, including the aura model. Only the glowing materials' emitters are scaled, to a peak of PEAK x
GlareCol / 1.5. The hue is kept, they never go below v17's 2.0, and they never go past `GLOW_CAP` 8. Only
`supamario.010d0000.ptcl` changes.

| Peak | v17 | glow_3 | glow_4 | glow_5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| charge ring (2.0) | 2.00 | 4.00 | 5.33 | 6.67 |
| sparkles / drops (1.5) | 2.00 | 3.00 | 4.00 | 5.00 |
| lines (1.1) | 2.00 | 2.20 | 2.93 | 3.67 |
| the rest, the aura | as v17 | as v17 | as v17 | as v17 |

**Haze risk:**
- glow_3's ring (4.0) is near Frizz's 4.4.
- glow_4 and glow_5 put the ring and the sparkles past it.

The user is asked to report where the glow appears and any purple. The aura stays at 2.5: it's an opaque-ish mesh
like fix 23's, and there's no evidence it's safe to raise.

## Super God Fist: buffer fix v1 (`buffer_commands_v1.zip`, exe B `exe_v46l`)

User: "Super God Fist should clear the hit opponent's buffer after releasing them, so they don't go into the hold of
the move with a buffered attack". The game keeps a press's commands 10 frames (docs/native-notes.md "The input
buffer") and a fighter acts on the first one still there when it can; what the victim pressed before the catch or while
held (its controller is on until the launch) could come out when it's let go. The game script now empties its buffer
(exe_v46l's `Buffer_Clear`, everything) at the catch (`Buffer_Clear(2, 0, 0)`, before Target_Slot_Get) and at the
hand-off (`HANDOFF`, by its slot like the other hand-off commands: `Buffer_Clear(2 + slot, 0, BUFFER_CLEAR_FRAMES)`,
5: again after its next 4 input reads). From the launch to gf_tick's release its controller is off (Input_Set 21),
so nothing new is buffered; at the release a button it is holding counts as a new press, as for any fighter.
No MSC change (globals still 127 of 128); only `game.bin` differs from Core Breaker v10's build (the God Fist's game
script). Built with the full Supamario build (Fake Death's, Core Breaker v10's source; CB_DIAG off).
`tools/supamario_godfist_test.py` checks both clears (the victim's log); every suite passes (God Fist, Core Breaker,
Fake Death, the MSC stack check). Not tried in game yet. (`BUFFER_CLEAR`, `0x0F0003C9`, is the MSC's way, if
gf_release should ever clear it too.)

## Core Breaker (up special): phase 1, the XV2 skill read and converted

User: put Xenoverse 2's **Core Breaker** (Fu's Ultimate, skill folder `2070_FIT_COR`) on his up special, the way the
Super God Fist went on his dash attack. Phase 1 reads the skill, converts its animation and effects and proposes the
Smash version; the move itself (status 0x154, game / effect / sound scripts, the package) is phase 2, after the
user's answers.

### What the files say (`tools/supamario_corebreaker_xv2.py report` -> `reference/xv2/corebreaker_skill.json`)
Readers: `tools/xv2bac.py` (BAC / BCM, now with the BCM tree links and `read_bdm`), XV2-Tools' names for BAC types,
EEPK types (0 Common = vfx/cmn/BTL_CMN, **1 StageBG = the stage's own ground effects**, 5 Super, 6 Ultimate),
functions (0x2 Damage, 0x6 Invisibility, 0xB ThrowHandler duration, 0x28 Invisible opponents) and ThrowHandler flags.
The BAC's item frames are the skill EAN's own frames (an entry starts at its animation's start frame).
- **BCM:** two chains. #1 (an extra +0x10 condition 0x80000: giant opponents) BAC 0 -> 1 -> 2 -> 7 (a dive without a
  grab) -> 11 (hits of 5 and 450) / 5. **#9, the normal one:** BAC 0 -> 1 -> 2 -> 3 (a target found: homing first) or
  4 (none) -> on connect the user plays 10, the victim 17; -> 5 when the dive meets nothing.
- **0-25 (entry 0):** crouch 0-9, then straight up: the root (b_C_Base, MoveWithAxis XYZ) rises 10.0 m by 25 and
  10.9 m by 40, plus BAC Movement (0, +0.16, 0.1 forward) a frame 9-25. Invulnerable (no damage) 0-215. Skill SE cue
  1 at 0; StageBG 140 (ground smoke) at 1; Common 40 (Fly_Wind trail) at 8.
- **25-110 (entry 1):** hovering at the apex, charging: screen darkened (BPE 70 / 55), opponents hidden (0x28),
  skill SE 2 at 25, skill effect 0 at 45 (the Cor_Crg orb + Cor_Crg_Loop at the head).
- **110-168 (entry 2):** effect 1 (charge end) at 110, SE 3, effect 5 (blue flame aura on 7 body bones) at 125; the
  turn and wind-up into the dive pose; at 164-167 three 16 m "probe" spheres 22 m below, 14-20 m ahead (BDM #2: type
  29, no damage): they pick entry 3 (a target) or 4.
- **168-214 (entries 3 / 4):** the dive, fist first: effect 2 (Cor_Fire blazing fist + its cone model) at 168, SE 4,
  wind (effect 7) at 173 / 181. Entry 3 homes onto the target (+0.8 m up, HomingMovement) then dives from 188, entry 4
  dives at once: **Movement (0, -1.0, 1.1 forward) a frame = 1.49 m a frame, 48 degrees below level**, up to 46
  frames. Hitboxes 168-214: the connect box (BDM #0, flag 8) and 10 damage 1.8 m ahead.
- **On connect (user 10, victim 17):** he goes 2 m up at once and holds the victim chest to chest (ThrowHandler,
  bones 18 / 18) **86 frames** (195-281) in his dive pose, the fist in it: effect 8 (impact + loop), effect 12 (a
  flare) at 228; the victim takes 0x2 Damage -15 every 10 frames (9 ticks). Then he **drives it down at 45 degrees**
  (0.9 / 0.9 m a frame) until the ground (BacJump_ReachGround -> entry 12) or 64 frames (-> entry 13).
- **Entry 12, the crash (290-426):** effect 3 (the explosion: Cor_Bomb_*), StageBG 440 and 160, SE 5 + 6; he lands
  crouched and slides 6.5 m back, standing by 426. **The victim (18 -> 19 -> 20):** 0x2 Damage 405, blown back
  (CMN #71 BLOW_F, 0.6 m a frame back, 0.2 up, gravity) 30 frames, then CMN #96's face-up slide (0.8 m a frame
  decaying, ~29 m), CMN SE 52 Hit_L. **Entry 13 (no ground):** the release in the air, a 250 hit.
- **Entry 5 (a miss lands):** effect 9 (Cor_Nohit blast), StageBG 640 / 60, SE 6; no hitbox.
- **Damage split (XV2's numbers):** 10 (dive) + 9 x 15 (hold) + 405 (crash) = 550; air release 250 instead of 405.
- **Cameras** (`2070_FIT_COR.cam.ean`, 4): #0 follows him up and closes in on the charge, #3 looks down from above
  him over the dive, #1 the close-up of the hold, #2 starts at the impact point on the ground and pulls back 10 m
  up / 8 m away. Smash 4 can't take the camera: the cinematic parts (the 85-frame charge, the hidden opponents,
  the darkened screen, the 86-frame hold) are shortened in the proposal.
- **Body type:** the base `2070_FIT_COR.ean` (`_C`, as the God Fist's 030_GOD_CSA.ean); `_F` / `_MAM` the other
  builds, `_FIT` Fu's own skeleton (143 bones).
- **Sound:** `CAR_BTL_B2070_GLT_SE.acb` is **not** Core Breaker's: it holds 2 cues (0 S2061_SHS Shot_F21,
  1 B2060_GLO ChargeShot) and the BAC plays skill SE cues 1-6. The skill's own bank (the Ultimate's,
  likely `CAR_BTL_U2070_COR_SE.acb` / `.awb`) is still needed. CMN cues it uses: 52 Hit_L (the crash, on the
  victim) and, as the God Fist inferred, 106 Down for the slide. Voices (skill voice 1-4, 7; character voice 26, 39,
  51, 82, 88) left out.

### Converted (phase 1, scratch only; the tools)
- `tools/supamario_corebreaker_anim.py`: SpecialHi / SpecialAirHi (the same motion) from `2070_FIT_COR.ean`,
  retargeted as the God Fist's; one 292-frame motion of segments the MSC jumps between (`SEGMENTS`: crouch 0-5,
  rise 6-29, charge 30-39, flare 40-59, dive 60-65, dive loop 66-84, miss landing 85-124, grab hold 125-160, drive
  loop 161-179, crash 180-247, air release 248-291). The rise is TransN (as vanilla Super Jump Punch's), 54.8 units
  (XV2's 10.9 m x 5.04; vanilla 44.8); everything else's travel is left to the MSC (God Fist v16). `--json`: the
  timeline, the root table and his joints per frame.
- `tools/xv2_smash_fx.py` (shared): the God Fist's final effect settings as defaults (x1.75 over 5.04 a metre;
  facing-safe emitters; calls on bone 0 with only height / forward offsets and Y 0 / 180, or a body bone with
  nothing; model turns baked into the model's own animation) plus **HDR from each material's glare**: colour keys x
  the material's GlareCol (XV2's glare tint), colour scale x2 (the God Fist's), the brightest drawn channel capped at
  2.0 (Smash 4 blooms past 1; far past it Hero's effects hazed purple, fighters/brave fixes 21-27); refraction
  materials (heat haze) hidden instead of drawn as light, subtractive ones kept subtractive.
- `tools/supamario_corebreaker_fx.py`: the 17 EMPs -> 20 sets `PtcSupamarioCb*` (162 emitters; skipped: 4 ConeExtrude,
  3 Default nodes), the models `EffSupamarioCbCrg` (the charge orb, turned to face the side camera) and
  `EffSupamarioCbFire` (the fist's flame, its bulb leading, pitched 48 degrees down along the dive), textures from
  0x15141C00; Cor_Wev (refraction) left out; `corebreaker_fx.json`: every BAC effect's parts, bone 0 offsets from
  the built motion, sizes, frames; previews of each effect at its final size beside his pose, both facings.
  Not converted: the ECF colour fades (CBIND), the EMA lights, the trace Fly_Wind.
- `tools/supamario_corebreaker_test.py`: the facing rule, the HDR cap, the sizes, the motion (0 problems).
- `tools/xv2vfx.py`: reads CBIND containers (ECF colour fades, kept as bytes) and EMP headers with +0xA set (XV2-Tools
  never reads it). `tools/xv2bac.py`: an EEPK type it has no name for printed as a number.

## Core Breaker v1 (`supamario_corebreaker_v1.zip`): the up special

Install over Super God Fist v17, exe B (`exe_v46i`, no exe change). Not tested in game. Built by
`tools/supamario_corebreaker_build.py` (it runs the God Fist's build first: the dash attack's files are v17's byte for
byte except what the two moves share: the motion pacs, the four ACMD files, the MSC, the effect bank, the sound bank and
the soundlabeltable, where only SpecialHi / SpecialAirHi, specialhi / specialairhi, func_2287-2289 and new entries
change).

### The user's answers, and the defaults used where there was none
| | |
|---|---|
| Movement | "The animation movement should become speed values that are set": the motion has no TransN; the MSC sets his speeds every phase (gravity energy 1: speed / acceleration / limit, the Kamehameha's ops; energy 3 for along his facing), so landing, walls, platforms and ledges are the game's. |
| Homing | "fixed and light for the dive part": 48 degrees, bent at most 10 degrees (1.5 a frame) toward a fighter ahead of and below him within 50, inside a 30-degree cone; none in the rise or hover. |
| Off-stage hit | "a catch into a multihit that then explodes and can KO": the hold's 4 burn hits, then the drive; with no ground before the drive's end (30 frames, or his start height) the explosion in the air: 17%, angle 40, BKB 30, KBG 60. Mario-weight near the ledge: knockback 120 from ~75% (their % before the move); 0%: 62, 50%: 102, 100%: 142. |
| Armor | "armor the rise and armor during the dive, but no armor during the hover": super armor (DamageModule 0xB type 1) frames 0-29 and from the dive's first frame; off for the hover, at a catch, a landing and the status end. |
| Landing lag | "very long": 60 frames (XV2's own landing, entry 5, 59 frames). |
| Damage | "26%": catch 3% + 4 x 1.5% burn + 17% crash or explosion, both endings. |
| Q1 rise (default) | ~55 units (XV2's 10.9 m; vanilla Super Jump Punch ~45): speed 4.66 slowing by 0.19 a frame over 24 frames. |
| Q2 hover (default) | 30 frames; the stick back turns him (B-reverse). |
| Q4 dive off the stage (default) | the dive (and the drive) never go below the height he started the move from (less 3): no height lost overall. With no ground by then (or after 40 frames) he goes into FallSpecial. This stands in for "only dive with ground in reach" (no ground probe is used: GroundModule(0xC)'s isn't verified here). |
| Q5 (default) | the dive is a catch plus its small hit (3%); the miss's landing blast does no damage. |
| Q6 on the stage (default) | the God Fist's ending: put down into DownWait on the stage by gf_tick (v17, no tech), the slide, no KO. |
| Q9 rest (default) | FallSpecial after an off-stage ending or a spent dive; ledges grabbed during the dive (not while carrying the victim). |
| Q10 (default) | the explosion and the charge orb / its flash at half the x1.75 scale. |

### The move, frame by frame (motion frames; the motion plays straight through, the MSC only jumps forward)
| Frames | Phase (`cb`) | What |
|---|---|---|
| 0-5 | 0 | crouch (ground: still; air: held in place), armor |
| 6-29 | 0 | the rise: lift-off, up 4.66 a frame slowing 0.19 a frame, 0.4 forward; armor |
| 30-59 | 1 | the hover: still (speed, gravity 0), no armor, B-reverse; Ki_Rservoir at 30, Ki_Rlease at 40 |
| 60-103 | 2 | the dive: 7.49 a frame at 48 degrees (+-10 homing), armor, air cap lifted, ledges; the catch box (3%) on his fist and body; Rush |
| 104-163 | 5 | a miss landed: the landing blast, 60 frames, then Wait |
| 164-199 | 3 | caught: invincible; the victim at his fist (its feet 9 ahead, 2 below him), held (hitstun / hitstop each frame, no stage collision, controller off); he rises 10 over 6 frames; burn hits at 168 / 176 / 184 / 192 |
| 200-237 | 4 | the drive: 6.41 a frame at 45 degrees with the victim |
| 238-305 | 6 | landed: the crash (17%, a flinch) at 239; at 241 the victim to gf_tick (gf_phase 5: put down, the slide); he slides back 1.6 a frame (friction stops it); Wait at 305 |
| 306-349 | 7 | off the stage: the explosion (17%, KO) at 307, the victim let go there (stage collision back, controller on); he stays in place, FallSpecial at 349 |

### How (files)
- `supamario.c`: `cb_*` (end of the file; func_2287 / func_2288 / func_2289, the vanilla SpecialHi main / loop / end,
  call cb_init / cb_update / cb_end). Each phase is its own function with its own constants (`CB_*` from
  `supamario_corebreaker_build.msc_values()`, also in `supamario_kame_build.py --msc-only`). The victim goes through its
  player slot's variables, never its rotation (no lean: nothing to turn back). New_Var slots 0x20-0x22 (`cb`, `cb_slot`,
  `cb_tmp`); 9 new globals (98 of 128).
- `tools/supamario_corebreaker_anim.py`: the motion (350 frames, TransN 0; the dive and the drive loops written out
  twice). `tools/supamario_corebreaker_fx.py` + `tools/xv2_smash_fx.py`: the effects onto the God Fist's bank (HDR from
  each material's glare, capped at `xv2_smash_fx.HDR_CAP` = 2.0: one constant for the glow test's pick).
- The game script: the catch every dive frame (Hit_Check kinds 2, not lying, not invincible; a refused hit ends the
  checks), the burn / crash / explosion hits guarded by `cb` (the motion jumps run the commands on the way). The
  effect script: the BAC's effects at their frames on bone 0 (up / forward offsets, no turn) or on his body bones (the
  aura), guarded the same way, the loops terminated as their part ends. The sound script is empty: the MSC plays them
  (the Kamehameha's test 25).
- Sounds (`CB_SE`): the skill's bank `CAR_BTL_U2070_COR_SE.acb` (cues 1 Rise, 2 Ki_Rservoir (cut to 50 frames with a
  10-frame fade), 3 Ki_Rlease, 4 Rush, 5 Explosion; cue 6 is "Stop_4_Rush": the MSC stops Rush) and CMN cue 52 Hit_L,
  levelled to -0.5 dBFS peak as the God Fist's: sound ids 0x0CB10092-0x0CB10097. Down at the hand-off is the God Fist's
  (0x0CB10090).
- The bank: ptcl 3.09 -> 3.89 MB (20 sets `PtcSupamarioCb*`), models `EffSupamarioCbCrg` / `EffSupamarioCbFire`
  (textures 0x15141C00 / 0x15141C10).
- No new motion or script slots (SpecialHi / SpecialAirHi replaced): ModAnimNames.txt unchanged.

### Splitting the rise and the dive later
The rise (cb_rise_phase, CB_RISE_*) and the hover end in `cb_dive_start()`, the only way into the dive; the dive and
everything after it (catch, hold, drive, crash, explosion) don't read anything the rise set except `cb_sy` (the start
height, the dive's floor) and the facing. To make two moves: keep phases 0-1 in SpecialHi ending in FallSpecial (or a
new hover-end), and call `cb_init`'s reset + `cb_dive_start()` from the other move's status (e.g. down special in the
air), with that move's motion holding the dive / land / hold / drive / crash / release segments (the frame constants
are the segments' starts: `supamario_corebreaker_build.SEG`).

### Tests
- `tools/supamario_corebreaker_test.py OUT MODEL_VBN`: the compiled MSC in the interpreter with a physics model (the
  rise's height and armor, the hover, the dive's speed / angle / armor / air cap, homing (only ahead and below, its
  turn rate and limit), a miss landing (60 frames, Wait), a dive off the stage (no lower than the start, FallSpecial),
  the B-reverse, a catch on the stage (hold, drive, crash, the hand-off to gf_tick), a catch off the stage (the
  explosion, the victim let go, FallSpecial), cut mid-hold (let go), an earlier God Fist victim let go at the catch),
  the scripts' damage (26 both ways), TransN 0, the effects' facing and HDR cap: 0 problems.
- `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models` on this build: 0 problems (it now finds gf_tick by its place in
  supamario.c: the cb functions come after it). `msc_stack_check`: 0 of 2355. `msc_fingerprint` vs v17: only
  func_2287 / func_2288 / func_2289 differ (+27 functions).
- `port_check` doesn't apply (not an Ultimate port).

**Not confirmed in game:** that op 0x11 on energy 3 with the air cap lifted (flag 0x20000044, op 0x15 -1) holds 7.5 a
frame in the air (the roll-off's ops; the air one untested); that the status lands him (global18 0) and func_56 grabs
ledges with these kinetics; the rise's exact height (+-2: when the game adds the acceleration); the crash's 1.6
slide (Mario's ground friction assumed 0.08); the victim's place at his fist for big / small fighters.

## Core Breaker v2 (`supamario_corebreaker_v2.zip`): the charge

Install over v1, exe B (no exe change). User, v1's clip (`MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261114220855534`): "Up special's
charging effect seems to be played incorrectly".

### What the clip shows (clip frames ~66-90 = the hover)
- From the hover's start a dark grey disc round his head and chest for ~8 frames, then nothing: the orb (BAC effect 0,
  `EffSupamarioCbCrg`).
- Then a hard-edged cyan square beside him for ~12 frames: the charge's end flash (effect 1).

### Causes and fixes
- **The orb was black.** Its three materials are `T1_VFX_PRLX_MTN`. `xv2_to_nud` takes a material's colour as
  MatCol1 x MatCol2. core_a and core_b2 have MatCol2 (0, 0, 0), so they were drawn as opaque black. MatCol2 0 can't be
  a tint here: those materials glare (Glare 1, GlareCol up to 1.2). For a PRLX material whose MatCol2 RGB is 0, the
  colour is now MatCol0: core_a (0.5, 1, 1), core_b2 white. core_b keeps its cyan MatCol2. The God Fist's and the
  Kamehameha's models aren't PRLX, so they are unchanged.
- **The orb was ended before it showed.** XV2 plays it from 45 to 111 (66 of its 72-frame animation, which fades in
  over ~16 frames). v1 squeezed XV2's 70-frame charge into 10 frames and ended the orb at the flare, so only its
  fade-in was ever seen. Now:
  - the hover's charge is 18 frames (30-47) and its flare / wind-up 12 (48-59; the hover is still 30);
  - the orb is on from 31 (`ORB_ON`) to 48 (`FLARE_FROM`);
  - its own animation (omo and mta) is played in `ORB_FRAMES` = 19 frames (`xv2_smash_fx.retime_model`).
- **The square.** The end flash's `ptcl01.dds` is a quarter of a glow. XV2's sampler mirrors it (Mirror / Mirror, UV
  x2 x2) into a whole one, but `xv2_to_ptcl.first_cell` took the UV rectangle clipped to the texture. Its bright
  corner was drawn as a square.
  - `first_cell` now repeats past the edge by the sampler's RepetitionU / V (Mirror / Clamp / Wrap).
  - This is opt-in (`xv2_to_ptcl.REPEAT_UV`, on for Core Breaker's conversion), so the God Fist build stays byte for
    byte v17's.
  - The God Fist's `Csg_Bas.emp` has one such sampler (`4circle00.dds`, Mirror x2 x2): its burst ring may be drawn
    from a quarter too. Not changed (the user's call).
- Ki_Rlease (the release sound) moves with the flare to 48.

### Tests
`supamario_corebreaker_test.py`: 0 problems. New checks:
- the orb's animation is 19 frames;
- every orb material is coloured;
- ptcl01's textures are round (bright centre, dark corners);
- the orb is on at 31 and off at 48.

On v1's build these new checks fail with exactly the clip's three problems.

Other results:
- `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems.
- `msc_stack_check`: 0 of 2355.
- `msc_fingerprint` v1 vs v2: only func_2336 (the release sound's frame).

## Fake-glow halo test (`supamario_halo_test.zip`): both moves, three strengths

The God Fist glow test showed that colours drawn past white don't visibly bloom (glow_3 / 4 / 5 looked the same),
so `xv2_smash_fx.HDR_CAP` stays 2.0. This test fakes XV2's glare instead.

How it is built (`tools/supamario_halo_test.py` over the combined build: God Fist v17 + Core Breaker v2; the logic is
in `tools/xv2_smash_fx.py`):
- **Which emitters:** every converted emitter whose XV2 material has its Glare flag on (not refraction, not hidden):
  113 of the two moves' emitters.
- **The halo:** an additive copy of the emitter (`halo_emitter`):
  - the same timing, emission and motion;
  - a soft round falloff texture (`soft_glow_rgba`);
  - `HALO_SIZE` x2.5 larger (rings x1.2; streaks x2.5 wide);
  - `HALO_ALPHA` x its alpha;
  - its colour capped at `HDR_CAP`.
- **Where the halo goes:** into the emitter's own set while the set has room (16 emitters). Otherwise into a
  companion set `<name>H`; 9 sets needed one. `add_halo_calls` makes every Graphic_Effect / External_Graphic /
  Terminate_Graphic_Effect of the set in the attackdash / specialhi / specialairhi effect scripts also call its
  companion, growing the enclosing True blocks' offsets to match.
- **Glowing models** (`model_halo_emitter`): one soft particle at the model's centre for its animation's length, the
  looping fist flame's one per loop, with the model's hue:
  - Core Breaker's orb: radius 2.4, cyan;
  - its fist flame: core radius 3, blue;
  - the God Fist's aura: orb radius 2.5, orange.
- **Strengths:** `HALO_ALPHA` is one constant; the test has halo_0.25 / halo_0.45 / halo_0.7, all at size x2.5.
- **Facing:** every halo is a copy of a facing-safe emitter (no turn, at the origin); the model halos are made
  facing-safe too.
- **Tests:** `supamario_corebreaker_test.py` and `supamario_godfist_test.py` on the 0.45 build: 0 problems. Every
  True block of the changed scripts ends on a command and still holds its original commands.
- **The bank:** ptcl 3.89 -> 4.16 MB.

## Core Breaker v3 (`supamario_corebreaker_v3.zip`): the rise and the hover / dive split

Install over v2, exe B (no exe change). The user asked for three things:
- "make the rise from up special cancelable after the initial burst of speed into any attack";
- "make the hover and dive happen of the next up special input. Up special would be usable once per airtime,
  resetting when getting hit, ledge, grabbed, ko'd";
- "The moment the button is pressed for the hover, there should be a very small detection box ... If it hits an
  opponent it should skip Supamario to the catch part of the dive"; then "The detection box ... should be able to hit
  a downed opponent".

### How it is split: one status, two entries
Both halves stay in the SpecialHi status (0x154, the cb_* phases unchanged). `cb_init` picks the entry from
`CB_USED`, a New_Var (slot 0x23, per fighter, object scope):

| CB_USED | Up special does | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| 0 (or on the ground) | **the rise** (phase 0); CB_USED 1; his height kept (`CB_RISE_Y`, slot 0x24) | |
| 1 | **the hover / dive** (phases 1-7; the motion jumps to the hover, frame 30, as the God Fist's jump); CB_USED 2 | |
| 2 | nothing | `cb_can_up` is the up special's own hook (`global26`, set in func_2249). The game's special-move check (`func_72(global26)` in every status's transition code) refuses it, so the up special input does nothing. `cb_init` also falls straight to Fall as a safeguard. |

- Two statuses would need new status slots; one status keeps the phases, the motion and the scripts as they are.
- The game's own up special state: Mario has no "used" flag; vanilla Super Jump Punch ends in FallSpecial, which
  allows no specials. The rise now ends in Fall (actionable), so the once-per-airtime rule is CB_USED's.
- After the hover / dive: FallSpecial (a spent dive, the explosion) or the landing lag / the crash on the ground, as
  in v2.

### Resets
`cb_reset_tick`, from the per-frame hook func_82, every status. CB_USED goes back to 0:
- on the ground or a ledge (`global18` 0 / 1);
- in a damage / capture status 0x3F-0x4C (CapturePull .. DamageFall: being grabbed, thrown, hit, tumbling);
- in a cliff status 0x72-0x7A;
- on Dead 0xB1, Rebirth 0xB2 or Entry 0x14F (a KO also clears New_Vars).

It never resets while he is in the up special itself in the air (status 0x154 with situation 2). Not covered:
special captures by other moves (Ganondorf's 0xB8 and the like, item captures).

### The rise (first up special)
- Crouch, then about 55 up with super armor, as before.
- From frame 16 (`RISE_CANCEL`: 10 frames into the rise, ~37 of its ~55 units, the speed down from 4.7 to 2.7) it can
  be cancelled into any attack: an aerial (AttackAir), the neutral / side / down special, or up special again. Up
  special again starts the hover / dive in place: CB_USED 2, the motion to frame 30.
- The cancels go straight through the game's status change (`cb_rise_attack`, as `gf_air_act`'s direct actions),
  not `func_41`. So jumps and air dodges are not offered in the rise.
- With nothing pressed, it ends at frame 30 in **Fall** (not FallSpecial). Fall itself allows everything (jumps,
  air dodge, aerials, specials), and up special there is the hover / dive.

### The hover / dive (second up special)
- As v2: the hover (30 frames, no armor, B-reverse), the dive (48 degrees, super armor, light homing, ledges), the
  catch, the hold, the drive, the crash / explosion, the 60-frame landing lag, FallSpecial after.
- **The dive has no floor and no frame limit** (see "The dive goes on" below). The drive (with an airborne victim)
  keeps a floor, for the explosion in the air off the stage: the rise's start (less 3), but at least
  `DRIVE_MIN_DROP` (30) below the catch.
- **The detection box:**
  - on the hover's first frame, 2.5 across (`DETECT_SIZE`; his body is ~4) at his middle (1 ahead, 6.5 up);
  - the catch's 3%; caught on the frames after it;
  - its catch goes straight to the hold, then the drive and the crash / explosion (no hover, no dive); a victim on
    the ground: the hold on the ground and the crash (below);
  - **It hits fighters lying down** (Ground/Air 3, without the God Fist's bit 31; no down-status refusal). A caught
    downed fighter is put in Damage (0x46) by the MSC: out of the down status, held in hitstun as any held victim.
  - A God Fist / Core Breaker victim being held down by gf_tick: the same fighter is taken over (gf_phase and
    gf_lock to 0: this move holds it); another fighter is let go first (gf_release), as before.
  - Shields, invincible and intangible fighters are still refused (the hover goes on).
- The dive's own catch is unchanged (it misses fighters lying down).
- The dive starts with Hit_Reset and Hit_Log_Clear(0), so a refused detection hit can't become a late catch.
- The scripts' New_Vars are now all object-scoped. A move-scoped `cb` was reset to 0 by the script's start, after the
  MSC's init had set it to 1 for the second entry, which would have skipped the detection checks.

### Tests
`supamario_corebreaker_test.py`. New checks:
- the rise alone ends in Fall at 30 with the rise used, armor to its end;
- up special again gives the hover / dive;
- cb_can_up refuses a third up special in the air, allows it on the ground, and is the global26 hook;
- every reset event (landing, ledge, hit, tumble, grab, throw, KO, respawn), and no reset while falling, attacking or
  in the move;
- each attack cancels the rise from frame 16 and not before; up special in the rise gives the hover / dive in place;
  jump / air dodge inputs don't cancel it;
- the detection box hit skips to the hold / drive / crash, and a miss gives the hover and the dive;
- the detection box catches DownWait, DownBound and a God Fist victim held down (into Damage 0x46, gf_tick stops
  holding it; on the ground: the hold on the ground);
- the v2 scenarios (homing, catches on and off the stage, cut mid-hold, an earlier victim) through the two entries;
- the later v3 changes' checks: under "The dive goes on", "A victim on the ground" and "The crash launches".

### The effects' size and the orb (user, v2's clip `MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261114224043912`)
- "The effects for Core Breaker are too small". In the clip, Supamario is ~90 px tall (12 units):
  - the charge orb: ~37 px radius (~5 units);
  - the charge flash: about his size;
  - the explosion: about Donkey Kong's size.

  In XV2 the orb (EEPK scale 2.8 on a 0.47 m disc) is 1.3 m in radius round a 1.75 m Fu: it surrounds him. v1 / v2
  halved the orb, its flash and the explosion (`SIZE_MUL`, the Q10 default); v3 has no halving.
- "I'd like Core Breaker's effects to be larger in scale than Super God Fist's effects": Core Breaker has its own
  scale, `supamario_corebreaker_fx.CB_FX_SCALE` = **2.25** (each placement's size = XV2's ScaleMin x 2.25). The God
  Fist keeps `xv2_smash_fx.SIZE` 1.75; 2.25 is ~1.3x it (XV2's own proportion, Fu's height to Supamario's, is
  ~x1.36 of the unit scale). The God Fist's files and sizes don't change.

  | Effect | v3 size (v2) |
  |---|---|
  | the orb (`Cor_Crg.emo`) | 6.3 (2.45): it surrounds him from his head |
  | the charge particles (`Cor_Crg_Loop`) | 1.35 (1.05) |
  | the charge flash | 1.575 (0.61) |
  | the explosion | 4.5 (1.75) |
  | the fist fire | 2.25 particles, 3.375 cone (1.75 / 2.625) |
  | the wind, the dive impact, the flare | 2.25 (1.75) |
  | the miss blast | 3.375 (2.625) |
  | the aura | its 7 bones' 0.4-0.8 x 2.25: 0.9-1.8 (x 1.75) |

  Placements are unchanged: bone 0, at the same joints, unturned.
- "the initial circle looks flat. It should cover around supamario like the auras": the first circle of the move is
  the charge orb (`Cor_Crg.emo`). XV2 draws its three meshes (core_a ring, core_b / core_b2 swirls) as flat discs that
  face the camera (EEPK Orientation Camera; the PRLX shader's depth makes it read as a ball). v1 / v2 turned the discs
  to face the side camera: flat, drawn behind him. `xv2_smash_fx.inflate_discs` now makes each disc a closed shell:
  - every vertex is lifted onto the sphere of the disc's radius;
  - a front and a back copy share the disc's UVs, colours and triangles, normals outward.

  Face-on it draws as XV2's disc did; he is inside it (the front half over him, the back half behind). Its centre is
  where it was (his head; at x2.25 it covers him). Facing-safe as before: the turn is baked into the
  model, and the call is on bone 0, unturned (its size 2.8 x 2.25 = 6.3).

### The hitboxes (user: "I'd like for the hitboxes to match the adjustments")
The hitboxes sized to an effect grow with it, by the effect's v3 scale over its v2 one, centred on the effect
(`HB` in `tools/supamario_corebreaker_build.py`; Size as the ACMD's, bone 0):

| Box | v2 | v3 | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| the dive's catch (ID 0, on his fist) | 5.5 | 7.07 | the fist fire: 1.75 -> 2.25 |
| the dive's body box (ID 1) | 4.5 | 4.5 | his body, not an effect |
| the detection box | 2.5 | 2.5 | the user's "very small" box: not scaled |
| the burn (4 hits on the held victim) | 7.0 | 9.0 | the hit loop: 1.75 -> 2.25; moved from the victim's middle (9, 3) onto the hit effect (6.24, 2.57), still round the victim |
| the crash (on the stage) | 9.0 | 23.14 | the explosion: v2's halved 0.875 -> 2.25; at the explosion's place (6.26, 1.93) |
| the explosion (off the stage) | 11.0 | 28.29 | the same ratio, at his fist (6.3, 3.1), where the effect plays |

Preview: `hitboxes_on_effects.png` in the package (each box over its effect at its frame, both facings; grey: the v2
size). The crash / explosion boxes are big (radius 23 / 28 units, about two and a half Supamarios): they hit
bystanders round the explosion as the effect shows it.

### The halos (on, both moves)
After the halo test the user chose the weakest strength, then "the halo glow at 0.25 is too strong. Maybe reduce to
0.16": `xv2_smash_fx.HALO_ALPHA` = **0.16**, size x2.5, built into v3 for **both moves** (the God Fist's effects
change too: approved). `supamario_corebreaker_build.py` runs `supamario_halo_test.build` over the finished bank by
default (`--no-halo` leaves them out); `halo_report.json` lists them: 113 glowing emitters, 9 companion sets, the
three model halos, 63 calls added (attackdash 3, specialhi / specialairhi 30 each). The halo calls carry their
effect's size, so Core Breaker's halos grow with CB_FX_SCALE. The bank: ptcl 4.16 MB.

### The dive goes on (user: "please make the Core Breaker dive continue until either landing or something (unless KOd)")
v1 / v2 ended the dive in FallSpecial after 40 frames (`DIVE_MAX`) or below the rise's start (less 3). Both are gone.
The dive keeps its speed (7.49 a frame) and angle (48 degrees, the light homing) until:
- he lands: the miss's landing, 60 frames of lag (however far below his start);
- the catch connects (the dive's box, or the hover's detection box before it);
- a wall ahead of him or a ceiling (`cb_dive_blocked`: GroundModule 0x2722, 1 the ceiling, 2 / 4 the left / right
  wall, as Sephiroth's `edge_hi_slide` reads them): FallSpecial with no speed, against it. Chosen over sliding along
  it: the dive is straight, so a wall it meets is the stage's side; sliding down it would only end lower;
- he grabs a ledge (as before), is hit out of it (super armor permitting), or is KO'd: diving past the bottom blast
  zone is a self-KO (the user accepts it).

Nothing else ends it:
- his speeds are set every frame: gravity's limit (30) above them, the air speed cap lifted (as before);
- **the motion**: the dive loop's second copy (85-103) is played back to the first's end when it reaches 103
  (`MotionModule(0x2713, frame - 19)`, the game's set-frame, as vanilla Mario's MSC uses it): it loops as long as he
  dives, so the status never reaches the landing segment by itself;
- **the catch checks**: the game script's per-frame checks in the dive were frame blocks (61-103), which stop when the
  motion goes back. They are now one loop at 61, `Set_Loop(180) { if cb = 2 { the check; WAIT(1) } } Goto(-words)`, as
  vanilla's loops (Mario's game.bin: `Goto` minus the block's words). 180 frames is past any blast zone (830 units
  down at the dive's slowest, 4.6 a frame). Once cb isn't 2 (a catch, a refusal, the motion jumping through) the rest
  of the loop runs at once with no waits and the script goes on to the later blocks. Threads were not used: the God
  Fist's in-game test showed a fighter's `Thread_Start` block doing nothing (the runner's bug, fixed in exe_v46m: a
  thread would work now, the loop stays).
- The dive's wind streaks (effect 7) play at their frames on the first pass only; the fist's fire loops until the
  dive ends.

Tests: a dive off the stage still diving after 330+ frames, 1500+ units down, at its speed, armor on, the motion looped
back by 19 each time (never into the landing); a landing 400 below the rise's start (the miss's landing, Wait); a wall
ahead (FallSpecial against it), a wall behind (no stop); the game script's loop (its count, the check, WAIT 1, the
Goto, no frame block in the dive).

### A victim on the ground (user: "if a grounded opponent is hit by Core Breaker, Supamario and the opponent should do the grounded part of the animation instead of being dragged up")
At the catch (the dive's or the detection box's), the victim's situation (SITUATION, `0x0F000116 + 0x20 x target`,
read before its status is changed) picks the path:
- **on the ground: the hold on the ground (phase 8)**, then the crash:
  - he comes down onto its height (`cb_vy`, its Y at the catch: at most the dive's speed a frame) and lands; no lift;
  - it stays on the ground at his fist (9 ahead, its own height), held as before (hitstun, controller off, no stage
    collision);
  - the motion: a new segment `hold_gnd` (frames 238-273, before the crash), the crash's first pose (XV2 #1 290-292,
    nearly still, his feet on the floor), so they're both in the grounded part of the animation;
  - the same 4 burn hits (1.5% each, 4 + 8k frames into the hold), the impact / hit-loop effect, the hit spark and the
    rumble, under their own phase (cb 8), so jumping through one hold never fires the other's;
  - at its end the motion goes straight on into the crash (no drive);
  - over no ground at its end (it stood on an edge he isn't over): the explosion in the air instead (phase 7).
- **in the air: as before**, the hold with the lift, the 45 degree drive, the crash / the explosion in the air.

Total damage on every path: 3 + 4 x 1.5 + 17 = 26% (checked per path in the script).

The segments after it moved by 36: the crash 274-341, the air release 342-385 (the motion is 386 frames).

Tests: a victim on the ground (the phases hold on the ground -> crash, he lands, never goes up, the victim at his fist
on the ground, held every frame, the motion inside hold_gnd, let go at the crash); an airborne victim over the stage
(hold, drive, crash); the detection box on a downed victim (the hold on the ground).

### The crash launches (user: "The grounded explosion also seems extremely weak in terms of knockback")
v1 / v2's crash was a flinch (BKB 20, angle 0), then the victim was handed to the God Fist's put-down (gf_tick:
DownWait, no tech, no KO). Now:
- `CRASH_KB`: **angle 42, KBG 75, BKB 45** (the explosion in the air: angle 40, KBG 60, BKB 30; the crash is
  stronger at every percent); 17%, shieldable (the victim is in hitstun; bystanders can shield), normal DI;
- the MSC lets the victim go (`cb_release`: stage collision and controller back; its rotation is never changed) at the
  crash's first frame, the frame before the hit (CRASH + 1), so it takes the full knockback; no hand-off to gf_tick.
- Knockback for a Mario-weight victim (% before the move, the move's 26% added): 0%: 85, 40%: 125, 80%: 165,
  100%: 186 (the explosion in the air: 62 / 94 / 126 / 142). A rough estimate (the test prints it: launch speed KB x
  0.03 less 0.051 a frame, Mario's gravity, Final Destination-size blast zones 240 to the side and 200 up, no DI, no
  rage) puts the KO from mid-stage at **~105%** before the move (the explosion in the air: ~155% from mid-stage,
  much sooner off the stage, where it happens).

### Tests (v3 build, halos on)
- `supamario_corebreaker_test.py`: everything above, plus every placement at CB_FX_SCALE, the orb shell, and the
  hitbox sizes equal to `HB`: 70 checks, 0 problems;
- `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems;
- `msc_stack_check.py`: 0 stack problems (2364 scripts).

**Not confirmed in game:**
- that `global26` (the hook the game's own special transitions read) refuses a third up special everywhere in the
  air;
- that a status change straight from the rise to an aerial / special works as the God Fist's direct actions did;
- that a downed fighter put in Damage from DownWait leaves its lying pose at once;
- that `MotionModule(0x2713)` (the set-frame) keeps the game script waiting where it is (the catch-check loop is
  built not to depend on it either way), and the wall / ceiling flags (GroundModule 0x2722) during the dive;
- the KO percent of the crash (an estimate).

## Core Breaker v4 (`supamario_corebreaker_v4.zip`): the user's v3 feedback

Install over v3, exe B (no exe change). Seven changes.

### 1. The ground up special uses the rise (user: "The first press of up special on the ground should consume its one rising use")
**Cause:** `cb_reset_tick` was meant to skip its reset while he is in the up special itself (`st == 0x154`). `st` is a
float (the status variable) and `0x154` an int literal, and the compiled compare never matched. So every frame of the
rise's crouch on the ground (status 0x154, on the ground) reset `CB_USED` to 0, right after `cb_init` had set it to 1.
The rise then counted as unused: up special again gave another rise.

**Fix:**
- the compare is `st == 340.0f` (0x154);
- the ground resets the use only after he has been in the air since it was used (`CB_AIR`, New_Var slot 0x25, object
  scope; set in `cb_init` and by the tick whenever he's airborne). This also covers the frame where the status
  variable still reads the status before the up special.

Tests:
- a ground up special with the per-frame reset running, its first frame read as Wait: the use stays 1 from the first
  frame; the second up special gives the hover / dive (use 2); a third is refused in the air; the landing gives it back;
- the reset cases gain "in the move on the ground" (no reset: the v3 bug) and "on the ground, never in the air since"
  (no reset).

### 2. The dive catches again (user: "The catch doesn't happen anymore on the dive unless the point-blank detection box is hit")
v3 had moved the dive's per-frame catch checks into one game-script loop (`Set_Loop(180) { if cb = 2 { Hit_Check
...; WAIT(1) } } Goto`), so that they would keep running while the MSC loops the dive animation.

**What the files show:** I couldn't confirm offline why the loop never caught in game.
- Vanilla Mario's Super Jump Punch uses the same `Set_Loop` / `WAIT` / `Goto` shape, so the loop itself is valid.
- The game's interpreter state (docs/native-notes.md "ACMD threads") keeps its own frame counter, advanced each frame
  by the motion's rate. Setting the motion frame back doesn't rewind the script, so the loop isn't restarted by the
  dive's loop either.
- So the exact cause stays open. One candidate is v3's ordering at frame 60: `Hit_Reset` / `Hit_Log_Clear(0)` came
  right after the dive's hitboxes were set, and clearing Part 0's hit records right after setting its hitboxes could
  disable them.

**Fix, robust either way:** the dive's catch is the MSC's own (`cb_dive_catch`, every dive frame, before the landing
check, since the hit and the touch-down can come together):
- **the hit:** his attack's hit result, `AttackModule(0x2726, 2)` / `AttackModule(0x2727, 2)` (a hurtbox; kind bits as
  in docs/native-notes.md; the same flags ACMD `Hit_Check` reads). The flag is cleared at the dive's start
  (`AttackModule(0x2724)`, the game's own Hit_Reset), so a detection-box hit before doesn't count.
- **who:** target 2 (the last fighter hit: its status, body state, damage, kind, docs/position-variables.md). Its
  player slot is the slot whose status, damage % and fighter kind are the same as target 2's, and which isn't him.
- **refused** as before: lying down (the down statuses, the dizzy status) or invincible / intangible. The hitboxes go
  (`AttackModule(0x2719)`) and the dive goes on.
- **caught:** invincible 187 frames (the hold: the self `INVINCIBLE` variable, `HitModule(1, N, 0)`, as the
  detection catch's `Body_State_Set`), the hitboxes gone, the slot in `cb_slot`, then `cb_catch` as before (the hold
  on the ground or in the air).

The game script keeps the dive's hitboxes (now after `Hit_Reset` / `Hit_Log_Clear(0)` at frame 60) and the hover's
detection box with its own checks (which work, per the user).

Tests:
- a fighter standing in the dive's path, more than 30 from the detection box: caught (slot 2, the hitboxes cleared,
  invincible), the hold on the ground, the crash;
- a fighter lying down and an invincible one: refused (the hitboxes gone, the dive goes on and lands);
- the game script has no catch checks between 60 and the landing, and the hit flag / Part 0 record are cleared before
  the dive's hitboxes.

### 3. Effects x1.5, but not the aura (user: "The effects look better but only for the dive's aura. Please resize them to be 1.5x larger than what they were before")
Read as: the aura on his body (BAC effect 5) stays at its v3 size; every other Core Breaker effect is 1.5x its v3 size.
Done in `SIZE_MUL` (effects 0, 1, 2, 3, 7, 8, 9, 12) and `CMN_MUL` (the common dust / clouds / sparks the move calls).
The halos' calls carry their effect's size, so they follow. The God Fist is unchanged.

| Effect | v4 (v3) |
|---|---|
| the orb | 9.45 (6.3) |
| the charge particles | 2.025 (1.35) |
| the charge flash | 2.3625 (1.575) |
| the fist fire | 3.375 particles, 5.0625 cone (2.25 / 3.375) |
| the wind, the dive impact / hit loop, the flare | 3.375 (2.25) |
| the explosion (crash and air) | 6.75 (4.5) |
| the miss blast | 5.0625 (3.375) |
| the aura | 0.9-1.8 (unchanged) |
| the rise dust / the landing and crash clouds / the crash smoke / the hit spark | 1.5 / 1.2 / 1.8 / x1.5 (1.0 / 0.8 / 1.2) |

The hitboxes sized to those effects grow with them (`HB`, `FX_MUL` 1.5):

| Box | v3 | v4 |
|---|---|---|
| the dive's catch | 7.07 | 10.61 |
| the burn | 9.0 | 13.5 |
| the crash | 23.14 | 34.71 |
| the explosion in the air | 28.29 | 42.44 |
| the dive's body box, the detection box | 4.5, 2.5 | unchanged |

### 4. The burn hits are coins (user: "please make the catch multihits of the dive use the coin hit effect")
The 4 burn hits (both holds) are Element 6 (Coin), SFX type 4 (Coin 1), SFX level 1. These are vanilla Super Jump
Punch's (Mario's specialhi: every hit Effect 6, SFXType 4, level 1, its last hit SFXType 30 "Coin 2";
reference/spreadsheets Element / SFX_Type). Damage and knockback are unchanged. XV2's hit effects on the victim (the
impact and its loop, the hit spark) stay, drawn with the coins.

The crash and the explosion in the air had Element 1 ("Detect", Raptor Boost's: transit on hit) since v1. They are
now 0 (normal); nothing else about them changed.

### 5. The rise stays in its animation, actionable (user: "make it so that the Up special leap doesn't cut to fall, but keeps the same animation while being actionable")
No Fall after the rise:
- the rise's motion plays to its last frame (29) and holds it (rate 0) until he acts or lands. It never reaches the
  hover's frames, so the detection box isn't set by mistake;
- from frame 16 (`RISE_CANCEL`) he acts as in Fall (`cb_rise_act`, the God Fist's `gf_air_act` pattern):
  - once: the air transition groups (`func_40`) and the terms on (`WorkModule 0x16`: aerials, specials, air dodge,
    jumps, the landings 0x1e00001c / 0x1e00001b);
  - every frame: the game's own air transitions (`func_41`), then the inputs directly: down / side / neutral special,
    air dodge (0x22), an aerial (0x34), a double jump (0xC / 0xD) while he has one;
  - up special: the hover / dive in place (as v3);
  - fast fall: the fall's own `func_550` (`func_553`'s stick flick, the game's fast-fall flag);
- **drift:** from frame 16 the kinetic type is 0 (FALL: "air mobility enabled"), with his own forward rise speed off,
  so the stick drifts him;
- **the leap's height is kept:** its vertical speed and its own slowing go on to its top (55 up, as before). From the
  top (the held frame), his own gravity and fall speed;
- no armor (see 7);
- **landing:** the game's own landing (`func_55`: LandingLight / LandingHeavy by its terms), else LandingLight (0x17).
  No landing lag beyond normal. The use stays spent until that landing.

Tests:
- the rise alone: no Fall, its last frame held (never frame 30), LandingLight on the floor, the rise still used, armor
  to its top, the FALL kinetic type with no forward rise speed;
- each action (an aerial, the three other specials, an air dodge, a double jump) works from frame 16 in the rise and
  in its held top, and not before it (pressed at 14 it just lands);
- up special in the rise or from the held top gives the hover / dive;
- an air start keeps its top held in the air (no Fall).

### 6. The rise's B-reverse (user: "make the up special leap B-reversable/change facing direction based on direction held at the start of the move")
- **The window:** the rise's first 4 frames (`TURN_WINDOW`, before the lift-off at 6). Vanilla Super Jump Punch checks
  its turn at frame 4 (its flag 0x2100000F, then `func_33`). Once per rise.
- **The turn:** the stick held back along his facing (below -0.5, `TURN_STICK`, as the hover's) turns him
  (`PostureModule(1)` ReverseLr, then `PostureModule(2)`).
- **In the air:** his horizontal momentum turns too, as Smash's B-reverse: energy 2 (the stick's air control, his air
  speed) and energy 3 get their x negated (`KineticModuleF(0xa / 0xb, energy)` read their x / y, as vanilla's MSC
  does). For that momentum to mean something, an air rise now keeps his air momentum at the lift-off (only its
  vertical part is set). v3 zeroed energy 2 there; a ground rise still does.
- Forward or neutral: nothing.
- The rise's own forward speed follows his facing, so it goes the new way.

Tests: ground and air, both facings, stick back / forward / neutral: turned once only with the stick back, the air
momentum turned with him; a stick back after the window does nothing.

### 7. Armor and invincibility (user: "make it so there's no armor during the leap or hover, and the dive has armor instead of invincibility", then "He should keep invincibility during the hold only")
| Part | v3 | v4 |
|---|---|---|
| the rise | super armor to frame 30 | nothing |
| the hover | nothing | nothing |
| the dive | super armor | super armor, never invincible |
| the hold (catch -> burn hits -> drive -> crash / explosion) | invincible 187 frames from the catch | the same (the user: "invincibility during the hold only"), no armor |

The only invincibility writes are at the catch: the detection box's `Body_State_Set` (the game script) and the dive
catch's INVINCIBLE variable (the MSC). Nothing else in the move sets a body state: XV2's own invulnerability window
was never ported. Any end of the status during the hold still lets the victim go (`cb_end` -> `cb_release`: its
controller and stage collision back).

Tests: no armor in the rise or the hover; the dive armored with no invincibility written before the catch; one
invincibility write, at the catch; no armor in the hold / crash; a status cut mid-hold lets the victim go.

### Tests (v4 build, halos 0.16)
- `supamario_corebreaker_test.py`: 81 checks, 0 problems (the earlier lists still hold, except where v4 changed them);
- `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems;
- `msc_stack_check.py`: 0 stack problems (2366 scripts).

Package: only the MSC and the game / effect scripts changed from v3 (the effect sizes are in the effect script's calls;
the ptcl / efc and the motion are v3's).

**Not confirmed in game:**
- the MSC catch: that target 2 (the last fighter hit) is the dive's victim on the frame its hit flag shows, and the slot
  match (status, damage, kind) finds it; two fighters of the same kind, % and status hit together would be ambiguous;
- the kinetic FALL type during the rise: that the stick drift and the game's fast fall work from the rise's status as
  in Fall; that the leap's own slowing holds after the type change;
- the B-reverse's momentum flip through energy 2 / 3 (KineticModuleF 0xa / 0xb and KineticModule 0x11 on energy 2);
- the burn hits' coins on a held victim (hitstun, no stage collision).

## Core Breaker v5 (`supamario_corebreaker_v5.zip`): the user's v4 clips

Install over v4, exe B (exe_v46j: v46i plus acmd_ext's Motion_Set fix; no exe change). Four clips
(`MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261115000020473` / `...000243660` / `...000416068` / `...000723961`, 1296x778 at 60 fps).
Six fixes, each with a test that fails on the v4 build (`supamario_corebreaker_test.py` run on v4's output: 20
problems, among them each of the six below).

### 1. A hit but no hold (clip 1)
**What the clip shows:**
- he hovers with the orb over Donkey Kong (the detection box at his middle misses DK's body);
- the dive starts point-blank: DK flashes red on its first frames (1.5-1.7 s), takes 3% (118 -> 121);
- he passes through and lands behind DK, with no hold.

In clip 3 a dive that travelled before hitting did catch.

**Cause:** v4's `cb_dive_start` called `AttackModule(0x2724)` (the hit flag's reset) on the dive's first frame. A hit
on that frame (the game script sets the dive's boxes at 60, they connect, then the MSC's frame runs) was wiped. Its
hit record then kept the boxes from hitting DK again, so the flag never came back.

**Fix:**
- no reset in the MSC: the game script's `Hit_Reset` at 60, before the hitboxes, is the only one;
- if target 2's values match no player slot, `cb_near_slot` takes the nearest other fighter to his fist (within 20).

**Test:** a hit on the dive's first frame catches (v4: phases hover, dive, landing; no slot).

### 2. The grey invincibility flashing after the move (clip 4, from 2.9 s)
**Cause:** the hold's invincibility is a timed state, 187 frames from the catch (the air path's catch-to-crash-end).
The hold on the ground's path is shorter (catch at 238, the crash's end 341), so it ran past the move's end; nothing
cleared it.

**Fix:** `cb_end` (every end of the status: the crash's Wait, the air explosion's FallSpecial, any cut) writes
INVINCIBLE 0 when the catch set it (`cb_inv`). Nothing is written without a catch, so respawn invincibility is never
touched.

**Test:** the crash, the air explosion and a cut mid-hold each end with INVINCIBLE 0; a rise alone writes nothing.

### 3. The leap's hitbox (clip 2: DK takes 3% from the leap's held pose falling on him, no hit effect)
**Cause:** it was the dive's catch box and body box (3%, Element 0), from game-script frame 60 to the landing
segment's `Remove_All_Hitboxes`.
- v4 held the rise's last frame with motion rate 0, but the game script runs on its own frame count. It reached the
  hover's detection box at 30 (a 2-frame blip) and the dive's boxes at 60, with cb 0.
- Those blocks weren't guarded by the phase (v3 left the status at frame 30, so they were never reached there).

**Fix:**
- the rise no longer holds (item 4);
- the detection box is guarded by cb 1, the dive's boxes and the dive's rumble by cb 1-2;
- the hover / dive XV2 effects by their phases: the orb and flash cb 1, the aura / fist fire / wind cb 1-2. So no
  frame count can bring them out in the rise.

**Test:** every hitbox and every XV2 effect call sits inside a cb guard that cb 0 fails (v4: 3 hitboxes and the
effects unguarded).

### 4. The leap ends in Fall
The user called v4's endless hold a bug. The rise plays its whole animation (actionable from frame 16 as in v4: the
air actions, drift, up special = the hover / dive in place), then Fall at its last frame (29). From Fall everything
is normal; up special there is the hover / dive (the use rules unchanged).
