**Tests:** Fall at 29 (never 30), in the air, the rise used; each action from 16 to the end, not before; an air
start: Fall at 29.

### 5. A dive catch on the ground plays the air hold (clip 3)
**What clip 3 shows:** the dive catches DK standing on the stage. Supamario holds him in the air pose (the grab_hold
segment), then the drive and the crash. The point-blank detection catch (clip 4) took the hold on the ground.

**Cause:** both catches go through `cb_catch`, which read the victim's situation live. The detection catch reads it
in the game script's check right after the hit. The dive's MSC catch reads it after the hit's freeze, by which time
the catch hit (BKB 20) can have the victim's situation in the air.

**Fix:** `cb_gtrack` keeps the player slots on the ground every hover / dive frame until his attack has hit (and once
at the hover's start). `cb_catch` uses that frame-before-the-hit mask for both catches; the live read is only a
fallback.

**Test:** a fighter on the ground whose situation reads the air by the time the catch is read: the hold on the
ground and the crash (v4: the air hold, the drive).

### 6. The ending effects too large
The user: far too large, except the explosion.
- Back to v3's sizes: the hit and its loop (8), the miss blast (9), the flare (12), and the common dust / landing
  cloud / crash cloud / crash smoke / hit spark (`CMN_MUL` 1.0).
- In clip 3's crash (2.9-4.0 s) the smoke and the clouds spread wider than the explosion's own core. I didn't
  measure them to a size; v3's are the last sizes the user didn't call too big, so they go back to those.
- The explosion (3), the orb, the flash, the fist fire and the wind keep v4's x1.5.
- The burn box follows its hit loop back: 13.5 -> 9.0. The catch, crash and explosion boxes are unchanged.

**Test:** those calls at v3's sizes, every common one at 1.2 or less.

### Tests (v5 build, halos 0.16)
- `supamario_corebreaker_test.py`: 87 checks, 0 problems;
- `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems;
- `msc_stack_check.py`: 0 stack problems (2368 scripts).

**Not confirmed in game:** that writing INVINCIBLE 0 (`HitModule(1, 0, 0)`) ends the timed invincibility at once, and
that target 2 is readable from the MSC on every catch (the nearest-fighter fallback covers a failed match).

## Core Breaker v6 (`supamario_corebreaker_v6.zip`): the dive's catch on the ground = the point-blank catch's end

Install over v5, exe B (exe_v46j, no exe change). The user's clip (`150542b9-...-render.mp4`, 1920x1080 at 60 fps):
"The point-blank hitbox plays the animation how I like it currently. Hitting the dive on a grounded/near ground
opponent should work the same, using the same final hit and leaving Supamario in the grounded finisher animation."

### What the clip shows
- **0-2.6 s, the point-blank catch:** Donkey Kong is in the air over him. The detection box catches him, the air hold
  rises with the coins, then the drive down and the crash on the stage: the big yellow explosion (CRASH_KB, angle 42 /
  KBG 75 / BKB 45) launches DK far (27% -> 53%). Supamario ends in the crash animation on the ground (the grounded
  finisher: crouched, the slide back, standing).
- **4.4-8 s, the dive on DK standing:** the hold on the ground with the coins (v5's fix), then the hold's end took the
  explosion in the air (phase 7: angle 40 / KBG 60 / BKB 30, the weaker hit at his fist). DK is thrown a short way
  and lies on the stage (79%); Supamario ends with the air release, not the crash.

### Cause
The hold on the ground eased him down onto the victim's height exactly (speed = the distance left). He could stop just
above the floor with no speed left, so he never touched it and the game never landed him. At the hold's end
`global18` still read the air, so it took the explosion in the air.

### Fix
- **Landing:** in the hold on the ground he goes down at the dive's speed until he lands (the ground stops him). If
  he gets more than `GROUND_MISS` (15) below the victim's ground, there's no ground under him: he stops there, and the
  hold's end takes the explosion in the air.
- **Near the ground counts as on it:** `cb_gtrack` now also keeps each player slot's height whenever it's on the
  ground during the hover / dive (New_Vars `cb_gy1`-`cb_gy8`, object scope), until his attack hits.
  - At the catch (`cb_catch`, the same function for the detection box and the dive), a fighter on the ground the frame
    before the hit takes the hold on the ground.
  - So does one seen on the ground this hover / dive that is now no more than `NEAR_GROUND` (15) above that ground,
    and not more than 3 below it (below it is off the stage).
  - The victim is held on that ground (a near-ground one is put down on it).
- **Clearly airborne fighters** (higher than 15, never seen on the ground this hover / dive, or off the stage) keep
  the air hold, the drive, then the crash on the stage or the explosion in the air.
- **The same end on both paths:** the hold on the ground and the drive both end in `cb_crash_start` and the crash
  segment (the victim let go at its first frame, the crash hitbox / effects at CRASH + 1, the slide back, Wait). The
  final hit and the finisher animation are the same code and frames as the point-blank catch's.

Note: the point-blank catch in the clip was on a DK in the air, so it took the air hold and the drive. The dive now
uses the hold on the ground for standing and near-ground fighters (the v3 request: "the grounded part of the
animation instead of being dragged up") and ends with the same crash. If the user wants the air hold and drive there
too, that's a one-line change in `cb_catch`.

### Supamario takes 7% from the hold (user: "make Supamario take 7% in damage during the hold multihits for all versions of Core Breaker", "he should also have the burn effect put on him")
- On each of the 4 burn hits' frames (4 + 8k into either hold), `cb_burn_self` adds 1.75% to his own percent through
  the DAMAGE variable (`0x0F000117`: the game's counter, no hit). He doesn't flinch and nothing breaks the hold (he's
  invincible). It counts as normal damage.
- **The burn on him:** on the same frames the effect script plays the common "fire puff (fire damage)" effect
  (`0x1000011`, Global_GFX_IDs 0x11, the puff fire-element hits draw) on his middle: bone 0, 6 up, facing-safe, size 1,
  guarded by the hold's phase.
- Smash's colour flash for fire hits comes with a real hit, and he's invincible, so it isn't reproduced. The puff is
  the visible burn.
- The victim's coins (Element 6) and the XV2 hit loop are unchanged.

### A hit after the rise keeps the dive (user: "make it so if Supamario is hit during or after the leap part of Core Breaker (without doing the dive yet) he does the dive next time he uses up special (until it gets reset by landing, getting hit out of the dive if possible, grabbing ledge, ko, etc)")
`cb_reset_tick` splits v3's reset range (0x3F-0x4C):

| Event | Rise used (1) | Hover / dive used (2) |
|---|---|---|
| a hit: Damage .. DamageFall (0x45-0x4C), during the rise or after it | kept: the next up special is the hover / dive | reset (as before) |
| grabbed / thrown (CapturePull .. Thrown, 0x3F-0x44) | reset (as before; `CB_HIT_LO` moves the line) | reset |
| landing (on the ground after being in the air), a ledge, KO / respawn / entry | reset | reset |

A rise interrupted by a hit leaves the status with the use at 1, so it keeps the hover / dive too. A hit in the hover
counts as the dive's (2): reset.

**When the uses are spent** (user: "make sure that getting hit during/after the rise only consumes it after the
vertical speed begins being added"):
- the rise is used (1) at its lift-off, frame 6 (`RISE_FROM`, the first frame its upward speed is set), not when up
  special is pressed. Cut in the crouch (frames 0-5, e.g. hit), it isn't spent, and the next up special is the rise
  again. On the ground it's spent at the lift-off too, so v4's ground fix holds.
- the hover / dive is used (2) at the hover's start (the up special press), as before: the move commits there.

Tests: a hit in the crouch -> the rise again; a hit after the lift-off -> the hover / dive.

Tests:
- with the rise used: a hit, a tumble or a ground hit keep it, while grabbed / thrown reset it; with the dive used, a
  hit resets it;
- rise -> hit -> up special = the hover / dive; rise -> hit -> landing -> the rise; dive -> hit out of it -> the rise;
  rise -> ledge -> the rise.

### No staling (user: "make all of Core Breaker's hitboxes unaffected by staling")
- The up special status's setup (`func_2286`, `StatusModule(0x2713, ...)`) now always passes 0 as the 6th value: the
  status's attack log. Vanilla logs Mario's up special there as 0x1422. Every Core Breaker hitbox is in that status,
  so none is logged: they don't stale, and using the move doesn't push anything into the stale queue.
- 0 is what vanilla itself does for its no-damage custom up special (Super Jump). The evidence and what isn't traced
  are in docs/native-notes.md "The status's attack log and staling".
- It isn't a hitbox field, so Sm4shCommand's labels don't change.
- Test: the status data's 6th value is 0 for every custom move ID.
- To check in game: Core Breaker on a training dummy several times running should stay at 3 + 4 x 1.5 + 17.

### Tests (v6 build, halos 0.16)
- dive catches:
  - a standing fighter: the hold on the ground, he lands, the victim on its ground, the crash;
  - one whose height reads 0.5 above the floor (v5: no landing, the air explosion): the same;
  - one that jumped 10 up at the dive's start: the same, put down on its ground;
  - one that jumped 40 up: the air hold, the drive, the crash.

  Each ends exactly as the point-blank catch does: the crash phase, the same crash frames, Wait on the ground.
- his percent +1.75 on each burn hit's frame, +7 in all, on the hold on the ground, the air hold with the crash, the
  air hold with the explosion off the stage, and the point-blank catch.
- the new reset rule's cases and sequences (above);
- `supamario_corebreaker_test.py`: 93 checks, 0 problems; `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems;
  `msc_stack_check.py`: 0 stack problems (2369 scripts).

**Not confirmed in game:**
- that the DAMAGE write raises his percent with no side effect;
- that the fire puff (`0x1000011`) reads as a burn on him;
- that he lands at the dive's speed in the hold on the ground on every floor (slopes, platforms).

## Core Breaker v7 (`supamario_corebreaker_v7.zip`)

Install over v6, exe B (exe_v46j, no exe change). The user's clip `MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261115005737991` (1296x778,
60 fps, 6.7 s):
- **0-1.3 s:** a crash on the stage. Supamario stands at the crash site afterwards; his HUD icon looks faded in some
  frames after the animation. At this size the clip doesn't show the grey flash clearly; the report is the user's.
- **2-4.4 s:** at the stage's right edge he hovers beside Donkey Kong, who is in the air just past the ledge. The
  point-blank box catches DK, the hold (coins, Supamario's own +7%: 98 -> 105), then the drive down and right, away
  from the stage. Both go below the ledge and the explosion goes off under the stage; DK is KO'd (109%).

### 1. The invincibility after the move
The user means Supamario: nothing in the move writes the victim's body state.

**Cause:**
- v5 / v6 gave him 187 frames of timed invincibility at the catch (`HitModule(1, 187)` through the INVINCIBLE
  variable, or `Body_State_Set` for the point-blank catch), sized for the air path's catch-to-crash-end.
- At the status's end they wrote 0 to clear it. If the game takes a 0-frame write as "no change" (or keeps the larger
  timer), nothing is cleared, and the hold on the ground's path is 84 frames shorter, so the rest runs on after the
  finisher.
- MSC can't read the timer's frames (INVINCIBLE reads 1 / 0), so the clear can't be read back.

**Fix:** invincibility is no longer one long timer.
- `cb_update` renews it every frame he holds the victim (`INV_STEP`, 2 frames), in the hold, the drive, the hold on
  the ground, and the crash / the explosion until the victim is let go.
- At the release it isn't renewed, so it ends within 2 frames whatever the game does with any write.
- The catch writes are 2 / 3 frames (not 187). The status's end writes 1 frame, not 0.

**Tests:**
- a write every frame of the hold, the last one at the release frame or before, none above 2;
- only the 1-frame end after the release;
- each end (the crash, the explosion in the air, a cut) ends on the 1-frame write.

### 2. Dragged under the ledge
**Cause:** the drive's floor (where it turns into the explosion in the air) was the rise's start less 3, but at least
30 below the catch. Caught at the ledge's height and facing away from the stage, the drive went 30+ down past the
ledge. And a catch from under a ledge pulled the victim down to his fist.

**Fix:**
- **The floor** (`cb_floor_at`): the highest known ground at or below him at the catch, less 0.5. Known grounds are
  the victim's last ground (tracked in the hover / dive), his own last ground (tracked every frame in
  `cb_reset_tick`) and the rise's start. On the stage the drive lands first (the crash); past the ledge it stops at
  the stage's height and explodes there. Nobody is carried below it.
- **Under** (`cb_under`), when either holds:
  - the victim is more than 8 above his feet;
  - the victim is on a ledge (its situation 1 or a cliff status);
  - he is below every known ground.

  Then the victim is held where it was caught (its offset at the catch, not at his fist). The hold rises as before,
  then there's no drive: the explosion in the air in place.

**Tests:**
- the clip's case (rose from the stage by its edge, the point-blank catch past the ledge, facing away): the drive
  stops within one step of the stage's height, and the explosion goes off there;
- under the ledge, a victim standing at it: the hold, the explosion in place, nobody lower than at the hold's start,
  FallSpecial;
- a victim hanging on the ledge: the same.

### Not confirmed in game
- whether a 0-frame INVINCIBLE write was really ignored (the renewal makes it not matter);
- that the INVINCIBLE write of 2 frames every frame keeps the grey flash on only while he holds.

## Core Breaker v8 (`supamario_corebreaker_v8.zip`): shields

Install over v7, exe B (exe_v46j, no exe change).

### 1. No invincibility from a shielded dive (user: "When Core Breaker hits a shield, Supamario gets invulnerability during the landing")
The only writes of his invincibility are the catch's (v7: 2-frame renewals while holding, the 1-frame end). A shield
hit reaching them means a catch started on a shielding fighter. Three ways that could happen:
- the MSC's hit flags (`AttackModule 0x2726 / 0x2727` with kind 2, "a hurtbox"); the kind bits are only known from
  vanilla's uses (docs/native-notes.md), not confirmed;
- the nearest-fighter fallback (v5), when target 2 doesn't match a slot after a shield hit;
- the point-blank box's check.

**Fix:** a fighter in a shield status (GuardOn 0x1B .. GuardDamage 0x1E) or its break (0x59 / 0x5A) is never caught:
- `cb_dive_catch` returns before anything is written;
- the nearest-fighter fallback skips shielding fighters;
- the point-blank box's check refuses them (1.25: the hover goes on).

No catch means no invincibility write, no hold and the normal miss landing.

### 2. 2/3 of the shield and 16 frames of shieldstun (user: "make Core Breaker's dive do 2/3 of an opponent's shield and have 16 frames of shieldstun")
`cb_dive_shield`, every dive frame, once a move: his attack's shield hit (`AttackModule 0x2726 / 0x2727` with kind
4). The shielding fighter is the nearest one in a shield status to his fist (within 40). Then:
- **Shield damage:** its shield health before the hit, less 2/3 of the max shield health.
  - "Before the hit" = now + the hit's own shield damage: 3 x the common shield damage multiplier `0x020000ca`
    (1.19) + the base `0x020000cb` (0), read from the game.
  - The max is the common param `0x020000c2` (fighter_param_common "Shield HP" 50), the same for every fighter, so
    2/3 is exactly 33.33 for all of them.
  - Written through SHIELD_HP (docs/position-variables.md). The catch box and the body box share Part 0, so the
    shield takes one hit, and it's counted once.
- **Too little left:** its shield breaks (status 0x59, Shieldbreak, as the docs' example breaks a shield).
- **Shieldstun:** 16 frames, whatever the hit's damage, through PUSH (1000 + 16: the guard damage status's count of
  frames left; Sephiroth's test 153).

No staling (v6) means the hit's own shield damage is always 3 x 1.19.

### Tests (v8 build, halos 0.16)
- a shielded dive with a full shield, with 40 left, and with 30 left:
  - no catch, no invincibility write, the miss's landing;
  - the shield at 16.67 / 6.67 (2/3 of 50 below its health before the hit);
  - shieldstun 1016 (16 frames);
  - with 30 left, broken (0x59).

## Fake Death (grounded down special): phase 1, the XV2 skill read

User: put Xenoverse 2's **Fake Death** (Yamcha's Super skill, skill folder `116_YMC_DIE`) on his **grounded** down
special; the aerial down special stays F.L.U.D.D. Files given: the skill's BAC / BCM / BAS / EAN (`_C`, `_F`, `_MAM`;
no EEPK, no BDM: it uses the common ones) and its SE bank `CAR_BTL_S116_DIE_SE.acb` (internal name
`BIRD_BTL_S116_DIE_SE`, waveforms in the ACB). `tools/supamario_fakedeath_xv2.py report` ->
`reference/xv2/fakedeath_skill.json` (names from LazyBone152's XV2-Tools, read from its source).

### What the files say
- **BCM:** two siblings under the root: **#1 "Standing"** (PrimaryActivator 0xC01, the button held: HoldDownConditions
  0x20000, MaximumLoopDuration 480) plays **BAC 0** (the ground version, the one ported); #2 "Floating" plays BAC 4, with
  a child #3 (the button still held in BAC 4's Supers link window 50-55) -> BAC 5 (a longer lie). BAC 1 / 6 (the wake-up
  alone) are not reached from the BCM. No counter: no CounterMelee / OnAttackHit condition, no "on connect" entries.
- **BAC 0 (skill EAN #0 YMC_SPA_DIE_C, 158 frames, MoveWithAxis X / Z: the root moves him):**
  - 0-19 **Invulnerability "All Attacks Miss"**; the collapse: a hop back (b_C_Base 1.14 m back, up 0.6 m at 8) onto his
    back by ~20, settled by 32. Frame 1: skill SE cue 0 **FakeDown**, a Stage BG effect 20 (the stage's own dust: not in
    the files), character voice 57 (left out: no voices).
  - 30: BTL_CMN effect 26 **Btl_RvlRingON** on b_C_Pelvis: XV2's **knocked-out "revive" ring** (he fakes a KO).
  - 32-92: function 0x22 **"BAC Loop (instant end)"**: lying still, looped while the button is held (BCM: at most 480
    frames); released (or tapped) it ends at once and goes on at 92. Function 0x17 ("Recover Health (instant)" in
    XV2-Tools) runs 0-92 with all parameters 0: nothing known it does. Not invulnerable while lying.
  - 92-157 the wake-up: the ring off at 93, **tracking 94-108** (94-101 forward and backwards: he turns to his target),
    skill SE cue 1 **WakeUp** at 95, voice 51 (left out), the wind-up back (1.5 m) then a kip-up kick springing forward
    108-120 (the root +5.4 m plus the BAC's Movement 0.2 m a frame 110-116), **the hitbox 112-116** (BDM #20 of CMN.bdm,
    20 damage / 2 guarded, at the root + 0.5 m up + 0.75 m ahead, 0.75 m), the recovery to his stance by 157. No
    BcmCallback (no cancel) in entry 0; entry 1's movement / guard / dash window is 122-144.
- **CMN.bdm #20:** a Standard hit (stun 30 frames, hitstop 9 / 9, a 0.25 m push); struck in a floating hit: a knock
  away (32 frames); struck guarding: blocked. Hit effect BTL_CMN 2 (Btl_HitL), no hit sound. XV2's damage 20 is small
  (Core Breaker's crash is 405): a light hit whose point is the stun.
- **xv2bac.py (shared) misreads two fields** (not changed here): an Effect item's bone is at +0xA (`i_a`) and its id an
  i32 at +0x10 (the `bone` it prints is the id's high half); a Hitbox's +0x10 is "stamina taken when blocked" and its BDM
  file is +0x13's low nibble (0 common). `supamario_fakedeath_xv2.py` reads them right. The SE bank is the older ACB
  layout (waveform `Id`, not `MemoryAwbId`): `xv2acb.py extract` fails on it; `supamario_fakedeath_xv2.py se-extract`
  handles it.

## Fake Death v1 (`supamario_fakedeath_v1.zip`): the grounded down special

Install over Core Breaker v8, exe B (`exe_v46k`, no exe change). Built by `tools/supamario_fakedeath_build.py` (it runs
Core Breaker's build, which runs the God Fist's: their files are theirs byte for byte except the shared ones below).

### The user's spec, and what was chosen
| | |
|---|---|
| The collapse | XV2 0-31 at 1:1 (motion SpecialLwStart 0-31), **intangible frames 1-19** (motion 0-18: XV2's "All Attacks Miss" 0-19) |
| The hold | the special button still held when the collapse ends (frame 33): lying, looped (XV2 32-51, a still pose) until it is released or **`HOLD_MAX` = 300 frames from the move's start** (then the attack comes out as if released). **Not intangible while lying** (XV2 isn't) |
| A tap | released before frame 33: the collapse plays out, the wake-up starts at frame 33 |
| The attack | XV2 92-157 at 1:1 (motion SpecialLwHold 0-65), **intangible from its first frame to its frame 52** (XV2 144: the end of XV2's own move / guard window), turned once at its frame 2 toward the nearest opponent if it is behind him (XV2's TrackForwardAndBackwards), Wait after its last frame |
| Hit frames | the attack's frames 20-23 (XV2 112-115): tap = game frames 53-56; he acts on frame 99 (tap) |
| On shield | the ground hitbox (6%): shieldstun INT(6 x 0.58 + 4) - 2 = 5; he acts 46 frames after the first active frame: **-41 on shield** (-38 on the last active frame); he is intangible to the attack's frame 52, so the punish window is its last 13 frames (53-65) |
| Grounded victim | **ground-only hitbox (Ground/Air 1)**: 6%, angle 0, knockback 1 (BKB 1, KBG 0), no SDI, no trip, hitstop 9 / 9 (XV2's), hitstun +60 natively (HitstunMul -60). The MSC (`fd_hits` / `fd_tick`) then holds its hitstun (renewed every frame: 60 x the repeat's factor once the hitstop ends) and its X where it was hit. **+14 on hit** from the first active frame (60 vs his 46) |
| Repeats | the n-th ground hit on the **same fighter (player slot)** within **300 frames** of its previous one: hitstun **x1, x0.6, x0.35, x0.2** (60, 36, 21, 12); 300 frames without one resets it. Another fighter has its own count. Kept in New_Vars 0x00-0x1F (count, last time, held hitstun, X per slot) and 0x3D (the clock) |
| Airborne victim | **air-only hitbox (Ground/Air 2)**: 3%, angle 45, **BKB 80, KBG 25**, the game's own hitstun and hitlag, no MSC hold, not counted. Mario weight, no DI: knockback 84.8 at 0% (hitstun 33, ~46 units away), 93.5 at 100% (37, ~56 away): about the same distance at any percent (tumble from 0%) |
| Which hitbox | the game picks by the fighter's own situation at the collision (before the hit's knockback), so a hit that pops someone up can't be misread; the MSC's own record (`fd_gtrack`: on the ground the frame before the hit, as Core Breaker v6) only decides whom it holds |
| Movement | his speed (energy 3) each frame from XV2's root (no TransN): back 5.9 in the collapse, forward 5.9 net over the attack (the root's +5.7 / -1.7 / +1.9 and the BAC's Movement 6.0); GroundModule's ledge stop as F.L.U.D.D.'s ground start; off the ground: Fall |
| Effects | Smash's dash cloud at frame 1 (XV2's stage dust); the **revive ring** (BTL_CMN 26, `PtcSupamarioFdBtl_RvlRingON`, id 0x03410057) at frame 30 on bone 0 at his lying hips (2.79 up), size **`FD_FX_SCALE` 1.36** (XV2's proportion: Supamario ~12 units / Yamcha 1.75 m / 5.04), HDR from its glare (capped 2.0), one halo (its glowing line, HALO_ALPHA 0.16); its rings turned Y 90 to face the side camera (XV2 draws them facing his front, which its camera behind him sees face-on; a symmetric ring looks the same both facings); ended at the wake-up (script and MSC) and at any end of the status. The hit's spark is Smash's own (XV2's Btl_HitL is on-hit only) |
| Sounds | FakeDown at 1, WakeUp at the attack's 3 (XV2 95), levelled to -0.5 dBFS peak: tones `fd_fakedown` 0x0CB10098, `fd_wakeup` 0x0CB10099 (RMS over 0.5 s -13.1 / -9.5 dB). No voices |
| Staling | the status's attack log is F.L.U.D.D.'s (0x1), unchanged |

### How (files)
- **Two motion slots** (`tools/supamario_fakedeath_anim.py`): 0x229 SpecialLwStart = the collapse and the lying loop
  (52 frames), 0x22D SpecialLwHold = the attack (66 frames). The attack is its own motion so its scripts start at 0
  whatever the lying lasted (the game script keeps its own frame count while the MSC loops a motion: Core Breaker v4 /
  v5). Supamario's motion table gives every custom slot (`_c2` ...) the same animation, so there is no free slot without
  an exe change: the ground F.L.U.D.D. start / hold were taken (they only played on the ground). An **air F.L.U.D.D.
  that lands** now keeps playing its air start / hold animation (0x239 / 0x23D) on the ground (`func_2267` / `func_2270`);
  everything else about it is as before. 0x22D is a looping slot (motion table flag 0x2): the MSC ends the attack at its
  wrap.
- **Scripts:** game.bin had none for these two (F.L.U.D.D. has no hitboxes): added. specialairlwhold's sound /
  expression JUMPed into speciallwhold: it now has its own copy of vanilla's (its JUMPs aimed at itself), so the air hold
  sounds / rumbles as before.
- **MSC** (`supamario.c`, end of the file): `fd_*`; `func_2265` (the main) takes the ground path, `func_2282` (the end)
  `fd_end`, `func_82` (every frame) `fd_tick`. `FD_*` from `supamario_fakedeath_build.msc_values()` (also in
  `supamario_kame_build.py`). `msc_fingerprint` vs Core Breaker v8: only func_82 / 2265 / 2267 / 2270 / 2282 differ, +11
  functions; globals 122 of 128 (7 new, declared last so no earlier global moves).
- `tools/supamario_fakedeath_xv2.py` (report, SE), `tools/supamario_fakedeath_build.py`, `tools/supamario_fakedeath_test.py`;
  `ModAnimNames.txt` names `speciallwstart` (not in AnimNames.txt).

### Tests
- `supamario_fakedeath_test.py OUT BASE`: 40 checks, 0 problems: the tap (wake at 33, Wait at 99), held to 60 / 150 (the
  loop, the wake the frame after the release), the cap (wake at 300), a release in the collapse, the intangible frames
  exactly (1-19 and the attack's 0-52, renewed 2 frames, the last 1; none while lying), the turn (behind only), off the
  ground -> Fall, a cut while lying (the ring killed, nothing after), his travel; five ground hits on one fighter (60, 36,
  21, 12, then 60 after 300 frames; kept where it was hit), another fighter's own count, an airborne fighter (launched,
  the game's hitstun, nothing written, not counted), a shield (-41, no hold), the air F.L.U.D.D. (its own path, the air
  motions on landing); the scripts (both hitboxes' fields, the ring's call, the sounds, specialairlwhold's copies, every
  other script the user's), the ring's emitters facing-safe and within the HDR cap, the motions' lengths.
- `supamario_corebreaker_test.py`: 0 problems; `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`: 0 problems;
  `msc_stack_check.py`: 0 of 2384.

**Not confirmed in game:** that a knockback-1 hit puts a grounded fighter in Damage (0x45) with HitstunMul's added
frames, and that writing its hitstun left from the MSC every frame (from its hitstop on) holds it for exactly that
long; that the slot search finds the victim on the frame the hit flag shows (in its hitstop, on the ground, near the
hitbox); GroundModule's ledge stop with the energy-3 speed; the intangibility renewal (2 frames a frame, 1 on the last);
the ring's look (its size, the rings facing the camera); the attack's end at the looping slot's wrap.

A flat ring is the same in both facings |
| 2 | "positioned fine but should move him a bit forward. It should also not clank." | +1 unit a frame over the attack's push frames 18-23: **+6.0 units**, the attack's forward travel 11.9 (v1 5.9; the ledge stop is unchanged). Both hitboxes **Clang 0, Rebound 0** |
| 3 | 6% on both | ground and air hitboxes 6% |
| 4 | 66 frames; the reduction only while the opponent is still in the previous Fake Death hit's hitstun | **66 frames** of hitstun; a hit landing while the fighter is still held in the previous Fake Death hit's hitstun (fd_tick still holding it, or its hold broken by this very hit's hitstop within `REHIT` = 12 frames) is a repeat: **66, 40, 23, 13** (x1, x0.6, x0.35, x0.2); otherwise the full 66 (no 300-frame window) |

From the release on, every collapse frame is intangible too, up to the attack (intangible from its first frame), so **released by frame 19 = intangible from frame 1 to the attack's frame 52 with no gap** (game frames 1-85 on a tap); released at 20-32, intangible 1-19 and from the release on. Held through frame 33 (lying): 1-19 only, as v1 |

Mario weight, no DI (`flight()`): **0%: knockback 170, ~183 units away (v1 ~46: x4.0), hitstun 68; 100%: 184, ~214 units, 73**; 150%: ~231. With an FD-size stage (side blast zones ~240 from the centre) it doesn't KO from mid-stage until ~170%, but near the ledge, sent off-stage, it does from 0% |

**Note on rule 4:** with the current timing one Supamario can't land a second Fake Death while the first one's 66
frames are running: he acts 46 frames after his hit and his next Fake Death's hitbox is 52 frames later, while the
fighter has ~20 left. So the reduction only shows if something else keeps the fighter in that hitstun (the test makes
the case by keeping the hold running). Repeats are counted per Supamario (his New_Vars).

## Core Breaker v9 (`supamario_corebreaker_v9.zip`): one catch, cleared at the hover, downed fighters

Install over Fake Death v2, exe B (exe_v46k, no exe change). Built with `tools/supamario_fakedeath_build.py` (the God
Fist, Core Breaker, Fake Death), halos 0.16.

### 1. Invulnerable in the hover and the ending (user: "It becomes invulnerable during the hover and ending if the leap isn't cancelled into the hover/dive")
**Cause:** v3-v8's game script checked the hover's detection box on frames 31-33 with `Hit_Check(When=0)`, "since the
start", and nothing cleared that flag before the hover.
- After a rise that plays out to Fall, the player can attack (an aerial). Its hit left his attack's flag set.
- The second up special's check took that for a catch: `Body_State_Set` invincible, cb 3, and `Target_Slot_Get(2)`
  (the last fighter hit: the aerial's victim, wherever it was).
- The MSC then held that fighter, renewing his invincibility every frame through the hold and the crash / explosion.
- Cancelled into the hover, the rise leaves no window to hit anything between, so it didn't happen there.
- Reproduced offline on the v8 build: an earlier hit makes the second up special catch a fighter 150 away and write
  invincibility from frame 62 on.

**Fix:**
- `cb_hover_start` clears the flag (`AttackModule(0x2724)`) at the press, before the box comes out at frame 30. The
  game script's `Hit_Reset` also runs at 30, before the box.
- **One catch:** the game script has no catch check any more (no `Hit_Check`, `Target_Slot_Get` or
  `Body_State_Set`), only the box. The hover's catch is the MSC's `cb_try_catch` (the dive's v4-v8 function: the hit
  flag, target 2, its slot or the nearest fighter), on the box's frames and up to 3 after (its hit can reach the flag a
  frame late). Caught: the hold as before.
- **Invincibility only on a catch with a victim:** the first write is in `cb_catch` after its slot check, and the
  per-frame renewal also needs that catch (`cb_inv`).

### 2. Downed fighters (user: "Core Breaker should be able to hit downed opponents as specified previously")
- The dive's catch box and body box are now Ground/Air 3: they hit fighters lying down (v3-v8: 0x80000003, bit 31
  set, so they missed them).
- `cb_try_catch` has no down-status refusal on any path; shields, a shield break and invincible / intangible
  fighters are still refused.
- A downed victim goes into the hold as the box's always did: out of the down status into Damage (0x46), and a God
  Fist victim held down is taken over (gf_tick stops holding it).
- The light homing now also steers toward fighters lying down.
- The crash, the explosion and the burn hits were already Ground/Air 3 (they hit fighters lying down).

### Tests (v9 build)
- rise -> Fall -> up special, with and without a hit from before the hover and a fighter far away: the hover, the
  dive, the landing; no catch, no invincibility write;
- the dive catches DownWait, DownBound and a God Fist victim held down (into Damage, the hold on the ground, the
  crash; gf_tick lets go);
- an invincible fighter: refused, the dive goes on;
- the game script has no `Hit_Check` / `Target_Slot_Get` / `Body_State_Set`, has `Hit_Reset` before the hover's box,
  and every box is Ground/Air 3;
- the point-blank catches (standing, downed, a God Fist victim) through the MSC as before.

## Fake Death v3 (`supamario_fakedeath_v3.zip`): reach, 40% less knockback, a wider ring

Install over Core Breaker v9, exe B (`exe_v46k`, no exe change). The user, with a v2 clip
(`MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261115032700479`: the attack whiffs on Donkey Kong) and a screenshot.

1. **Reach** ("make Fake Death able to hit slightly further from the distance it whiffed at"). The clip, frame by frame
   (60 fps): the kip-up's handspring (the hit frames, wake 20-23) at 1.60-1.63 s, standing at ~1.67 s with Donkey
   Kong's front arm ~5.6 units ahead of his TransN (~17.5 px a unit from his standing height, ~12 units). His travel
   from wake 23 to standing is ~3.8 units, so on the last active frame Donkey Kong's front was ~9.4 ahead; the hitbox
   reached 7.56 (3.78 ahead + 3.78). Both hitboxes now **5.5 ahead, size 5.5: reach 11.0** (+3.4), 2.52 up as before.
   The ground hitbox now also reaches a little behind his TransN (5.5 - 5.5 = 0).
2. **Knockback -40%** ("please reduce its knockback by 40%"): the air hit's **BKB 165 -> 99, KBG 25 -> 15**, so its
   knockback is exactly 0.6x v2's at every percent. Mario weight, no DI: **0%: 102 (~66 units away, hitstun 40); 100%:
   111 (~78 units, 44)** (v2: 170 / ~183, 184 / ~214). The clip shows no ground hit (a whiff), and the ground hit has no
   knockback travel (knockback 1, held in place): unchanged.
3. **The ring** ("Fake death's ring is wrapped too closely to Supamario"): the v2 screenshot puts the main ring's edge
   ~0.6 of his lying length from its centre (~6.6 units; his half-length ~5.4). **`FD_FX_SCALE` 2.6 -> 4.2** (x1.6):
   ~9.9 units, clear space round him; still flat (X 90), centred on his body, the heights unchanged.
4. The hitstun-reduction rule stays (the user).

Tests: `supamario_fakedeath_test.py` (new: a fighter 9.4 ahead of him on the last active frame is hit now, beyond v2's
reach; the air knockback 0.6x v2's at 0 / 50 / 100 / 150%; the ring's radius at least 1.5x his lying half-length + 1),
`supamario_corebreaker_test.py`, `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`, `msc_stack_check.py`: all 0 problems (Fake
Death 50 checks; 0 of 2384 scripts, globals 124 of 128).

## Core Breaker v10 (`supamario_corebreaker_v10.zip`): the invincibility hunt (diag), the dive's shieldstun

Install over Fake Death v3, exe B (exe_v46k). The user: "The invulnerability bug is still present" (clip
`MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261115033819819`, 1920x1080, 6.5 s), and "setting shieldstun frames seems to not work".

### 1. The clip
- **A (0-2.6 s):** a rise from the stage, cancelled into the hover in place, the dive, a miss landing. In the landing
  and after it (30 fps frames) his colours stay normal: no blink.
- **B (2.8-6.5 s):** a rise from the stage played out to Fall (off camera above), then up special again in the air:
  the hover, the dive, a miss landing. From the landing (about 5.0 s) he blinks white on alternate frames for about
  33 frames, through the landing lag into Wait: Smash's invincible blink.
  - At B's rise start (2.95 s) there's one pale frame.
  - In the hover the orb covers him. The user's "during the hover" can't be seen in this clip.
- Nothing hits anyone in the clip (DK stays at 26%). There's no catch, so no Core Breaker invincibility write can run.

### 2. Everything that can make him invincible / intangible (current build)
- **MSC, Core Breaker:** the catch's first write (`cb_catch`, after its slot check), the hold's renewal (needs that
  catch: `cb_inv`), the status end's 1-frame write (needs `cb_inv`). None on a miss.
  - The model runs B's exact path (ground rise -> Fall -> air up special -> hover -> dive -> miss landing, the per-frame
    hook on) and A's.
  - Neither makes a HitModule call or a body-state write; the only DamageModule calls are the armor's (on at the
    dive, off at the landing).
- **MSC, Fake Death:** `fd_intangible`, only inside its own status (`fd_on`).
- **MSC, God Fist (`gf_tick`):** no body-state writes.
- **Vanilla MSC:** the timed / status body-state setters run only in vanilla statuses Core Breaker never enters.
  - `HitModule(1)` in `func_243` (rebirth), `func_1081`, `func_1140`; `HitModule(0 / 2 / 0x2710)` in others.
  - The up special status (0x154) has only Core Breaker's three functions (`func_2286` / `2287` / `2289`).
- **ACMD (every script in game / effect / sound / expression):**
  - `Body_State_Set`: only in attackdash (the God Fist);
  - bone intangibility: only in attackhi4;
  - `Body_Collision`: only in final;
  - the up special's four scripts have none (v9 removed the last).
- **Engine:** the setters' 22 direct callers in the executable (`0x0279EB90` .. `0x031CBC5C`).

Nothing in the mod's code explains B's blink, so the diag build is there to find it.

### 3. The diag build (`diag/` in the package; exe_v46k + two diagnostic caves)
- `tools/diag_hitstate_cave.py` (new, temporary): HitModule's three body-state setters each print a line on every
  call:
  - `0x02C42F00`: the status's body state (op 0; 0 isn't printed);
  - `0x02C43188`: invincible frames (op 1);
  - `0x02C43108`: intangible frames (op 2).

  The line: `S4HS op= v= a= lr= lr2= m= n=`, with the value, the second argument, the setter's caller, that caller's
  caller and the module. Unicorn test `tools/diag_hitstate_test.py`: every setter prints once, comes back to its
  second word with every register kept; op 0 with 0 doesn't print.
- `tools/diag_godfist_cave.py` (as the God Fist's): a write to `0x0F0003F0 + k` prints `S4GF k= v= c= n=`.
- **The diag MSC** (`supamario_godfist_build.CB_DIAG`: supamario.c's `//CBD` lines):

| k | When | v |
|---|---|---|
| 11 | every frame after the dive's shield hit, until 10 frames after its stun | the shielder's status x 100 + the frames left kept (+ 10000 in its hitstop) |
| 12 | each up special start | `CB_USED` + 10 x the situation (0 ground, 2 air) |
| 13 | every frame he is invincible or intangible, any status | his status x 100 + intangible state x 10 + invincible |
| 14 | each Core Breaker phase change; 99 at the status end | phase + 10 x the motion frame |
| 15 | each Core Breaker invincibility write | 1 catch, 2 renewal, 3 end |

How to read the user's log for B:
- the k 13 lines give the frames and the status he's invincible in;
- the S4HS lines just before them give which setter, with what, from where (`lr` / `lr2`);
- k 14 places it in the move.

If the caller is engine code, its address names it.

### 4. The dive's shieldstun (user: "setting shieldstun frames seems to not work")
**How the game does it** (Supamario's own MSC, the common statuses):
- GuardDamage (0x1E) init (key 3, `func_460`): count `0x11000009` = INT(the hit's damage `0x100000b` x 0.58
  (`0x020000cc`, x the projectile / power-shield multipliers) + 4 (`0x020000cd`)).
- Its per-frame substatus (`func_415`, `global16`) takes 1 off while it isn't frozen.
- Its loop (`func_414`) goes back to Guard when it reaches 0 (and term `0x1e00002a`).

**Why v8's one write could be lost:** it was a single write of the count (PUSH 1016: the position variable sets that int
for another fighter). If it landed before GuardDamage's own init ran (its status start can follow the hit by a frame),
the init replaced it with the hit's own count (INT(3 x 0.58 + 4) = 5).

**Fix (`cb_shield_tick`, every frame, every status, from the per-frame hook):**
- While the shielder is in GuardDamage, its count is written as the frames left, starting at 16.
- One frame comes off only on frames it isn't in hitstop (+3 HITSTOP).
- It waits up to 10 frames for GuardDamage to start, and stops if the shielder leaves it.
- So the count is 16 when its freeze ends and goes 16, 15 .. 1: 16 frames whatever order the init and the write come
  in.
- This is a general method (keep the count GuardDamage itself counts down, every frame); a general shieldstun command
  could do the same.

**Sephiroth:** forward smash's push (fighters/edge, `edge_s4_push`) also writes PUSH 1000 + 3 every frame while the
shielder slides. A per-frame write like this one, so it should hold too; the user wasn't sure it did. The diag's k 11
lines (the shielder's status each frame to 10 frames after the stun) show the frame GuardDamage really ends: they
confirm both.

**Test:**
- the dive's shield hit: the count kept at 1016 through the shielder's 6 hitstop frames, then 1016, 1015 .. 1001,
  one a frame;
- the shield's 2/3 as before.

### Tests (v10 build)
`supamario_corebreaker_test.py`, `supamario_fakedeath_test.py`, `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models`,
`msc_stack_check.py`; the diag cave tests on the diag executable (below the package).

## Core Breaker v11 (`supamario_corebreaker_v11.zip`): the up special's intangible window; the shieldstun read

Install over Core Breaker v10 and the buffer commands v1 (Fake Death v3 under them), exe B (exe_v46l). The user ran
v10's diag build (log
`babdf45b-log.txt`, 405 lines: 271 `S4GF`, 10 `S4HS`).

### 1. What the log says
- Every up special start has `S4HS op=0 v=2 a=0 lr=02E24F70 lr2=02BF2E30` (6 times): the status's body state set to
  2, intangible (not invincible; the k 13 value 34020 = status 0x154, intangible 2, invincible 0).
- **Rises** (k 12 v=0; 04:07:24.121, 32.387, 39.207, 58.153): k 13 on exactly 4 frames, then normal: frames 3-6.
- **A** (04:07:39.207, the rise cancelled into the hover in place: k 14 161 = phase 1 at motion frame 16, 602 the
  dive, 675 the landing, 99 the end): the same 4 frames, nothing after. No blink, as in the clip.
- **B** (04:07:33.252 and 59.018, the second up special in the air: k 12 21 = used 1, air; k 14 1 = the hover at
  frame 0): intangible from its first frame to its end, 100 frames (hover, dive, the 60-frame landing) and 106 frames.
  The clip's blink after the landing.
- The 4 `S4HS op=1 v=0` lines at the match start are the 4 fighters' invincible frames cleared (benign).

### 2. Who sets it (exe_v46k)
- `0x02BF2E30`: the object loop's return after its call of the fighter update (vtable `+0x1E4`, `0x02BF2E2C`).
- `0x02E24F70`: the return of `bl 0x02C42F00` (`li r4, 2`) at `0x02E24F6C`, in the fighter update `0x02E24270`'s tail
  (`0x02E24D1C`-`0x02E24F90`). It takes the current motion kind (MotionModule `[fighter+0x74AC]` `+0x38`: 0x221
  SpecialHi / 0x225 SpecialAirHi) and its 6 bytes in his vl file's group 1 (`[[mgr+0x25B0+4*kind]+0xC] + kind*6`;
  the vl is `fighter_param_vl_supamario.bin`, Mario's copy): byte 3 = 3 the start, byte 4 = 7 the end (Mario's Super
  Jump Punch: intangible 3-6). Each frame: now >= start > the frame before -> state 2; **else** now >= end > before
  -> state 0.
- **B:** `cb_init` -> `cb_goto(HOVER_FROM)` sets the rate to 30, so the motion goes 0 -> 30 in one frame: the start
  crossing sets 2, and the end check (an `else`) is skipped that frame; after it no crossing. 2 stays to the status
  end (the status change resets it).
- **A, the rise:** the motion plays 0, 1, 2 ... 7 at rate 1, so the end clears it at frame 7; A's hover begins at 16,
  past the window. The clip's "one pale frame at B's rise start" is these frames 3-6.
- docs/native-notes.md "The motion's intangible window".

### 3. Fix
- **The data:** the package has `param/fighter/fighter_param_vl_supamario.bin` = Mario's vl with SpecialHi /
  SpecialAirHi bytes 3 / 4 = 0 (4 bytes changed; byte 4 0 skips the window). `supamario_corebreaker_build.py vl
  MARIO_VL OUT_DIR` writes it (`vl_no_window`; it fails if the window isn't Mario's 3 / 7 or already 0 / 0).
- **The status (`cb_update`, every frame of 0x154):** `HitModule(0, 0)`. With an old vl file installed, the window
  can still set 2 on its crossing frame, but the next frame clears it: at most that one frame, once a status.
- The hold's invincibility is the INVINCIBLE frames (`HitModule(1)`, another field): unchanged.
- The rise loses Super Jump Punch's frames 3-6 intangibility (Core Breaker's own rise never asked for it).

### 4. The shieldstun (user: "The shieldstun didn't seem to work. Maybe self shieldstun and victim shieldstun are different?")
The k 11 lines (`status x 100 + frames left + 10000 x hitstop`), B at 04:07:59:
- 13016 x 4: the shielder in GuardDamage (0x1E), frozen (hitlag), our count at 16.
- 3016 .. 3001: GuardDamage, 16 frames, one a frame. **The 16 frames worked** (vanilla for this 3% hit: INT(3 x 0.58 +
  4) = 5).
- 2800 .. 2790: Guard (0x1C, shielding) with our count past 0 (0, -1 .. -10: the diag's 10 frames after). From Guard
  every out-of-shield option is immediate (jump, grab, up special, up smash); a shield drop takes 8 (`0x120000df`).

Frame by frame (k 14 / k 11 times, 60 fps; N = the dive's start, 59.533):
- N+4: the shield hit; both frozen about 4-5 frames (his motion goes 60 -> 66 in 11 frames).
- N+8 .. N+23: the 16 frames of shieldstun; N+24: the shielder can act.
- N+11: he lands (phase 5 at motion frame 66); his landing is 60 frames (`LAND_LAG`), the status ends at N+71.
- **On shield: -47** (he is free 47 frames after the shielder). With vanilla's 5 frames: -58. The 11 extra frames
  don't change what can punish him.

"Self" shieldstun: Smash 4 has none for the attacker; his only lag on a shield hit is his hitlag (the box's
`Hitlag` 1.0, the same frames as the shielder's) and then his own move: here the 60-frame miss landing.

To make it feel like they want (not built; for the user to choose):
- **A shorter recovery on a shield hit:** e.g. the dive's shield hit goes to a bounce (FallSpecial) or a short landing
  (15 frames: about -2 with the 16 frames of stun).
- **Or more shieldstun:** about 63 frames for 0 with the 60-frame landing.

  without the fix (A: 4 frames, B: the whole status); with Mario's 3 / 7 and the fix: at most the crossing frame,
  once a status; with the package's vl: normal on every frame of the rise, A, B, a grounded catch, a catch in the

  `Buffer_Clear`), shipped with the MSC and the vl file.
- The diag executable is exe_v46l + `diag_godfist_cave.py` + `diag_hitstate_cave.py` (the same words as v10's on

  `diag_hitstate_test.py` / `diag_godfist_test.py` on it: 0 failed.

special (F.L.U.D.D. leaves the air; the grounded down special stays Fake Death). Files given: the skill's BAC / BCM /
BAS / BDM / EAN (`_C`, `_F`, `_BDK`, `_CL3`, `_SLG`), its EEPK / pbind / ptcl, and its SE bank `CAR_BTL_S164_HMR_SE.acb`
(cues 0 Charge, 1 Furi; the older ACB layout, as Fake Death's). Install over Core Breaker v11 / buffer commands v1 /
Fake Death v3, exe `exe_v46u_threads` (no exe change: Extra hitboxes, New_Var, Target 12's variables are all in it).

  (OnAttackHit, no button, ReceiverLinkID Supers) plays **BAC 3**, reached through BAC 0 / 2's BcmCallback "Supers"
  on the hit frames. BAC 1 (100 damage, a 0.25 TimeScale) is in the file but no BCM entry plays it.
- **BAC 0 (uncharged)**, skill EAN #0 VGT_SPA_HMR_C (45 frames, speed 1: the animation frame is the BAC frame):
  TimeScale **0-12 x0.5, 22-26 x0.3, 26-37 x0.5** (so 44 BAC frames = 76.3 real frames); Tracking 0-10 (no backwards
  flag); Movement 2-10 (0.075 up, 0.55 forward a frame, drag 0.04: a lunge); **ChargeControl 6-8, ChargeTime 30**;
  skill effect 0 (Hmr_Crg, the left fist's glow) 5-8, effect 1 (Hmr_Fire, rings round the pelvis) at 12; sounds CMN
  cue 42 swing_42 at 1, skill cue 0 Charge at 6 (flags 0x28: it stops with its entry), cue 1 Furi at 9, voice 49 at 0
  (left out: no voices); **hitbox 16-20**, skill BDM #0, **60 damage** (6 blocked), b_C_Base + 0.75 m ahead, 1.0 m;
  BcmCallback Supers 16-20, Movement / Guard / FlyingAndStepDash **37-44**.
- **BAC 2 (charged):** the same but TimeScale 22-26 **x0.23**, Movement 0.65 forward, **hitbox 15-19**, BDM #1,
  **145 damage**, effect 3 (Hmr_Fire_L x1.2 + Hmr_Crg02 x1.2 on the fist) at 12, effect 0 off at 13.
- **BAC 3 (on hit):** BAC 0's items without the hitboxes / effects / sounds, TimeScale **0-12 x0.5, 32-44 x0.5 only**,
  BcmCallback **Combos 26-44**: XV2's on-hit cancel window. Its items line up with BAC 0's frames (the face / eye
  items at 0, 1, 7, 26 as BAC 0's), so it is read as played on from the hit on the same clock: on a hit BAC 22-32 is
  no longer slowed and the window opens at **BAC 26, 10 real frames after BAC 16** (the hit's hitlag aside).

### How (files)
- `tools/supamario_sledgehammer_xv2.py` (report, SE), `tools/supamario_sledgehammer_anim.py`,
  `tools/supamario_sledgehammer_build.py` (runs Fake Death's build, then the motions, effects, scripts, sounds, MSC),
  `tools/supamario_sledgehammer_test.py`. `SH_*` from `msc_values()` (also in `supamario_kame_build.py`).
- **MSC** (`supamario.c`, end of the file): `sh_*`; `func_2265` (the main) takes it in the air, `func_2282` (the end)
  `sh_end`. **No new globals** (127 of 128): New_Var slots **0x3E `sh`** (the phase), **0x3F `sh_chg`** (the charge's
  frames; the game / effect / expression scripts read it), **0x2F `sh_vic`** (the hit fighter's slot) and **VAR_0 /
  VAR_1** (the followed Y, the swing's last frame; Supamario used no VAR_n). New_Var slots are now all taken
  (0x00-0x3F); VAR_2-31 are free. The victim: Target 12's `ATKHIT_OFFSET_X / _Y` matched against the slots (exe_v46k),
  else the nearest fighter in hitstop near the hitbox. `msc_fingerprint` vs Core Breaker v11: func_2265 / func_2282 and
  12 new functions.
- **Scripts:** specialairlwstart / specialairlwhold replaced in all four files (F.L.U.D.D.'s had no hitboxes). Both
  names are vanilla (AnimNames.txt), no new slot: ModAnimNames.txt unchanged.
- **soundlabeltable:** the package's is byleth_userdata_v2's (the user's installed one: rows 27 / 78 Byleth's) with
  row 3249 grown by the three labels (the build's own is based on the God Fist's table).

## Sledgehammer v2 (`supamario_sledgehammer_v2.zip`): the user's v1 test (screenshot 24) and requests

Install over Sledgehammer v1, exe `exe_v46u_threads` (no exe change). Changed: the ptcl, game.bin, effect.bin, the MSC.

| # | User | v2 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | "The charge effects don't follow Supamario" | Every emitter of Hmr_Crg / Hmr_Crg02 / Hmr_Fire / Hmr_Fire_L (and their halos) gets ptcl follow type 0 (+0x310: the particles on the emitter's live matrix; v1's templates gave the charge glow 1, none). The hit effect (world-placed, `EffectModule(3)`) stays where it hit, as XV2's |
| 2 | "The release arc is drawn flat" (a thin yellow streak along his body) | Hmr_Fire's are open ShapeDraw arcs (~110-130 degrees) spinning 15 a frame. v1 laid them flat (X 90), so the side camera saw them edge-on. Now they are in bone 0's YZ plane (`arc_turn`: Y -90, X along his facing), facing the camera, and spin forward and down with the swing (their spin negated; Hitbox Viewer: frames 13 / 15 / 17 sweep front -> below). Their strip is **x3** (`xv2_to_ptcl.RING_WIDTH_MUL`, default 1: XV2's 0.15 m over a 1.15 m radius). No halo on them (a soft full disc behind him) |
| 3 | Effects and hitbox x1.2 | **Hitbox size 6.05** (5.04; centre unchanged, 3.78 ahead at his feet, the fists 2.3 from it). **Effects x2.1** (1.75): Hmr_Fire_L / Crg02 2.52, Btl_HitLOD 3.15, Btl_HitS 1.68, sweat 2.1 |
| 4 | Uncharged "slightly more base knockback and slightly more growth", 8% | **8%, BKB 50, KBG 20** (+6 hitstun, 270, hitlag 10): knockback **54.7 / 61.7 / 68.7** at 0 / 50 / 100% (v1 42.0 / 44.5 / 46.9), hitstun 27 / 30 / 33. The follow keeps within one frame's fall of the faster victim (it falls 26.1 in the test, the gap 6.0-7.6); the on-hit window is still 10 frames after the hitlag |
| 5 | Charged 20%, the same launch | **20%, BKB 23, KBG 61** (hitlag 20): **52.8 / 99.7 / 146.7** (v1 53.3 / 99.8 / 146.4) |
| 6 | Fast fall in the move | `sh_fastfall` runs the game's own check (`func_553`: a stick flick down while falling sets 0x21000001) every frame, the charge too. It only counts a flick made on the move's 2nd frame or later (its age `global47` under the move's frames, VAR_2), so the down special's own input never fast-falls. `sh_init` ends a fast fall carried in (as `func_81` does for specials). `func_81` keeps a fast fall started in Sledgehammer. **During the uncharged follow it is ignored** (the follow clears the flag and doesn't run the check), and allowed again once the follow ends |
| 7 | "standardize Sledgehammer's landing lag to 10 frames", light landing animation | Before the free frame: LandingFallSpecial **10 frames** (work float 0). `func_586` plays **LandingLight (0x3A, 16 frames) at rate 1.6** over them when it is entered from 0x155 (only Sledgehammer lands from it). After the free frame: a normal landing (0x16) |
| 8 | Grounded opponents | `sh_hit` notes a victim on the ground (its situation in the hitlag); `sh_tick` (func_82) waits for its hitstop to end, then: **uncharged** writes **DownBound (0x4D)**, the missed tech's floor bounce (no tech), and lets go. **Charged** writes DownBound, turns its **input off**, holds it **40 frames** (back to DownWait 0x50 if it leaves 0x4D-0x53), lets go when it is hit (0x45-0x4C, DownDamage 0x57) or at 40. Airborne victims: the spike, unchanged |
| 9 | Shield damage by the charge | `sh_shield` (Core Breaker's cb_dive_shield way): **uncharged 24%** of the max shield (50), **8 frames** (Smash's own for 8%: INT(8 x 0.58 + 4)); **charged 80%, 30 frames**. Shieldstun held by `cb_shield_tick` (its globals reused; the dive and Sledgehammer never overlap); a shield left below the take breaks (0x59). XV2 has two charge levels (0 or 30 frames), so these are the only two values. A level between them could come from the frames held (0-29), but there isn't one |

State: VAR_2-5 (the move's frames, the shield hit handled, the grounded victim's slot and frames); New_Vars unchanged;

## Sledgehammer v3 (`supamario_sledgehammer_v3.zip`): the glow, the charged knockdown, the neutral getup

Install over Sledgehammer v2, exe `exe_v46u_threads` (no exe change). Changed: the ptcl, game.bin, the MSC.

### 1. The glow (user: "Sledgehammer's glow is extremely bright", screenshot 25: a white-yellow dome over him)
**Cause: the swing arcs** (Hmr_Fire / Hmr_Fire_L's ShapeDraw arcs, called at swing frame 12), not the charge glow, the
fist flash or the hit burst. v1 drew them flat, edge-on to the side camera (a thin streak). v2 stood them up facing
the camera, made their strip x3, the effects x1.2, and made them follow him, so the 3 arcs each emitter lays over 3
frames stack on him; all still at the HDR gain meant for small glowing points (`xv2_smash_fx.hdr_emitter`: XV2's
glare tint, colour scale x2, capped at 2.0). Additive yellow at 2.0 over a large face-on area is what Smash 4 blooms
into the dome.

Measured with `tools/supamario_fx_glow.py` (fx_render's Smash 4 side, the converted emitters through their own
programs, black background; square units drawn over 1.0, Smash 4's bloom, and over 0.5; energy = the summed colour):

| | brightest frame: peak / over 1.0 / over 0.5 / energy |
|---|---|
| v2 uncharged swing | 1.88 / **73.0** / 165.9 / 399 |
| v2 charged swing | 1.94 / **144.4** / 296.2 / 738 |
| v2 charge (fist glow) | 2.41 / 2.6 / 2.9 / 12 |
| v2 hit burst | 1.83 / 0.1 / 0.4 / 87 (at most 3.1 over 1.0 in a frame) |
| Super God Fist (attackdash) | 2.00 / 2.0 / 33.3 / 95 (at most 6.4 over 1.0) |
| Core Breaker's charge (specialhi < 55) | 1.67 / 0.1 / 0.7 / 691 (a soft, wide aura; at most 62.1 over 1.0 in one frame, its burst) |
| **v3 uncharged swing** | 0.85 / **0.0** / 61.9 / 180 |
| **v3 charged swing** | 1.55 / **4.4** (the fist's flash; at most 7.2 in a frame) / 121.8 / 345 |

Fix (`supamario_sledgehammer_build.py` `ARC_GAIN` 0.45, `arc_dim`): the arcs' colour keys x0.45, so each arc draws
at most 0.9 (2.0 before) in XV2's yellow, and where two overlap they stay under 1.0: nothing from the arcs blooms. The
size, the x3 strip, the follow and the spin stay; the charge glow, the fist flash and the hit burst (already at the God
Fist's level) are unchanged. The charged swing's remaining 4.4 over 1.0 is the fist's flash (Crg02), as the God Fist's
fist. The test checks every arc's drawn peak <= 2.0 x ARC_GAIN. Offline renders (black background, no bloom, so v2's
look clipped rather than glowing): the package's preview/glow_v2_v3.png. If the arcs read too dim in game, ARC_GAIN 0.5
still keeps two overlapping arcs under 1.0 (0.94).

### 2. The charged hit on the ground (user: "The grounded charged hit bounces just like the uncharged hit rather than forcing the opponent down into the laying-down hurt state")
**Cause:** v2 hit grounded fighters with the spike (20%, 270, knockback 52.8 / 99.7 / 146.7 at 0 / 50 / 100%). From
80 up Smash's ground spike bounces the fighter off the floor (DamageFly); v2 then wrote DownBound after the hitstop
(the bounce the user saw) and its hold let go as soon as it saw a damage status (0x45-0x4C), the launch's own. On the
test's model of the game v2's MSC does exactly that (status 0x47, let go, Fall at the hold's end).

**v3, the Super God Fist v17's put-down** (`gf_tick` phase 5):
- The charged swing is two hitboxes on the same frames, place and size, Part 0 (one hit a target): **ID 0 the spike,
  aerial only (Ground/Air 2)**, unchanged; **ID 1 grounded only (Ground/Air 1): 20%, angle 0, BKB 1, KBG 0, no SDI**,
  the same hitlag (20) and SFX: Fake Death's grounded hit, which has never launched anyone in game. A fighter on the
  ground is no longer launched at any percent.
- `sh_down_start`, on the frame the MSC sees the hit (in the hitlag): its controller off (`INPUT_OFF`, the God Fist's
  Input_Set 21), its buffer emptied (`BUFFER_CLEAR`), its place noted (VAR_14 / 15), then `sh_put`: at that place, on
  the ground (SITUATION 0, stage collision 2), **DownWait (0x50)** (its own DownWaitU / D) unless it is already lying.
  No DownBound, no bounce: it lies down at once.
- `sh_tick` (every frame): through its hitstop and **40 frames** after it, put down again if it is out of the down
  statuses (0x4D-0x53) or in the air (its down timer running out, anything that moved it); then its controller back
  and its buffer emptied. Let go early when it is hit again (frozen again after its hitstop ended, or DownDamage 0x57)
  or gone. DownWait's loop (`func_963`) has no tech; with the controller off a CPU can't get up (the God Fist in game).
- A new charged hold lets go of an earlier one first (v2 left that one's controller off).

### 3. The neutral getup (user: uncharged Sledgehammer on a fighter in its neutral getup "hits the same way it would in the air"; then: the neutral getup only)
The loop: bounce -> jab lock -> getup -> Sledgehammer -> bounce. An **uncharged** hit on a fighter in **DownStand (0x54,
the neutral getup)** gets no bounce: nothing is written, the hit is the spike's own, as on an airborne fighter (its knockback,
54.7-68.7 at 0-100%, is under the 80 from which Smash's ground spike bounces a fighter: not checked in game). Every other state keeps v2's bounce
(the user): standing, lying (DownWait / after a bounce), the roll getup (DownStandFb 0x55), the getup attack
(DownStandAttack 0x56). The charged hit puts a fighter in DownStand down like any other (the rule is the uncharged
hit's).

Its status at the hit: `sh_rec` (every frame) keeps each slot's status from the last frame it wasn't frozen in a
hitstop (VAR_6-13); `sh_getup` looks at that and at its status now. On the hit's frame the MSC may already see the
hit's damage status (the model does: 0x45), so the status before the freeze is what tells.

### State, tests
VAR_6-13 (statuses), VAR_14 / 15 (the held victim's place); VAR_4 / 5 as v2 (VAR_5 -1 on the put-down's frame). New
functions `sh_rec`, `sh_getup`, `sh_let_go`, `sh_put`, `sh_down_start`; `sh_hit` / `sh_tick` changed. No globals.

`supamario_sledgehammer_test.py`: 91 ok, 0 problems. New model of a fighter on the ground (`GVictim`: its
damage status from the hit, Smash's ground spike bounce from 80, a launch speed kept through any status change, status
writes taking effect the next frame, DownWait -> Fall in the air, its down timer, a CPU pressing to get up every
frame). Checks: the charged hit at 0 / 100 / 150% (with and without the CPU) lying on its floor every frame from the
hit to the end of the 40 frames, never in the air, the controller off from the hit to 40 frames after the hitstop and
nothing else, getting up on the release frame; its down timer ending in the hold (put back down); hit again in the
hold (let go); a charged hit on DownStand (put down); the uncharged hit on DownStand (nothing written) and on Wait /

47 ok, `supamario_corebreaker_test.py` 104 ok, `supamario_godfist_test.py --all-models` 546 ok: 0 problems;
`msc_stack_check.py` 0 of 2405 (globals 127 of 128). The 8 scripts round-trip through Sm4shCommand v1.7.2 (decompile, compile, the same words); compared with v2's build only the six arc emitters in the ptcl, game.bin's specialairlwhold and the MSC differ.

**Not confirmed in game:** that DownWait asked in the hitlag of the grounded box's hit plays straight away (the God

### 4. Winning clanks (user: "make it so Sledgehammer wins any clanks")
Smash 4's clank, read from the exe (docs/native-notes.md "Clanks"): a hitbox with Clang 0 meets no other hitbox;
each side of a clank resolves its own record: it wins when its damage - battle_object param 55 (9.0) >= the other's
(x the other's multipliers) and goes on untouched; else it loses (Rebound 1: a rebound whose length and pushback come
from the larger damage; Rebound 0: its attack cancelled). One losing record loses.
- The real hitboxes (charged IDs 0 / 1, uncharged ID 0) are **Clang 0**: they never meet a hitbox, never lose.
- **ID 2, a clank-only box** on the same place, size and frames (both swings): **Clang 1, `CLANK_DMG` 50**, HitBits 0
  (no fighter / item / enemy / floor category: it hits no one), Shieldable / Reflectable / Absorbable 0, no knockback,
  Flinchless, NoGFX, no SFX, **Rebound 0**, Hitlag x`CLANK_HITLAG` 0.3 (the loser's clank hitlag comes from the larger
  damage x the larger one's multiplier: ~7 frames). It beats any attack or projectile up to 41% (after its owner's
  multipliers).
- The MSC's hit checks (AttackModule kinds 2 / 4) never see it (it hits no body or shield); the air spike / ground box
  pair is unchanged.
- **Not traced:** where Smash 4 decides that an aerial and a grounded attack don't clank (nothing in the
  attack-vs-attack pass tests the situation): where no clank happens, both hit as before. A losing attack with Rebound 1
  rebounds longer than in a normal clank (from 50 + 3 instead of its own damage + 3).

### 5. The fists out of his head (user, clip `MedalTVCemuWiiUEmulator20261116121746352`: "The charge glow is almost invisible ... Supamario's hands are positioned in his head rather than above his head")
The clip (2.2 s): the charge pose with his hands at his cap. Measured (`anim.fist_clearance`: each fist's centre,
HandN + 1.0 along the forearm, its side-view distance to his head's mesh points, model.nud's meshes under HeadN): v3
0.0-0.6 from the wind-up through the swing, 2.8-3.2 under the cap's top. **His arms can't do it**: 3.47 from shoulder
to wrist, his shoulders 10.1 up in the pose, his cap's top 16.3 (the head 6.7 tall); a search over head tilts, shoulder
raises and IK targets with his own arm lengths found no fist centre higher than his eyes 1.4 clear of the head.
`tools/supamario_sledgehammer_anim.py` (`overhead`, blended in over Start f2-6, out over Hold f11-13):

- the head tilted back **20** more (`HEAD_TILT`: he looks up at his fists), the shoulders raised **40** (`SHOULDER_RAISE`);
- both fists put by two-bone IK (`_ik`: ShoulderJ / ArmJ turned, the elbow kept in its plane, the hand's turn from the
  forearm) at HeadN + **(1.5 forward, 4.5 up)** (`FIST_AT`), clasped (x +-0.6);
- `push_clear` (Start f5-12, Hold f0-16): a fist nearer the head than 1.2 is moved straight away from the nearest head
  point by IK: the swing's fast first frames (Hold 12-14) go round in front of his face instead of through it.
- The hit frames (Hold 15-19: the hitbox is on bone 0, unchanged) and everything after are XV2's as before.

Result: the charge pose's fists 1.75 / 1.82 clear, their centres 0.59 / 0.67 above the cap's top; at least 1.0 clear
from Start f6 through Hold f16. Before / after: `preview/sledgehammer_pose_v3_v4.png` (also built by
`anim.pose_sheet`). The charge glow (on LHandN) and the charged flash follow the fists out of the head. Not done: Start

## Super God Fist v18 (in `supamario_sledgehammer_v4.zip`): tracking to 75 degrees, range 55

User: "allow Super God Fist to track up to 75 degrees above or below Supamario", "increase Super God Fist's tracking
and speed-up range by 5 units". MSC only (`gf_pick`, `gf_track`; `supamario_godfist_build.py` `TRACK_ANGLE`,
`HOMING_RANGE`).
- **75 degrees:** `gf_pick` takes an opponent in front within 75 degrees of level (`dy^2 <= GF_TRACK_TAN2 dx^2`,
  tan^2 75 = 13.93; v12-v17 60: `dy^2 <= 3 dx^2`). One steeper **isn't tracked**, as at 60 before (no clamp to the
  limit). The same pick decides the 2x startup.
- **Range 55** (v15-v17 50): `HOMING_RANGE`, still one constant for the tracking and the 2x startup (`GF_TRACK_D2`
  3025).
- **Kept:** the burst's first frames (motion 9-11), the k cap 1.5, down at most `TRACK_DOWN` 3 a frame in the air, and
  the v12 rule (older than this request): **started on the ground over the stage it never aims down** at a lower
  opponent; down-tracking happens only past the edge / in the air. So "75 below" works off the stage.
- **New guard:** at 75 degrees the aim's climb a frame (k x the burst's way x tan) is up to 3.7 x the way; a steep near
  opponent was overshot. The vertical step is now never past the opponent's own height (`vy` at most its `dy`).
- Tests (`msc_v18_tests`; the earlier cases moved to the new limits): from the ground over the stage, 20 away, at 0 /
  45 / 74.9 / 85 degrees above and below: tracked up to 74.9 (he rises above, never aims down below: the v12 rule),
  85 not; past an edge in the air, 74.9 below aimed down (at most 3 a frame), 85 not; `gf_pick` at 54.9 / 55.1 (picked /
  not) and 74.9 / 75.1 up and down (picked / not); the 2x startup at 54.9 / 55.1, 74 / 80 degrees up; rising at 74
  degrees never above the opponent. `--all-models` 566 ok.

## Sledgehammer v5 (in `vars_reset_v1.zip`, exe `exe_v46w_vars_line`): the lockout's release, real hits only, armor, 28 frames, v3's pose, the glow in front

Install over Sledgehammer v4 with the package's executable (exe_v46w_vars_line = exe_v46v_threads + tools/var_reset_cave.py
+ tools/line_test_cave.py: it replaces Byleth v9's exe_v46v_line / exe_v46w_vars_line). Changed: the MSC, main.pac
(the two Sledgehammer motions back to v1-v3's), effect.bin (the glow's offset), the ptcl (the arcs' gain).

### 1. The 40-frame lockout bug (user: "The resetting on KO should be helpful to fix the 40-frame control lockout bug")
**Cause:** the charged grounded hold turns the victim's controller off (its INPUT_OFF, position_vars' per-slot input
state) and only Supamario's `sh_tick` turns it back on, from his own variables (VAR_4 / VAR_5 / VAR_17). If he is KO'd
in the hold, his respawn's set-up clears them: the victim's controller stayed off until its own KO (position_vars
clears a fighter's inputs in Dead / Rebirth / Entry / Win / Lose). Also, a victim KO'd in the hold could be put down
again by his tick at the old place (it left the down statuses: `sh_put`).
**Fix (exe_v46w's variable resets, docs/acmd-extra-commands.md "Variable resets"):** at the hold's start
(`sh_down_start`) `lock_resets(s, 9)` registers, through MSC's selection IDs (`sh_reset_item`, `0x0F0003CA`-`3CD`):
- on the **victim**, its own `SELF_INPUT_OFF` (`0x115`) back to 0 on its **KO** or **a hit on it** (mask 9): its
  controller comes back whoever hits it, and at its KO even before position_vars' own clear;
- on **him**, the victim's INPUT_OFF as he sees it (`0x115 + 0x20 x (slot + 2)`) back to 0 on **his KO** (mask 1):
  the exe writes it at his Dead status (or his set-up, whichever comes first), whatever his variables are then.
`sh_let_go` removes both. Every frame of the hold `sh_tick` lets go (and writes nothing more to the victim) when its
controller is already back (`SELF_INPUT_OFF` reads 0: a reset fired, or anything else turned it on) or the victim is
KO'd / respawning / entering / in the results (`sh_gone`: Dead `0xB1`, Rebirth `0xB2`, Entry .. Lose `0x14F`-`0x151`).
Release paths and what gives the controller back: his KO (his reset), the victim's KO (its reset; the game's own clear),
the victim hit by anyone (its reset), the match ending (Win / Lose: the game's clear; his tick lets go), the 28 frames
(his tick), on an older exe (resets ignored) the last three as v4.
**The Super God Fist's knockdown** (`gf_tick`) gets the same: while the victim is held (phase 1, the flight: its KO
and his KO only, since the God Fist's own hits are still to land; phase 2 on: its KO or a hit on it too), released when
its controller is back. **Core Breaker's hold** (`cb_hold`): his KO lets the held victim go (its burn hits would fire a
hit reset, so the victim's own isn't set).

### 2. No bounce / hold on hits that don't really land (user: invincible, intangible, shield, counter; armored)
`sh_hit` starts the grounded bounce (uncharged) or the hold (charged) only when `sh_struck(s)`: the victim's body state
at the hit is 0 (`INTANGIBLE` 1 invincible, 2-4 intangible: no), it isn't shielding (GuardOn .. GuardDamage, a shield
break, now or before the hit's freeze), and it **took damage** (its DAMAGE now over what `sh_rec` noted on its last
unfrozen frame: v5 keeps the damage with each slot's status, `status + 1024 x round(damage x 4)` in VAR_6-13): a counter
or invincibility takes none. Then at the end of its hitstop `sh_tick` asks for a **reaction** (`sh_reacted`: Damage ..
DamageFall `0x45`-`0x4C`, DownDamage `0x57`): super / heavy armor (and anything else the hit left in its own status)
gets no bounce, and the charged hold lets go there.
### 3. The charged hit ignores armor (user: "Charged Sledgehammer should bypass armor though")
Smash 4 has no hitbox flag that ignores armor (reference/spreadsheets: none; the armor check `0x02C24C88` reads the
victim's DamageModule only), so `sh_unarmor` lifts it: on the charged hitbox's active frames (and the one before, while
it hasn't hit) the MSC, which runs after the game script made the hitbox and before the frame's collision, sets
`ARMOR = 0` (`DamageModule(0xB, 0)`) on every fighter whose body (feet to +10, 3.5 either side) the hitbox overlaps,
keeping its type and threshold in VAR_18-21 (`slot x 1e6 + type x 1e5 + threshold x 10`, up to 4 fighters). The hit
then takes effect as on an unarmored fighter: in the air the spike's own knockback, hitstun and DI; on the ground the
bounce and the hold. After the active frames (and at the status's end) `sh_rearmor` gives the armor back (threshold,
then type) to those it didn't hit (not frozen, not in a reaction / down status). Invincibility, intangibility,
shields and counters still stop it (section 2). The uncharged hit leaves armor alone.

### 4. 28 frames (user: "28 frames instead of 40")
`DOWN_HOLD` 28, still counted from the first frame it lies after the bounce. Sledgehammer's own: the God Fist's knockdown
is `GF_DOWN_FRAMES` (unchanged).

### 5. v3's pose, the charge glow in front of his head (user: undo v4's animation change; draw the glow closer to the camera)
- `supamario_sledgehammer_anim.py` `OVERHEAD = False`: XV2's pose as retargeted (no arm stretch, head tilt, shoulder
  raise, IK fists or push); the code stays for the record. The built main.pac's two Sledgehammer motions are byte for
  byte v1's (the last package that shipped them before v4: v2 / v3 didn't), and lhand / rhand / mariopt1's too
  (`supamario_sledgehammer_test.py OUT BASE supamario_sledgehammer_v1.zip`).
- The charge glow (Hmr_Crg) and the charged fist flash (Hmr_Crg02) on LHandN with External_Graphic's last word **0**
  (the request's flag, docs/native-notes.md "EffectModule requests": the bone's position only, X x the facing, **Z =
  world depth**) and **Z = 4.64** toward the camera: his head's half-depth beside the left hand at the charge pose
  (3.64: the largest |head mesh point X - LHandN X| at start frame 12, which covers either facing) + 1.0. It follows
  the hand's place; the depth is world space whichever way he faces (a bone-space offset turned with the hand).
  `sledgehammer_frames.json` `glow_depth` has the measure.

### 6. Brighter arcs (user, screenshot 27: "make the Sledgehammer's arcs slightly brighter")
`ARC_GAIN` (R, G, B) **(0.27, 0.19, 0.27)** (v4 0.2 / 0.14 / 0.2, +35%, the yellow-orange hue kept). The charged fist
flash cap (`FLASH_PEAK` 1.0) stays. `supamario_fx_glow.py` (the whole swing, every effect):

| brightest frame | grass (0.5, 0.65, 0.3): over 1.0 / energy | grey (0.5): over 1.0 | black: peak / over 1.0 / energy |
|---|---|---|---|
| v4 uncharged swing | 0 / 68 | 0 | 0.37 / 0 / 68 |
| **v5 uncharged swing** | **1.0** / 92 | **0.7** | 0.51 / 0 / 92 |
| v4 charged swing | 0 (3.5 at most in a frame) / 133 | 0 (3.2) | 1.12 / 0 / 133 |
| **v5 charged swing** | **3.6** (4.0 at most in a frame) / 174 | **3.3** (3.5) | 2.46 / 1.7 / 185 |

v3's dome was 107 / 198 square units over 1.0 on grass: v5's few units are the arcs' hottest overlap and the fist's
sparks / flash.

## Time Skip (side special): the XV2 skill read
User: put Xenoverse 2's skill **1352_HIK_DSH** (Hit's "Time-Skip"; the user also calls it "Jump Spike") on his **side
special**, ground and air (the Cape goes). Files given: the skill's BAC / BCM / BDM / EAN (`_C`, `_F`, `_HIK`, `_MAM`) and
its SE bank `CAR_BTL_S1352_DSH_SE.acb` (6 cues, waveforms in the ACB). No EEPK (the skill's own effects 0 and 11), no
BSA. `tools/supamario_timeskip_xv2.py report` -> `reference/xv2/timeskip_skill.json` (XV2-Tools' names; ThrowHandler /
Functions / ScreenEffect / TargetingAssistance decoded).

### What the files say
- **BCM:** #1-#8 (SuperSkill1, by the opponent's size: OpponentSizeConditions 0x70000 ... 0) all play **BAC 0**, charge
  **BAC 2**, victim on connect **BAC 5**, user on connect BAC 9 / 10 / 4 / 11 (the same entry but for the hold distance:
  1.5 m, 1.8 m for the biggest, 1.5 m and 0.5 m up for #5-#7). No airborne entry (XV2 fights in the air: one move).

  |---|---|---|
  | 0-20 | 0-20 | the wind-up (a crouch, the root 0.24 m back and forward); weak tracking 0.25; skill effect 0 at 0 (no EEPK); skill SE 0 **Basa** at 6 |
  | 20-50 | 20-25 | **ChargeControl 20-50, ChargeTime 40**: held, the entry pauses at 20 (the charge); BPE 85 (a white body outline flash) at 20; tracking 0.05 |
  | 50-60 | 25-35 | **tracking 1.0** (turns to the target), TargetingAssistance Z 50-83; the root launches 1.5 m into the horizontal dash pose (52-60) |
  | 54-83 | 29-58 | **the detection box**: b_C_Base + (0, 0.5, 1.0 m ahead), 0.5 m, skill BDM #0 (type 10, no damage, the victim's BPE 37 black screen), flags 0x2000 ImpactType_Single. BcmCallback Supers 54-85 |
  | 56-85 | 31-60 | **Movement 0.7 m a frame forward, drag 0.003** (~19 m); stage BG effect 60 (the stage's dust) and skill SE 1 **Dash** at 56 |
  | 84-120 | 59-95 | the stop (the root 1.7 m more), back to the stance; skill SE 2 **Hazure** (the whiff) at 86; BPE 85 at 100 |
- **BAC 2 (charged):** the same, the dash pose (EAN 60-85) played three times: **Movement 56-135, the box 54-133**
  (~46 m), Hazure at 136. BAC 1 (a middle length) no BCM entry plays.
- **BAC 4 (the user on connect, skill EAN #1, 81 frames):** **invulnerable 0-80**; ThrowHandler 0-16 **the victim held
  1.5 m in front of his ground point** (g_x_LND, 0.1 m up); BPE 78 **Invert World Colors** at 0 (the time stop) and BTL_CMN

  its 16), entry 7 CMN #96 the face-up slide.
