## Time Skip v1 (`supamario_timeskip_v1.zip`): the side special
Install over Sledgehammer v5 (`vars_reset_v1.zip`), exe `exe_v46x_whipmot` (no exe change: Stage_Freeze, variable resets,
Target 12, Extra hitboxes are in it). Built by `tools/supamario_timeskip_build.py` (it runs Sledgehammer's build, which
runs the earlier moves'; their files are theirs byte for byte except the shared ones). The user's spec (relayed) wins over
XV2 where they differ.

### The move
| | |
|---|---|
| Motions | **0x219 SpecialS** = the dash (XV2 #0 at entry 0's real timing: 96 frames; 0-19 = BAC 0-19, 20-24 = BAC 20-50 at x6, 25-95 = BAC 50-120), **0x21D SpecialAirS** = the connect (#1 1:1, 81 frames); both in either situation (XV2 has no air entry): they were the Cape's ground / air motions. `tools/supamario_timeskip_anim.py` (retarget as the earlier ports, 5.04 units a metre, no TransN, the pelvis's height on RotN). No new slot: ModAnimNames.txt unchanged |
| Charge | special still held at frame 20: the pose held (XV2's ChargeControl), up to **40** frames; let go (or 40): the dash; the motion restarted at 20 so its scripts' clocks start again there |
| Speed (the user) | **constant, by the charge: 2.4 a frame uncharged to 4.2 fully charged**, in proportion to the frames held (20 held: 3.3). His dash / run speed 1.6, the God Fist's burst 4.53 (XV2's own 0.7 m = 3.53). The moving part frames **27-59** (XV2's launch at BAC 52 to the movement's end 85: 33 frames); on a miss its last 8 frames eased down to 1.85 (the stop's first root speed); then on the ground the stop (the root's 8.5 units). Straight travel: uncharged **85.6** (ground) / 77.1 (air), half 111.2 / 102.7, full **136.9 / 128.4** |
| Tracking (the user) | each moving frame: the nearest opponent **in front** (along his facing) **within 80**, **at most 65 degrees up or down** (feet to feet): the dash aimed at it, the same speed; none: the last aim kept. Never turns him. On the ground never aimed down; aimed up off the ground: into the air |
| Gravity (the user) | none through the move (his speeds set every frame, the stick's drift held at 0). After it in the air (Fall, no free fall): **his gravity eased back in over 20 frames** (0.0045 a frame more each frame, Mario's 0.09 at 20) and a fall faster than his max (1.5) brought down **0.05 a frame** (the fall limit lowered step by step, not snapped). Anything but Fall (a jump, an attack, a landing, a hit) ends the ease with his normal gravity |
| Use (the user) | **once per airtime**: VAR_26 set in the air, its variable reset (exe_v46w) **KO 1 / landing 2 / ledge 4 / hit 8** -> 0 registered on him from MSC (`sh_reset_item`); `ts_can_side` (global25, the side special's own check) refuses it in the air while set. On the ground always |
| Detection | XV2's box: bone 0, **5.04 ahead, 2.52 up, 2.52** (frames 29-57; off 2 before the moving part ends), 2%, knockback 1, Clang 0, **shieldable**, Ground/Air 3 (fighters lying down too). **Beats armor**: Sledgehammer's armor lift (now `armor_lift(z, y, r)`, `sh_unarmor` its call) on every frame it is out, given back to fighters it didn't catch. Real hits only: invincible / intangible / countering fighters aren't caught (the dash goes on, another fighter can be) |
| Time stop (the user) | a hit **or a shielding fighter**: the connect motion; the stage and background stopped (`Stage_Freeze(1, 30)` at its frame 0, `(0)` at the punch), **every other fighter frozen** (a 2-frame hitstop renewed every frame by the MSC) until the punch. The victim: a hit one held **7.56 in front, 0.5 up** (XV2's 1.5 m / 0.1 m) in its hitstun, no stage collision, controller off (the KO resets on it); a shielding one kept where it is, shielding, frozen, controller off. Supamario **invincible from the catch through the punch's freeze** (the user's Core Breaker rule) |
| Punch | connect frame 16, Part 1, on the victim's middle (7.56 ahead, 5.5 up, 5.04), **16% (18 with the detection), angle 10 (XV2's blow ~3), BKB 70, KBG 70**, both frozen **30** frames (XV2's time lock), SDI 0, NoGFX (XV2's look from the MSC). Knockback from 0 / 50 / 100%: **98.5 / 142.6 / 186.7** (hitstun 39 / 57 / 74; Mario weight, no DI ~86 / 179 / 306 units) vs Sledgehammer charged 52.8 / 99.7 / 146.7, Core Breaker's crash 75.5 / 125.3 / 175.2 |
| Shields | **uncharged**: the punch on the shield takes **50%** of the max shield (the hit's own taken back: Sledgehammer's way; a break if that empties it) and holds **26 frames of shieldstun** (`cb_shield_tick`); he acts at the connect's **frame 32**: the 16th frame after the shared freeze, the shielder on its 27th: **+11** (the offline model, whose shieldstun counts down a frame early: +9). **Fully charged**: the punch is **unblockable** (Shieldable 0) and does **22%** (+6) to a fighter caught shielding |
| Ends | a miss: Wait at 95 on the ground (the stop), Fall at 60 in the air. A hit: Wait / Fall at the connect's 80 (64 frames after the freeze) |
| B-reverse | the stick back past 0.5 on frames 0-3: his facing only (Sledgehammer's) |
| Staling | none (the status's attack log 0; vanilla 0x1412 the Cape's) |

### Effects and sounds
- **The punch's look** (skill BDM #1's BTL_CMN effect 2), from the MSC where the punch lands (the victim's middle,
  unturned): Btl_HitLOD x1.5 (Sledgehammer's set 0x0341005C), **Btl_HitL x0.8** (new: two sets, the converter splits its
  19 emitters, 0x0341005F / 0x03410060), the sweat x2.0 on its head (0x0341005E), at x1.75. Vibrant (glare x2, capped at
  2.0), halos 0.16 (2: the first set had no room). **Brightness** (`tools/supamario_fx_glow.py ts_hit`): its white flash
  discs and glaring streaks drew 28.6 / 8.9 square units over 1.0 on grass; now capped (flash 0.35, line 0.5, Line00 1.0:
  `PEAK_CAP`): over 1.0 at its brightest frame **0.3 black / 7.4 grass / 4.9 grey** (at most 8.4 in a frame on grass;
  Sledgehammer's hit burst, unchanged since v3, reaches 10.8).
- Smash's dash cloud on the ground at the launch (XV2's stage dust, BG 60). Not converted: the skill's own effects 0 / 11
  (no EEPK), BTL_CMN 108 (a model on the camera), the BPE screen effects (no Smash equivalent).
- **Sounds** (levelled to -0.5 dBFS peak, into his bank: ids **0x0CB1009D-0x0CB100A3**): Basa at 6 (wind-up only), Dash at
  31, Hazure at a miss's end (MSC), TimeStop at the connect's 0, PumchiFuri at 8, Hit_TmLck at the punch (MSC, on a hit or
  the shield), CMN CharaAway_HKT at the connect's 63 (the victim's entry 6: 47 frames after its launch). No voices.

### How (files)
- `supamario.c` (end): `ts_*`; `func_2259` (main) -> ts_init / `ts_loop`, `func_2280` (end) -> ts_end, `func_82` -> ts_tick,
  `func_2249` global25 = ts_can_side, `func_2251` attack log 0, `func_2262` / `func_2264` (the Cape's init / exec) skipped.
  **No new globals** (127 of 128); **VAR_22-30** (TS_V_PH, CHG, SLOT, K, USED, BREV, EASE, AIM, SHV); VAR_31 free.
  `msc_fingerprint` vs Sledgehammer v5: those 7 functions, `sh_unarmor` (now `armor_lift`'s call) and the new ts_*.
- Scripts: `specials` (the detection box at 29 when TS_V_PH > 2.5, Basa / Dash, the dust, a rumble), `specialairs`
  (Stage_Freeze, the three punches by TS_V_CHG / TS_V_SHV, TimeStop / PumchiFuri / CharaAway, a rumble).
- `tools/supamario_timeskip_xv2.py`, `_anim.py`, `_build.py`, `_test.py`; `reference/xv2/timeskip_skill.json`,
  `timeskip_root.json` (the root's speed table the MSC's `ts_vroot` is built from).

### Tests
`tools/supamario_timeskip_test.py OUT SLEDGEHAMMER_OUT`: the MSC in the interpreter with a model of the game: ground /
air taps (phases, the box's frames, the speeds and their ease, travel, Fall, no gravity, the gravity's ease after, the
fall limit stepping 0.05 down after a steep dive), the use and its resets (landing / hit), charges (half 3.3, full 4.2,
the restart), ground and air catches (held, the controller, the bystander frozen to the punch, 18%, the look and the
sound once, invincibility, the ends), shields (uncharged +9 in the model, 50% / 26 frames; fully charged through it, 22%),
refusals (invincible, countering, intangible), armor beaten (lifted on contact), a lying fighter caught, tracking (45 /
64 degrees tracked, 70 / behind / 85 away not, down in the air, not on the ground, level on the ground stays grounded,
never turned, the speed unchanged), B-reverse, a cut / a KO in the hold; the scripts, motions (only SpecialS /
SpecialAirS differ from Sledgehammer v5's build), effect sets (Sledgehammer's untouched, facing-safe, HDR), the sound ids.
The Sledgehammer, Fake Death, Core Breaker and God Fist (`--all-models`) suites, `msc_stack_check`.

### Not confirmed in game / approximated
Stage_Freeze for a cinematic hold in a real match (the commands' in-game test was training); the other fighters' freeze
by hitstop (their projectiles and items keep moving); the take-off from the ground when aimed up (`func_20`); the energy-1
limit write easing the fall (the Fall status doesn't reset it each frame); the punch's explicit 30-frame freeze on a
shield; that `Shieldable 0` hits through a raised shield; the body stays horizontal whatever the aim (no rotation).

## Time Skip v2 (`supamario_timeskip_v2.zip`): the user's v1 test, the XV2 clip, the time stop's look
Over v1 on exe_v46y_hold (no exe change). Changed: the MSC, game.bin, the ptcl / efc (new sets after every earlier one),
a new effect model folder `model/EffSupamarioTsDmgGrs2`. The user's XV2 clip (`fc8b90ed`, a screen recording of XV2 at
60 fps) is the reference for the look; "Jump Spike" is only the move's title (no jump or spike).

### The user's points: cause and fix
| | cause (evidence) | fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1. never resets in the air | v1 cleared VAR_26 only through the exe's variable resets (`sh_reset_item(0x31A, owner 0, mask 15)`). Offline it is right: the ID (0x31A = VAR_26), owner 0 and mask are what the cave wants, `tools/var_reset_test.py` on the user's exe_v46y_hold passes all of its checks (MSC selection, landing / ledge / hit / KO events), and the MSC writes the item (timeskip test). The reset hooks were never confirmed in a match (docs/native-notes.md "Variable resets": the landing hook, the owner lookup `[sm+0x9C]+0x28`, the events' timing are static readings); in game the flag never cleared, so one of those doesn't hold in a match | `ts_reset` (func_82 via ts_tick, every frame, every status): VAR_26 back to 0 on the ground or a ledge (situation 0 / 1), in a damage / capture status (0x3F-0x4C), a cliff status (0x72-0x7A), Dead / Rebirth / Entry; not while he is in the move (a stale status read on its first frames). The exe reset stays registered (harmless) |
| 2. no movement in the air | v1 moved him with energy 3's speed (`KineticModule(0x11, 3, ...)`) on the ground and in the air. In the air the status is on kinetic type 0 (FALL), where energy 3 isn't on: the Cape's own air code makes it again and turns it on (`func_2263`: `KineticModule(0xE, 3, ...)` / `(0xB, 3)`), v1 never did. Energy 2 was held at 0 and energy 1's speed is the aim's vertical part: 0 on a level dash. Byleth v11 saw the same (loop-set energy-3 speeds never moved her in the air; position writes did) | `ts_move`: his travel is his position, `0x0F000004` forward / `0x0F000005` up each frame he isn't frozen, on the ground and in the air alike (the God Fist's gf_travel); his energies held at 0 (`ts_still`). A miss in the air hands its last speed (the eased 1.85 along the aim) to Fall (energy 2 / 1) |
| 3. can't leave edges | the same: the ground's correction 1 slides him off, and once in the air nothing moved him (he hovered at the edge) | the position travel goes on in the air; `ts_still` keeps correction 1 every ground frame; `ts_sit` gives the air kinetics; no Fall until the moving part's end |
| 4. a massive white flash | v1 played Sledgehammer's own Btl_HitLOD set: its `flash_1_1` is a white disc covering ~55 square units of a grass stage on its first frame (54.6 of the 70.5 energy at frame 0), held by the punch's 30-frame freeze. XV2 has a white screen flash there too (clip frame 44, one frame) | Time Skip's own copy `PtcSupamarioTsBtl_HitLOD` (0x03410061; Sledgehammer's set untouched), each emitter's peak capped (`LOD_CAP`: flash 0.22, grd01 0.45, ptcl / line 0.8); Btl_HitL's caps lowered (`PEAK_CAP`: flash 0.2, line 0.25, Line00 0.5, grd00 0.6, LINE / stripe 0.8). `supamario_fx_glow.py ts_hit` (v1's call: `ts_hit_v1`): frame 0's energy 63.7 -> 9.6, the most over 1.0 in any frame 4.1 -> 0.3 on grass, 0.9 -> 0.0 on grey (Sledgehammer v5's uncharged arcs: 1.0) |
| 5. the God Fist's tracking | v1's `ts_track` was its own code (no gf_* call), but on the ground it never aimed down, and an up aim took him into the air where nothing moved (2): in game only a level ground dash was left, like the God Fist's ground rule | `ts_track` without any ground rule: the nearest opponent in front within 80 (feet to feet), up to 65 degrees up or down, the direction only (never turned, the speed unchanged). Tested on its own: 70 away (past the God Fist's 55) tracked, 70 degrees (inside its 75) not |
| 6. nothing off the ground | the ground rule and (2) | aimed up from the ground he leaves it (situation 2, air kinetics) and moves up; an opponent below (off stage): the aim down kept, the floor takes the downward part while he is on it (no pushing through it or a platform), past the edge he goes down along it at the full speed |
| 7. point blank | XV2's box covers 2.52-7.56 ahead (radius 2.52 at 5.04), out from frame 29: a fighter on top of him was never in it, and by 29 he had already dashed 2 frames past it. The hit-confirm (Target 12's slot, else the nearest frozen fighter) was fine | the box from frame 26 (the frame before the dash) and a second one, ID 1 (Part 0: one hit a fighter), at his middle: 1.5 ahead, 4.5 up, radius 4 (-2.5 to 5.5 ahead, no gap to XV2's); armor lifted under both, after his move (the collision's place); the victim lookup measures from both |
| 8. the purple line | the clip: no line during the dash. The purple is XV2's skill effect 11 at the punch (BAC entry 4 item 8, frame 16: EEPK type 5 = the super skill's own, skill 1352, effect 11): purple speed lines from the victim forward along the punch (clip 60-100), then a long purple line along its flight fading (to ~130); and effect 0 (entry 0 item 9, frame 0, b_C_Base): pale lavender light shards rising round him in the wind-up (clip 8-30). There is no EEPK for 1352_HIK_DSH (the user checked the cpk), so the skill's EEPK is another file named by `system/custom_skill.cus`; not in what was sent | stand-ins (`lines_set` / `shards_set`, Btl_HitL's soft flash disc billboard stretched): `PtcSupamarioTsLines` 0x03410062 (8 speed lines 24 long in a band the victim's height + one 60 long, 45 / 85 frames, purple) at the victim's middle turned to his facing (`ts_lines`, with the hit look); `PtcSupamarioTsShards` 0x03410063 (6 vertical lavender streaks rising) at wind-up frames 6 / 14 and every 12 frames of a held charge (`ts_shards`). Additive, peak ~1: on a bright stage they read faint |
| 9. Sledgehammer dimmer (item 9) | nothing of Sledgehammer's changed: v1's ptcl against vars_reset_v1's, set by set, emitter by emitter (bytes), every texture decoded, every shader, the efc entries, effect.bin's other scripts, main.pac's other motions: all identical (`tools/supamario_timeskip_test.py` now checks it on every build). The one recent change to its look is v5's own (vars_reset_v1): the charge glow moved to External_Graphic flag 0 with Z 4.64 (if the flag is bone space in game, the glow sits along LHandN's Z, by or behind his head) and the arcs +35% | Time Skip's hit look no longer uses Sledgehammer's set (own copy) |

### The time stop's look (item 11)
- **XV2:** BPE 78 (Invert World Colors) and BTL_CMN effect 108 (`HIT_DmgGrs2.emo` / `.emm` / `.emb` / `.mat.ema` / `.obj.ema`
  in `vfx/cmn`, scale 0.36, attached to the camera, LoopCancel) from the connect (entry 4 item 7, frame 0) to the punch
  (item 9, frame 16, OFF). The clip: one white frame (44), then the world dark blue-grey with white crack lines to the punch (58).
- **Darkened world:** `Screen_Tint(Mode 0, 4 frames in, R 0x14, G 0x16, B 0x2C, A 0xB4, layer 0, priority 0xFF)` (Sonic's Final
  Smash darkening, docs/acmd-params/misc-a.md's real order) at the connect, `End_Screen_Tint(0, 15)` at the punch: in
  `time_stop_look()`, its own commands so the camera-attached command (camera_effects_v1) can take their place.
- **The glass:** HIT_DmgGrs2 converted by `tools/xv2_to_nud.py` (35 shard meshes, 99-frame animation: the shards fly out
  from about frame 25, fade by 75) into `model/EffSupamarioTsDmgGrs2` (efc 0x03410064, texture 0x15142380), played by the
  MSC at the catch (`ts_glass`): at the victim's middle, 15 toward the camera, x0.36 (XV2's scale). XV2 has it on the
  camera; this is world-placed until the camera command exists.
- **The hit look on the victim:** XV2's BDM #1 Effect1 (BTL_CMN 2) plays where the hit lands on the victim; ours is at
  the held victim's middle (its position + 5 up: the punch box's own place, 7.56 ahead / 5.5 up), world-placed (the
  victim is frozen for the 30-frame time lock, then launched).

### The time stop's release (the v2 bug) and the camera build
- **Default build** (`CAMERA_FX = False`): the darkening is the MSC's screen tint (`ts_tint`: MSC `EffectModule(0, slot,
  frames, R, G, B, A, layer, priority)` = Screen_Tint's EffectModule vt +0x19C, `EffectModule(1, slot, frames)` =
  End_Screen_Tint's vt +0x1B4, read in the MSC handler `0x02C2CEC8` -> `0x02004530`; Meta Knight's Final Smash uses both),
  on at the catch, off at the punch; the glass is v2's model in front of the victim. Any exit before the punch (a status
  change: Supamario hit / KO'd, the match ending, a cut) goes through `ts_end` -> `ts_stop_look_off`: the tint off, the
  glass terminated (`EffectModule(0x2723)`); the victim let go (v2); the other fighters' freeze isn't renewed (gone within 2
  frames); the stage's: the connect's game script renews `Stage_Freeze(1, 4)` every frame (a 16-pass loop; v2 set 30 frames
  once), so it lapses within 4 frames when the script stops.
- **Camera build** (`CAMERA_FX = True`, needs exe_v46z_camfx: Effect_Camera): the darkening an alpha-blended black-blue
  quad (`PtcSupamarioTsDark` 0x03410065, (0.08, 0.09, 0.17) at 70%) fixed to the screen (Mode 1, Size 2.2, Z 20, faded at
  the punch), the glass in front of the camera (Mode 0, Z 25, x0.1), both unslowed; the same exits terminate them. Off
  until camera_effects is confirmed in game (its v1 test drew a screen-filling white flash).

## Time Skip v4 (`supamario_timeskip_v4.zip`): XV2's own effects, the point-blank catch, the user's v3 test
Over v3. **Needs exe_v46z_camfx3 or later** (camera_effects_v3 / v4: Stage_Freeze On 1 is its stage-only freeze, no flash;
on exe_v46y_hold it turned the whole scene white, below). No exe in the package; CAMERA_FX still off. Changed: the MSC,
game.bin, effect.bin, the ptcl / efc. Also Sledgehammer v6 (below).

### The skill's EEPK (`1350_HIK_FRT.eepk`, the folder the user sent; `reference/xv2/timeskip_eepk.json`)
- **Containers:** EMO (`Frt_Bas.emo` / `.emm` / `.emb` / `.mat.ema` / `.obj.ema`: a refraction cone model) and PBIND
  `1350_HIK_FRT.pbind.emb` (`Frt_Line`, `Frt_CrgAura`, `Frt_CrgAuraB`, `Frt_CrgAuraH`, `Frt_Sucses`, `Frt_Hit` .emp) with
  `1350_HIK_FRT.ptcl.emm` (13 materials) / `.ptcl.emb` (11 textures). No ETR, ECF or light EMA. Effects 0-6 and 10-12
  (1350 FRT's own: charges on the hands / elbow, Frt_Sucses, Frt_Bas + Frt_Line on the hands, Frt_Hit on either hand).
  `xv2vfx.load_package` loads it with strict=False (three EMP blocks carry stray runtime words).
- **What the 1352 BAC plays** (every other effect item is BTL_CMN's or the stage's): **effect 0** (entry 0 item 2,
  frame 0) and **effect 11** (entries 4 / 9 / 10 / 11 item 16, frame 16). Their bones are the EEPK's (ESK), not the BAC's
  b_C_Base (effect 0's parts name three bones; effects 3 / 4 differ only by bone).
- **Effect 0 is the user's "purple aura left behind at the start"**: `Frt_CrgAura` on b_C_Pelvis at (0, -0.3, 0) m,
  `Frt_CrgAuraB` on b_C_Pelvis, `Frt_CrgAura` on b_R_Foot, none with PositionUpdate (left where they were made: the
  clip's streaks behind him at 32), x1.0; `Frt_CrgAuraH` on b_L_Elbow x0.5, following. Each EMP is one ConeExtrude
  ribbon (a stripe behind a particle rising 5 m/s off a 0.3 m circle, Vortex-spun), every 2 frames for 40 (H: 20).
  It replaces v3's Hit aura (BTL_AURA BoostV_Loop).
- **Effect 11** (the punch's purple lines): `Frt_Hit.emp` (31 emissions: the burst sprites, 8 `line_add` speed lines,
  navy `wave_nml` / magenta `wave_add` slashes, the backing lines, two rings, sparkles) on g_R_Hand, oriented by the
  hand, RotateUpdate only (world-fixed), x1.0. It replaces v2 / v3's stand-in lines.

### How they're converted and placed (`tools/supamario_timeskip_build.py` e0_* / e11_*)
| | |
|---|---|
| Effect 0, world (0x03410063 `PtcSupamarioTsFrt_CrgAura`) | the three parts in one set, each at its bone's place at SpecialS frame 0 (HipN 4.05 up; RFootJ 2.6 behind, 1.6 up) + the EEPK offset (x 5.04 a metre, / the call's x1.75), the EMP's own node offset kept (the stripes start by his knees / feet and rise). Played by the MSC at his ground point, turned to his facing (Y 0 / pi), at the move's frame 0 (`ts_init`, XV2's BAC 0; v3 frame 4) and every 20 frames of a held charge (v3's); follow none |
| Effect 0, elbow (0x03410069 `..._CrgAuraH`) | the effect script at SpecialS frame 0: External_Graphic on LArmJ (bone 15), x0.875, last word 0 (the bone's place only: XV2's Orientation User), its particles following |
| Effect 11 (0x03410062 / 0x03410066, halos 0x03410067 / 0x03410068) | split in two sets (16 emitters at most), halos (0.16) on the 19 glaring emitters in a companion set each; turned so the punch is +X (E11_TURN, Y +90: Smash draws a streak back along its motion, and the clip has the lines from the fist forward through the victim, so the hand's +Z is the punch). Played by the MSC with the hit look (`ts_e11`, on the punch's first hit frame) at his right fist (RHandN at SpecialAirS 16: 6.85 ahead, 8.13 up; the build checks the motion), x1.75, turned to his facing |
| ConeExtrude | `xv2_to_ptcl.CONE_STREAKS` (opt-in: every earlier build byte for byte): a ribbon as a type 3 streak along its velocity, the ribbon's length (5 m/s x 16 frames = 1.33 m) from birth, its particle living 16 frames longer; the alpha 0 -> the peak x2 (5-15% of its life) -> 0 (eft's 3 keys can't fall by 40%). The Vortex swirl isn't converted |
| Secondary colour | the normal-blend emissions with EnableSecondaryColor (Frt_Hit's navy slashes and backing lines, CrgAuraB) take the mean of Color1 and Color2 x the texture tint: Color1 alone drew dark navy smudges; the clip has them blue-violet |
| Vibrant | as the other XV2 moves: glare tint, colour x2 capped at 2.0, alpha x2 (`hdr_emitter`) |

## Sledgehammer v6 (in `supamario_timeskip_v4.zip`): the charged air spike can't be teched
User: "the fully charged Sledgehammer must be untechable for opponents hit in the air". Smash 4 has no hitbox or damage flag
that forbids a tech (the techs are the victim's own DamageFly / DamageFall landing and wall / ceiling paths, README "Super
God Fist v17"), so the held-down hold's way: on a charged real hit (`sh_struck`) on an airborne fighter, `sh_spike_start`
turns its controller off (Input_Set 21's variable: no shield press, a player can't tech), empties its buffer and registers
the lockout's resets (its KO / a hit on it by anyone / his KO give its controller back: `lock_resets(s, 9)`), SH_V_GS -
its slot, phase 3. `sh_spike_tick` (func_82): nothing in its hitstop; a tech it takes anyway (a CPU's tech request doesn't
go through the controller) replaced the same frame by the missed tech's own status: in place / roll (0x64 / 0x65) ->
DownBound 0x4D, wall / wall jump (0x66 / 0x67) -> WallDamage 0x49, ceiling (0x68) -> CeilingDamage 0x4A, EscapeAir (the
CPU's landing dodge) -> DownBound on the ground, DamageFall in the air; let go (controller back) when its hitstun reads 0,
when it lies or bounces (0x4D-0x53), or when it leaves every damage status. Uncharged hits stay techable.
Tests (`tools/supamario_sledgehammer_test.py` spike_tests): **141 ok** (127 + 14): controller off the frame after the hit,
buffer, the resets registered; back when the hitstun ends; each tech replaced; let go on its KO, a hit on it, his KO;
uncharged untouched. The test's model now keeps a shield the swing hit out of the swing (its hit record).
Not confirmed in game: that a status written over a tech on its first frame takes (Passive's own invincibility frames).

## Time Skip v5 (`supamario_timeskip_v5.zip`): the point-blank arming, the punch's burst, the glass on the screen, the outline, the landing lag
Over v4. **Needs exe_v46z_camfx5 or later** (camera_effects_v5.zip: Effect_Camera, confirmed in game; its flash-free
Stage_Freeze). Changed: the MSC, effect.bin, the ptcl / efc, the glass model's texture (`model/EffSupamarioTsDmgGrs2/model.nut`).
CAMERA_FX (the darkening on the camera) still off: the MSC's tint stays until the user answers.

### The user's v4 test (clips 6e213949, aaf3050c; XV2's screenshots 30 / 31 and clip b66e8a38 = fc8b90ed)
| | cause | fix |
|---|---|---|
| point blank: "the hit detection hit activates before the moving hitbox ... unable to hit if it hits on the frame it activates" (aaf3050c: a training DK touching him, 124 -> 126%, the dash on through it) | v1-v4 looked for the catch only from the motion frame TS_HIT_FROM (26) on, and v4's `ts_rec` noted the damage on every dash frame without a pending hit. When the game script makes the box a frame before the MSC reads that frame (an ACMD FRAME 26 run as the motion reaches 25) and it hits at once, the MSC read the box's frame as 25 through the hit's freeze: no catch looked for, while `ts_rec` noted the hit's damage; after the freeze `ts_took` saw none and the hit was dropped. Replayed on v4's build (the model's `script_lead=1`): 2%, no catch, no hold, no freeze (the clip) | the catch looked for on **every frame of the dash** (no frame guard: the flags appear only once the box exists); `ts_rec` never while the box's hit flags are set (`ts_flags`); the armor lift a frame earlier. The model test (an opponent touching him at activation, hit on the box's first frame, with and without func_82 first / Target 12 empty / the freeze unseen): caught, held, the bystander frozen, punched (18%) |
| "the punch effect looks much weaker and compressed" | v4: x1.75, every emitter capped for "over 1.0" (the purple lines 0.15, the magenta slashes 0.07), the navy flames (Color1 / Color2 mean) drawn opaque dark blue, the sparkle dots 0.01 m (under a pixel at Smash's camera). XV2 (screenshots): large translucent violet flames from the victim's back, white-pink sparkle streams, a wide cone of horizontal purple lines far past it | **x2.6** (x1.5); the normal-blend flames / backing lines (EnableSecondaryColor) Color2 x the texture tint lifted 60% to white (lilac), alpha x0.55 (translucent, after hdr's x2); wave_nml_4's black pair x0.35; the sparkle dots x6; caps (`E11_CAP`) raised: lines 0.38, slashes 0.25, sprites 0.4 / 0.45, rings 0.5, flames 1.0. The check is now the **white** area (purple past 1.0 blooms purple, the user's rule) |
| "the glass ... supposed to be clear and apply to the screen" | v2-v4: HIT_DmgGrs2 world-placed in front of the victim; XV2 draws it with T1_VFX_MTN_REFRACT and its DATA0.dds is a refraction offset map (R / G about 0.5, B low): drawn as colour, additive, it was the yellow panes | on the screen: `Effect_Camera(Mode 1, Z 20, Size 0.095, unslowed)` at the connect's frame 0 (its pane 38.6 x 28.8 units = 3.67 x 2.74 half screen heights: over a 16:9 screen), `Terminate_Graphic_Effect(1, 1)` at the punch (XV2 turns BTL_CMN 108 off at 16; the clip: the cracks over the stopped world 44-58, gone at 59); every exit before the punch: `ts_stop_look_off` (unchanged). Clear: `glass_cracks` bakes each shard mesh's border edges (1381, the UVs a planar projection of the pane) into a 512 texture, light blue-white (0.75, 0.9, 1.0), the panes 3%: additive (the material's own blend), only the cracks show; the shards still fly apart after the punch in the model's animation (hidden there) |
| "the outline made Supamario glow harshly" | v4's FLASH white at 110 overlays the whole body. Smash 4 has no rim-only colour command (the ACMD events list: FLASH / FLASH_FRM / FLASH_NO_STOP / COL_NORMAL tint the body; LIGHT_SHINE / LIGHT_AXIS are lights) | FLASH light blue-white (215, 230, 255) at **40** of 255 |
| landing lag (user: "high landing lag after using Time Skip if it ended in the air ... even after doing other moves", Bayonetta's up special) | (no Bayonetta files in the repo: the rule as the user states it) | `ts_end` marks an air end (VAR_31 = 100, free outside the move); `ts_land_lag` (func_82, before ts_reset): his first landing status (0x16 / 0x17 / 0x18 / 0x1A) becomes **LandingFallSpecial 30 frames** (work float 0, sh_land's way), whatever he did in the air before; a LandingFallSpecial landing keeps its own; cleared on a ledge, a hit / grab, Dead / Rebirth / Entry; not tied to TS_V_USED (the exe's landing reset could clear it first) |

### XV2's time stop look (for the user's decision)
The clip: catch at 44 (one white frame), **44-58 the world dark blue-grey and desaturated** (fighters normal) with the
glass's white cracks and pale wedges over the screen; at the punch (59) the cracks are gone and **the world turns white**
(a negative: the stage washed white with sketch-like dark lines, BPE 78 Invert World Colors at the punch again) through
the slow-motion to ~130, the fighters and the purple burst drawn normally on it. Ours: the dark tint from the catch to the
punch (matches 44-58), faded out over 15 frames after; nothing for the white negative at the punch.

## Time Skip v6 (`supamario_timeskip_v6.zip`): the white after the punch, the charge's white body, an outline, 3D glass, the landing
Over v5. **Needs exe_v46z_inv** (screen_invert_v1: exe_v46z_camfx5 + Screen_Invert; `port_check` X10) with `INVERT_FX`
on (the default); with it off, exe_v46z_camfx5. No exe in the package. Changed: the MSC, effect.bin, the ptcl / efc
(effect 0's colour, two new sets), the glass model (texture; with INVERT_FX its material too). Effect 11 unchanged.

### The user's v5 test (clip d8c96e8e, 60 fps; XV2 clip b66e8a38 = fc8b90ed, screenshots 30 / 31)
| | cause (evidence) | fix |
|---|---|---|
| "fix the purple aura during the charging" | d8c96e8e 42-94: from the charge's first held frame (44; the wind-up 24-43 looks normal) his whole body turns light blue-white, a smooth ramp to opaque white by ~64 (his silhouette, crisp: a body overlay, not particles), blooming into a white blob 76-94, gone at once at 96 (the dash: the motion restarts). That is v5's outline: FLASH (215, 230, 255) faded to 40 over 4 frames (`Change_Overlay`) running on at ~10 a frame while the MSC holds the motion's frame (`MotionModule 0x2713` every charge frame) to 255. The aura itself never showed: effect 0's streaks were capped at 0.32 and CrgAuraB had taken v5's Frt_Hit "secondary lift" (pale cyan). XV2 (8-29): one aura at a time, thin lavender streaks with white cores rising round him, left behind as he dashes; placement / timing of ours match it (each emits 40 frames, replayed as the last one's emission ends) | no body overlay at all (the outline is an effect now, below); effect 0's additive streaks violet (CrgAura / CrgAuraH (0.55, 0.33, 1.0)) at **0.34**, the alpha-blended CrgAuraB light violet (0.62, 0.5, 1.0) at 40% of its alpha (v5's dimmed colour on an alpha-blended streak drew dark navy in fx_render) (`E0_RGB`, `E0_CAP6`: lavender at 0.6 stacked to 8.6 square units of white on grass; this draws violet, the picture changed 32.2 where v5's 9.7); the replay not as the dash starts (v4 / v5 played one more aura there, on top of the last) |
| "the outline still turns Supamario into a glow" | v4 / v5's FLASH (Color_Overlay) tints the whole body (Smash 4's overlays have no rim mode; LIGHT_SHINE is a light). XV2 (`adam_shader`'s shader_age_ps / technique_age_sds, the user's folder): its body outlines are screen-space post passes (the `EDGELINE_CT_RIM` / `EDGELINE_CT_MUL` / `AGE_TEST_EDGELINE_SOBEL` families, edge colour `g_EdgeColor`, rim `g_vRimColor`): an edge detection, not a material tint. Smash 4 has none: its fighter shader's rim (NUD `NU_fresnelColor`) is a per-material constant (no ACMD / MSC switch; a per-object override would be an exe cave in the model draw: not done), the Final Smash-ready look is particles plus an overlay | **an effect-based rim**: one additive ring billboard (`PtcSupamarioTsOutline` 0x0341006A: white-blue (0.85, 0.92, 1.0), alpha 0.34, a soft ring: half inside, peak at 0.78 of the radius, 0 at the rim) on 16 bones (`OUTLINE_BONES`: HatN, HeadN, BustN, WaistN, shoulders, elbows, hands, legs, knees, feet; sizes from c00's T pose part radii + 0.6), `External_Graphic` at frame 0 of both motions (earlier rings ended first), ended at each motion's end and by `ts_fx_off` on any exit. Drawn through the bone's centre, each body part's own front half hides the ring inside his silhouette: only a soft glow just outside his edge shows, his colours untouched |
| "add XV2's photo-negative look after the punch" | XV2's BAC: BPE 78 "Invert World Colors" at the connect's 0, again (flag word 2: its off form, as BPE 85's) at 17; BPE 2 "Weak White Flash" at 16. Measured on the clip (mean colours, the same screen areas): the charge's ground (177, 193, 206), the stop (72, 83, 100): **an inversion of the world** (the fighters not inverted: Goku Black's hair stays black), after the punch (220, 228, 235) to +36, back to (175, 193, 202) by +50-60: **not a negative but the stage's own pale colours washed ~55% white** (the white strongest at +0-5: BPE 2). Hit's "sketch" lines are XV2's toon ink lines (there in the charge too) | the wash: `PtcSupamarioTsWash` (0x0341006B), two alpha-blended white quads on the camera (`Effect_Camera` Mode 1, Z 20, size 2.2, unslowed) at the punch (the connect's 16): (0.94, 0.97, 1.0) at 0.48 in 2 frames, held, out over the last 14 of 48 real frames; the punch's flash 0.45 out over 7. Alpha blended: it never goes past white (no bloom). Killed by `ts_fx_off` on any exit. **`INVERT_FX` (on, the default; the coordinator: the user asked for the negative)**: `Screen_Invert` (screen_invert_v1) instead: at the connect's 0 the stop's dark blue-grey negative `(1, 1, 16, 2, 4, Sat 0, Bright 0.8, + (19, 37, 60))` (screen_invert's fit to this clip), replaced at the punch by a white negative `(1, 1, 34, 0, 14, Sat 0.15, Bright 0.35, + (165, 170, 180))` (screen_invert's preset Bright 0.6 + (102, 104, 110) drew Smashville mid-grey; the clip's world after the punch is (220, 228, 235)): held to +34, out by +48; both end by themselves (Frames), so no exit leaves them on. No camera darkening / wash quads then (the filter would invert them). It filters the whole 3D picture: the fighters are inverted too (XV2's aren't) |
| the stop wasn't dark, the punch was (found in d8c96e8e) | the glass at 130-146 over an undarkened stage; the MSC's `Screen_Tint` (on at the catch) shows from the punch (148: the picture's mean 146 -> 66) and fades to ~160: the tint doesn't advance while `Stage_Freeze` holds the stage | `CAMERA_FX` on: the darkening is the camera's quad (`Effect_Camera`, drawn through the freeze), from the connect's 0 to the punch (XV2 44-58); the MSC's tint off (TS_CAMFX 1) |
| "use the real XV2 glass break / less 2D" | v2-v5 already use HIT_DmgGrs2's own 3D shards (37 meshes, `tools/xv2_to_nud.py`) and their .ema animation (flat to frame 10, breaking apart from 12, toward the screen's back), but v5 killed the effect at the punch (16), when the break had just begun, and its texture was outlines only (one value): it read as a flat crack decal. XV2 (44-58): the cracks plus pale wedges (each shard's own refraction offset), gone under the white at 59 | the glass stays **6 frames of his script past the punch** (`GLASS_BREAK`; his 30-frame hit freeze between: ~36 real frames of the shards breaking apart and flying off, unslowed); each shard's pane its own sheen (`GLASS_SHEEN` 0.03-0.2: the narrow slivers brightest, plus a little random), the cracks 1.5 texels, full on the side facing the upper left and 0.55 on the others (a bevel); additive: clear. With INVERT_FX the glass sits inside the filtered picture, so it is drawn pre-inverted: alpha-blended (NUD src 5 / dst 1) black with the cracks / panes in the alpha (BC3), coming out (0.87, 0.95, 1.0) light blue-white under the stop's filter, white under the white one |
| "use Mario's normal landing lag animation" | v5 played LandingFallSpecial's own (the special fall landing) | `ts_land_lag` keeps the 30-frame LandingFallSpecial and marks it (TS_V_RETRY = 300); `func_586` (the landing statuses' start) plays **LandingHeavy (0x3B) at its length / 30** over it and clears the mark (Sledgehammer's LandingLight way); the up special's special fall landing and Sledgehammer's unchanged |

## Time Skip v7 (`supamario_timeskip_v7.zip`): the outline through Outline_Set, the negative's fades
Over v6. **Needs exe_v46z_outline** (model_commands_v1.zip: everything of exe_v46z_inv / the camera exes plus
Outline_Set and Mesh_Visibility; `port_check` X10 / X11); the user confirmed Outline_Set, Screen_Invert, Mesh_Visibility
and Effect_Camera in game. No exe in the package. Changed: the MSC, effect.bin, the ptcl / efc (the outline's ring set
gone, the white fade quad added). Every other move's set byte for byte as before.

### The outline: Outline_Set instead of v6's rings
v6's `PtcSupamarioTsOutline` (16 ring billboards on his bones, an effect-only rim) is gone from the scripts and the bank.
The outline is `Outline_Set` (docs/acmd-extra-commands.md): his own edges lit through the fighter shaders' rim term,
edge only.
| | value | from |
|---|---|---|
| colour | (215, 230, 255), Tint 0 (edge only) | v5 / v6's light blue-white (the user); the clip's rim adds a near-neutral white |
| strength | **Alpha 140** (0.55 of the colour at a fully edge-on pixel; the material's exponent 2-4 thins it inward) | clip b66e8a38, the stop 46-50 against the dark world: a 2-3 px rim adding (+55, +59, +58) / 255 to the background beside him and taking his dark hair's edge from ~10 to 120-142 (~0.45-0.5 of white at the very edge), plus a faint +10-20 haze outside his silhouette that a rim can't draw |
| pulse | **none** (Flash 0) | the rim's peak is steady frame to frame through the stop (46 / 48 / 50) and the dash (32-41); BPE 85's own entry ("Flashing ...") lives in XV2's system BPE file, not in the user's files, so no rate to copy |
| on | **SpecialS frame 20** (WIND_END, the charge's start), in over 4 | `reference/xv2/timeskip_skill.json`: BAC 0's BPE 85 at 20 (flag word 0); the clip: faint at 15, clear from 21 |
| off (whiff) | **frame 75** (real of BAC 100), out over 4 | BAC 0's BPE 85 at 100 with flag word 2 (its off form); BAC 2 (charged) on again at 100, off at 160 = its stop: our charged dash is the same motion, so 75 too |
| off (punch) | **SpecialAirS frame 16, at once** | BAC 4's BPE 85 at 16 (off), under BPE 2's white flash; the clip: gone under the white at 59 |
| connect | renewed from SpecialAirS 0 to the punch | BAC 4 has no "on": the dash's outline stays through the stop (clip 46-58: the rim on him round the stop) |

**Ending it on every exit.** Outline_Set is an ACMD command (the MSC can't send it; `ts_fx_off` no longer kills
anything of it). The scripts renew it every 4 frames (`OL_KEEP`), each held 12 (`OL_HOLD`: the catch's 3-frame freeze
and its 3 retries between the dash's last renewal and the connect's first fit), then out over 4 (`OL_CUT_OUT`): a cut
move (hit, ledge, any status change) leaves it at most 20 frames (in 4 + held 12 + out 4: the hold counts once it is
full; 18 at worst in the test's model), fading over the last 4; a KO, the match's end and a
training reset drop it at once (the cave). A held charge stays lit: the scripts' clock runs on while the MSC holds the
pose (Core Breaker v4's finding, the test model's), so the renewals go on, and the restart at frame 20 renews it again.
A renewal fades from where it is (none once full): no flicker.

### The negative's fades (the user's Screen_Invert note: partial strengths show grey)
A fade shows `(1 - w) x + w f(x)`, whose contrast is `1 - w (1 + Brightness x Strength)`: somewhere in every fade the
picture goes flat.
| | v6 | v7 |
|---|---|---|
| the stop (connect 0) | in over 2 (w 0.5: flat grey for a frame), held 16, a cut move's end out over 4 | **at once**, held 21 (the punch + the catch's freeze + 2), a cut move's end at once (XV2's BPE 78 is a cut: clip 44 -> 45) |
| the punch (16) | the white negative at once, held 34, out over 14: its contrast -0.35 -> 1 passes 0 at w = 1 / 1.35 = 0.74 (the 4th frame) and stays near flat light grey (abs. under 0.3) for 6 of the 14 | the white negative at once, **ended by the script at the punch + 4** (its 30 frozen frames + 4 = v6's +34) at once, under **a white quad** on the camera started in the same frame (`PtcSupamarioTsFade` 0x0341006B: Effect_Camera Mode 1, Z 20, size 2.2, unslowed; alpha-blended (0.94, 0.97, 1.0) from 0.7 straight down to 0 over 14 real frames). The negative's mean (~0.84 on a mid picture) = 0.3 x 0.5 + 0.7 x 0.97: no step in brightness; the contrast only rises (0.3 -> 1), never through grey: the clip's white after the punch fading back to the stage by +50. The white negative's own Frames (38) end it should the script not get there |

The wash set (INVERT_FX off only) moved to 0x0341006A (the rings' id); the fade quad is killed by `ts_fx_off` on any
exit like the wash and the glass.

### Not confirmed in game
Alpha 140's look on his materials under each stage's light (g_fresnelOffset is added unscaled); the quad and
Screen_Invert's Mode 0 in the same frame (whether the quad's first frame draws on the frame it is made: a frame of the
pale negative darkened slightly, or a frame of the plain stage, at worst).

## Time Skip v8 (`supamario_timeskip_v8.zip`): the rotating square after the punch
Over v7, the same executable (**exe_v46z_outline**, model_commands_v1.zip). Changed: the ptcl only (the screen quads:
PtcSupamarioTsFade / Wash / Dark; effect.bin and the MSC build byte for byte as v7's, every other set too).

### The user's v7 test (clip 869ac5f5, 60 fps)
| | cause (evidence) | fix |
|---|---|---|
| "a rotating square appears during / after the punch" (new in v7) | 869ac5f5 156-171: as the white negative ends, a white square about 64% of the screen's width and taller than it, centred, its edges tilting a little more each frame, fading out over ~14 frames: v7's white fade quad (`PtcSupamarioTsFade` 0x0341006B, Effect_Camera Mode 1, Size 2.2). Its emitter is cut from Btl_HitL's `flash_1` (as v3's darkening and v6's wash), which kept the converted flash's **random spin** (`rotation_velocity_random` Z 0.035 rad: up to 2 degrees a frame; v3-v7 zeroed `rotation_velocity` only) and **scale 1**, which is 1 unit across, not 2 (Effect_Camera Mode 1: a particle W wide is W x Size / 2 screen heights): 1.1 screen heights at Size 2.2, against a 16:9 screen's 1.78 | every screen quad (the fade, the wash, the darkening: `_screen_quad`) **2 units across** (`QUAD_W`: 2.2 screen heights at Size 2.2) with **no rotation of any kind** (init, random, velocity, random velocity), no camera offset, follow type 0, a plain billboard, its alpha keys the only animation. The wash (0x0341006A) and darkening (0x03410065) are used only with INVERT_FX off but had the same two faults |
| the outline "barely visible; more like lighting than an outline, on the inside of the model rather than outside, thin" | Outline_Set is the shaders' fresnel rim: it lights his own edge pixels, never past his silhouette | not changed here (a true outside outline is being built as an exe feature). Every outline command comes from `outline_on` / `outline_off` (`OUTLINE_CMD`, the OL_* values) in tools/supamario_timeskip_build.py: swapping the mode or command is those two functions; the test takes its expected commands from them (and runs outline_cave's simulation only for Outline_Set) |

## Ultra Instinct v1 (`supamario_ultrainstinct_v1.zip`, exe `exe_v46z_ui`): his gimmick
Install over Time Skip v10 (`supamario_timeskip_v10.zip`) with the package's executable (exe_v46z_ui = the user's
exe_v46z_outline4 + `tools/supamario_ui_cave.py`). Built by `tools/supamario_ui_build.py` (it runs Time Skip's build, which
runs the earlier moves'; their files are theirs byte for byte except the shared ones). Source: Dragon Ball Xenoverse 2's
Awoken Skill **1510_GKS_MGT "Ultra Instinct -Sign-"** (the user's files: `.ean` (+ `_F` / `_MAM`), `.bac`,
`_PLAYER.bcm` / `.bdm`, three `.cam.ean`; the SE bank `CAR_BTL_M1510_MGT_SE.acb`; `custom_skill.cus` / `aura_setting.aur`
and vfx/cmn from Time Skip's / the God Fist's work).

### What the XV2 files say (`tools/xv2bac.py`, `supamario_fakedeath_xv2.read_bcm_full`)
- **BCM:** five roots (#1, #4, #7, #10, #13: button 0x2000, PrimaryActivatorConditions 0x2000C80, ReceiverLinkID
  Ultimate / Awoken / Evasive) play BAC 4, 4, 4, 8, 0 (the same stance); #16 (conditions 0xD00) plays BAC 13 (empty).
  Each root has two children (no button, ActivatorState Attacking, ReceiverLinkID Supers): **PrimaryActivatorConditions
  CounterMelee** (0x1000; the first child also 0x400000, unnamed), **not CounterProjectile** (0x2000): BAC 5 / 6 (under
  BAC 4), 9 / 10 (under 8), 1 / 2 (under 0). They are reached through the stance's BcmCallback (Counters / Supers,
  DisableKiBlastLink) **30-152**.
- **BAC 0 / 4 / 8 (the stance and the transformation, EAN #0, 325 frames):** effect 846 at 10 (BTL_AURA: MGT_HENSIN01 on
  the chest + the Mgt_AuraStart ECF), BPE 316 at 20, skill SE cue 0 (Start_F22) at 22, **invulnerability type 6 30-151**
  (the counter window), the aura (BTL_AURA 848: the Ultra Instinct loops on the feet, knees, legs, pelvis, chest, head,
  arms, hands) at 40, invulnerability type 5 151-324, the camera 151-324, skill SE cue 1 (Success) at 205, at **220**
  the burst (851 MGT_HENSIN02 under him, 849 Mgt_AuraEnd ECF, BPE 10, BDM #187 0-damage push box 3.5 m) and the start
  effects off.
- **BAC 5 / 6 (the counter, EAN #1 from 151):** at 151 **BDM #0** (damage type 18, 0 damage, a 150-frame hold) in a 3 m
  sphere, invulnerable (type 0) 151-176, BTL_CMN 82 (Ski_Ref.emo + SKI_line: the vanish), CMN SE cue 96 "Vanish",
  Movement 156-176 (flags 0x80: to the target) with tracking, 2.5 m a frame 159-161, BPE 315, 82 again at 170,
  invulnerable (type 3) to the end, the **finisher BDM #2 at 280** (type 7 launch, hitstop 7 / 7, damage 10), skill SE
  cue 1 at 335, the eye glint (850) and the end at 350. BAC 6 adds function 0x2B (100) at 151. BAC 1 / 2 / 9 / 10 (the
  other roots') add a 14-hit rush (BDM #1, 5 each, 210-275) before the same finisher. The first root's (BAC 5) is used.
- **custom_skill.cus:** MGT (26510 / 1510, no paths) and MGQ (26511 / 1511: EAN / camera EAN `MET/1510_GKS_MGT/...`, SE
  `CAR_BTL_M1510_MGT_SE`), both ending `0f 01 03 00 02 00 7a 00 1e 00 01 00 04 07 b1 04` (read as u8 0x0F, u8 1, u16 3,
  2, 122, 30 (PUP?), 1 (CUS aura?), 0x0704, 0x04B1: not decoded with certainty). The transformed body colours (silver
  hair / eyes) are a race BCS part set that wasn't sent: **not done** (open: the race .bcs, e.g. HUM.bcs, and the part
  set / PUP / CUS aura entries).

### The gimmick (`supamario.c` ui_* / ss_*; the user's spec where it isn't XV2's)
| | |
|---|---|
| Trigger (the user) | his first actionable frame at **100%** or more: Wait, Walk, WalkBrake, Dash, Run, RunBrake, Squat, SquatWait, SquatF / B on the ground; Fall / FallAerial in the air. Never in tumble, hitstun, a shield, a ledge, landing lag or a move; not in hitlag. Once a stock (transformed until his KO) |
| Transformation | status **0x157** (Mario's F.L.U.D.D. shoot, unreachable since Fake Death / Sledgehammer), motion **0x231 SpecialLwLight**: XV2's EAN #0 0-229 in **60** frames (0-30 in 12, the window 30-151 in 18, the power-up 151-229 in 30: the burst at 57). **Intangible every frame** (2 frames renewed: nothing reaches his body, no grab); held still (in the air no gravity, Fall at the end). Effects: 846's emitter at 4, the aura (7 parts, following) at 13, the burst at 57; sounds Start at 9, Success at 51 |
| Its counter (XV2's) | the cave's shape (ShieldModule group 1) on in **XV2's window: frames 12-30** (BAC 30-151). A **fighter's own attack** on it (CounterMelee: projectiles / items pass through, he is intangible) -> at once (XV2's BAC 5): the vanish effect where he was, **beside the attacker** (9 units on his side of it, facing it; its height if it is off the ground: airborne), motion **0x235 SpecialLwHeavy** (EAN #1 151-176 in 8 = the vanish, 270-320 1:1 = the finisher), its game script: **the freeze box** at 0 (BDM #0: no damage, no knockback, the attacker held to the finisher: HitstopFrames 18, a 3 m sphere, Part 1) and **the finisher at 18** (BDM #2: **10%** = XV2's 10 taken as %, angle 35, BKB 80, KBG 75), intangible to 21, the end at 58. Transformed afterwards |
| Autododge (the user) | transformed, **Wait / Walk / WalkBrake / Squat .. SquatRv on the ground**, a **fighter's** attack (the cave finds the attacker in the fighters' hit records; projectiles, items, gimmicks, grabs and uncounterable hits hit him as normal): the hit does nothing (the cave's slot 43 returns 0) and status **0x158**: motion **0x241 SpecialAirLwLight** (EAN #1 151-167: the vanish), away from the attacker 2 a frame for 8 frames (16 units, energy 3, not off an edge), **intangible 12 frames**, actionable from 12 (func_39 / func_41), Wait at 16. The vanish sound / effect at 0. No cooldown |
| Secret Sensation (the user's, DBFZ's UI Goku) | transformed, the **grounded down special** (status 0x155; Fake Death otherwise; the air one is Sledgehammer as before): the stance (motion **0x245 SpecialAirLwHeavy**: EAN #0 0-40 in 18, 40-151 in 27: 45 frames), the counter shape on **5-30**, Wait at 45. A hit on it from **anything an opponent owns** (its attack, projectile / weapon, item; never a stage gimmick; grabs never reach a shield shape) -> **no attack**: intangible, he slides in his **spot dodge (EscapeN)** over **14 frames** (smoothstep) to **behind** the fighter to answer (the projectile's / item's owner): a grounded one -> 10 past it, 6 above its feet; an airborne one -> 10 past, 10 above; **into the background** on the way (his position's Z eased to -6 and back to 0 by the end: a sine); airborne at the end, facing it, Fall (actionable). Nobody to answer (an unowned item): the stance goes on (a whiff) |
| The look | the aura while transformed: BTL_AURA 848's pelvis, head and feet loops (following requests, EffectModule 0x2730, ended and made again every 30 frames from func_82); the vanish lines (BTL_CMN 82's SKI_line) at the counter / dodge / slide. Not done: the silver hair / eyes (the BCS), the ECF colour effects (846 / 847 / 849), XV2's camera and BPE screen effects, Ski_Ref.emo (the afterimage model) |
| Resets | TIMER_0-2 to 0 (and the aura ended) on Dead / Rebirth / Entry (func_82); the fighter's set-up clears the user variables too (a KO) |

### State (no new globals: 127 of 128; New_Var / VAR_* are all taken)
The user variables **TIMER_0-4** (`0x0F000320`-`324`, unused by every earlier script and the MSC; they count down by
themselves each frame, so values are written every frame or used as signals that expire): TIMER_0 the state (0, 1000
transforming, 2000 transformed: the cave reads > 1500), TIMER_1 / TIMER_2 the cave's dodge signal (4 frames; the attacker
right / left), TIMER_3 the aura's renewal clock, TIMER_4 Secret Sensation's mark (func_2282 tells it from Fake Death).
The statuses' own work: floats 6-9 the cave's (damage, side, X / Y to answer), `0x0100000B`-`10` the phases / counters /
the slide's ends; flags `0x21000010`-`12` the cave's.

### The native part (`tools/supamario_ui_cave.py`, docs/native-notes.md "Supamario's Ultra Instinct")
Mario's specializer slots 0 / 42 / 43 for kind 0x41 only (Mario, kind 3, untouched): the shape built at set-up (Marth's
way), the counter's record in 0x157 (fighters only) and 0x155 (owners of attacks / weapons / items, not gimmicks), the
autododge in slot 43 (the damage event's message has no known attacker fields: the attacker is found from the fighters'
hit records), slot 43's 0 in 0x157 (fully invincible) and in 0x155 once countered.

### How (files)
- `supamario.c`: `ui_*` (the transformation, its counter, the dodge, the trigger / resets / aura in `ui_tick` from
  `func_82`), `ss_*` (Secret Sensation); hooks `func_2253` / `func_2254` (pre), `func_2268` / `func_2272` (main),
  `func_2283` / `func_2284` (end), `func_2275` (0x158's exec, empty), `func_2265` / `func_2282` (0x155 main / end),
  `func_82` (ui_tick).
- `tools/supamario_ui_anim.py` (the four motions), `supamario_ui_build.py` (values, scripts, effects, sounds),
  `supamario_ui_test.py` (the MSC in rolloff_hold_test's interpreter + the files), `supamario_ui_cave.py` /
  `supamario_ui_cave_test.py` (unicorn). Sounds `0x0CB100A4`-`A6` (Start, Success, Vanish), effects `0x0341006C`-`73`.
- ModAnimNames.txt: SpecialLwLight / SpecialLwHeavy / SpecialAirLwLight / SpecialAirLwHeavy (Sm4shCommand v1.7.17).

### Tests
`supamario_ui_cave_test.py` **144 ok** (words, set-up vs Marth's, slot 42 by status / category / owner / side, slot 43
autododge cases incl. Mario untouched); `supamario_ui_test.py` **136 ok** (trigger statuses and refusals, the 60 frames,
the window, the counter's placement / motion / intangibility, the dodge's way / frames / refusals / no cooldown, resets,
Secret Sensation's stance / slide / ends / depth / Fake Death otherwise, the scripts, motions, effect sets); the earlier
suites (their "unchanged" checks now except the four slots), `msc_stack_check` 0 problems, `import_call_check` 0.

### Not confirmed in game / approximated
- The cave-built counter shape for kind 0x41 (Sephiroth's works in game); that a projectile on Secret Sensation's shape
  is recorded (Marth's counters projectiles in vanilla); the autododge's attacker lookup through the hit records (written
  at the collision, before the damage event: a static reading) and the 1-frame gap between the cave's signal and the
  dodge status (the hit itself always does nothing); MSC effect op 0x2730 as a following request; position Z writes for
  the slide's depth (the camera's side assumed +Z; the stage's collision ignores Z).
- XV2's damage 10 taken as 10%; the launch's angle / knockback chosen; the freeze box's hitstop as XV2's hold; the
  transformation compressed to the user's 60 frames (XV2: 229 to the burst); BAC 5 (no rush) chosen over the rush roots;
  Mario's EscapeN for the slide (the user's); no hair / eye recolour (no BCS), no ECF / BPE / camera effects.

## Ultra Instinct v2 (`supamario_ultrainstinct_v2.zip`, same exe_v46z_ui): XV2's length, the rush counter, the white body
The user's XV2 clips (the transformation, its counter; 5 fps sheets): the transformation runs XV2's own length, the
counter is XV2's rush, the body glows white while transforming, no camera change (the user), the autododge a sway.
- **Timing 1:1 (60 fps):** SpecialLwLight = EAN #0 0-324 (**325 frames, 5.4 s**; the clip's gold burst -> white body ->
  purple-white burst at 220 -> normal is ~3.7 s of it). The counter window is XV2's **30-151**; intangible throughout.
  Effects: 846 at 10, the aura at 40, the burst at 220; sounds Start at 22, Success at 205.
- **The counter = XV2's rush (BAC 1):** the BCM's five roots share their conditions; #1 / #4 / #7 play BAC 4 (children BAC
  5 / 6: the finisher alone), #10 BAC 8 (-> 9 / 10), #13 BAC 0 (-> 1 / 2: the 14-hit rush). The clip shows the rush (the
  hit counter to 15), and BAC 0 is the stance with entry 4's functions (0x5C / 0x21, the player's), so **BAC 1** (root
  #13's first child; BAC 2 adds only function 0x2B (100)). SpecialLwHeavy = EAN #1 151-499 (**349 frames**). He **stays**
  where he is, turned to the attacker; XV2's Movement to the target only **closes a gap** (frames 5-10, at most 12.6 a
  frame, to 9 units short of it: `ui_gap`). Game script (motion frame = XV2 - 151): the **freeze** at 0 (BDM #0: no damage,
  the attacker held to the first rush hit: hitstop 59, a 3 m sphere, Part 1); the **rush** at 59, 64 .. 124 (BDM #1 x 14:
  **1.2%** each, angle 361, BKB 15, KBG 0, each followed by `Hitbox_Hold(0, 2)` and preceded by `Hit_Log_Clear(0)`, 2
  frames each); the **finisher** at 129 (BDM #2: **8%**, angle 35, BKB 80, KBG 75). **Total 24.8%.** Effects: the vanish
  at 0 and 19, the aura at 25; sounds Vanish at 0, Success at 184; the white body to 199 (XV2's 350).
- **Damage rule:** Core Breaker's (the user's numbers against XV2's): its dive 10 -> 3%, its 9-damage hold hits -> 1.5%
  each, its 405 crash -> 17%; so the rush's 5s -> 1.2% each and the 10-damage finisher (a launch) -> 8%. For the user.
- **The white body** (XV2's ECF 846 / 847 / 849, not readable as data here: the clip's look): `Color_Overlay(255, 255,
  255, 200)` + `Outline_Shell(0, 190, 215, 255, 200, 2.5, frames, 4, 10, 0, 1)` (a light blue-white band added) from
  frame 10 to the burst at 220 (`Terminate_Overlays`, the shell's Alpha 0); the counter's from 0 to 199.
- **No camera** (the user): XV2's cam.ean / camera items aren't used; the test checks no camera command is in any of
  the scripts.
- **The autododge:** 4 units away over 4 frames (v1 16 over 8: the clip's sway almost in place), still intangible 12,
  the vanish lines. XV2's own dodge motion / afterimage and the dodge's manual counter are **not in the skill's files**
  (its BCM has only the stance, its counters and #16 -> BAC 13: the awoken button again while transformed: character EAN
  #0 0-10, skill SE cue 2 "BRD_GOK_UL2_SS1_End", function 0xE: the transformation's cancel).
- **Not done / open:** the silver hair / eyes (HUM.bcs arrived; no BCS reader yet), the aura's colours not compared with
  the clip's lavender (the converted emitters keep XV2's EMP colours; the ECF / aura_setting tint isn't applied), the
  manual counter after a dodge (its files).

## Ultra Instinct v3 (`supamario_ultrainstinct_v3.zip`, same exe_v46z_ui): the crash fix, the silver hair / eyes / mustache, the lavender aura
Install over v2 (only the files that changed). Built by the same `tools/supamario_ui_build.py` (now also
`tools/supamario_ui_model.py`).
- **The crash fix (the user: v2 crashed near the end of the transformation, ~5.4 s):** the aura's MSC requests
  (`ui_aura`) are EffectModule **0x2717** (id, bone, x, y, z, rx, ry, rz, size, 0), the vanilla following request the
  Kamehameha already uses in game, instead of v1 / v2's 0x2730 (+ colour), which no vanilla MSC uses: its first call is
  the frame the transformation ends. The test checks the four calls' ids / bones / offsets / sizes and that no 0x2730 is
  left. (MSC edit by the coordinating session.)
- **Silver hair, eyes, mustache and eyebrows** (`tools/supamario_ui_model.py`, every costume c00-c07): silver copies in
  model.nud (and metal.nud, with its own textures): `Mario_HairUI_VIS_O_OBJ` (the sideburns / back hair), `Mario_NohatUI_VIS_O_OBJ`
  (the full hair, only in the side taunt's cap-off frames 17-72), `Mario_EyeUI_VIS_O_OBJ` (both irises; its materials keep
  NU_materialHash, so the eye animations move it as his own), and 10 **overlays** of the mustache / eyebrows: that
  polygon (texture ..0B) is part of each of the 15 expression faces, so one overlay per distinct shape (mustache: FaceN's
  in 10 faces, Furafura's in 4, Damageflyfront's; eyebrows: 7), its triangles only, pushed 0.03 out along the normals
  over his own. Two textures added to model.nut (Supamario's own ids `0x40C10000 + 0x400 x NN` + 0 / 1, every existing
  texture byte for byte): the silver hair (also the mustache / eyebrows) and the silver iris (Mario's blue ring greyed:
  the shading tone to the main tone by brightness, the pupil dark grey, the highlight kept).
  - Visibility: the copies have tracks hidden all through in `material_anime_eyelid(_lang).pac` (after each of his
    own hair / full hair / eye tracks); the overlays follow their faces (the OR of their faces' tracks per motion; hidden
    in the entry / win / lose / display motions).
  - The swap (game scripts, Mesh_Visibility): at the burst (SpecialLwLight 220) and at the counter's white body's end
    (SpecialLwHeavy 199): his hair / full hair / eyes hidden and the silver hair / eyes shown (Persist 1), the overlays'
    overrides ended (they follow his expressions). His own look (the swap's overrides ended, the overlays hidden Persist 1)
    on Entry (EntryL / EntryR frame 0) and in Wait1 while not transformed (TIMER_0 <= 1500; Rebirth plays Wait1, so a
    respawn too); Wait1 applies the swap again while transformed (> 1500). A KO / the match end / a training reset end
    every override (the cave), and the MSC resets TIMER_0 on Dead / Rebirth / Entry. The side taunt shows the silver full
    hair in its frames 17-72 while transformed (Persist 0). New game scripts (no game script before): wait1, entryl,
    entryr, appealsl, appealsr (motion slots unchanged: no ModAnimNames change).
  - **The colour (approximated, flagged: the user wants XV2's exact silver):** `XV2_COLOURS` (one constant) holds HUM.bcs
    entries HAIR_ 5 (main 0.8 grey, shading 0.4) and eye_ 5 (0.8), all five RGBA colours as the file has them
    (`tools/xv2bcs.py`). The exact Ultra Instinct indices are XV2's **CUS aura table** row for the skill's CUS aura (1):
    bcs_hair_color / bcs_eyes_color, kept in the **game executable** (XV2 Patcher's CusAuraData), not in any file sent:
    `powerup_parameter.pup` entry 30 holds only stat changes (`tools/xv2pup.py`), `aura_setting.aur` only effect ids,
    part set 122 only an eye part of eye_ colour 1 (dark grey 0.11). Mapping onto Smash: HAIR_ colour 1 = the hair /
    mustache / eyebrow texture's flat colour (Mario's own hair texture is flat; the game's lighting shades it; colour 2,
    the shading tone, isn't baked in); eye_ colour 4 = the iris ring's bright edge, its dark edge x 0.5 (HAIR_'s
    shading / main); a faint blue-white sheen (x 0.96 / 0.99 / 1.05) on both.
  - `tools/xv2bcs.py` (HUM.bcs: the part sets / parts / colour selectors, the colour groups; selector reading confirmed:

`tools/supamario_ui_cave.py`, reproduced byte for byte by it; the package was rebuilt before the user received it:
exe_v46z_ui2 was its first build). The second build's changes (the status move, the aura, Sm4shCommand v1.7.18) are
in "v4, second build" below. The user's v3 report: the silver eyes / mustache always
showed and the hair never changed; no effects / sounds / white glow during the transformation; the autododge never
fired; Secret Sensation blocked a Killer Eye's shot (placed by Donkey Kong) without sliding; the aura splotchy purple.
- **Why the copies always showed (from the files):** every model in the repo has `model.moi` table 1's count = its
  `model.nud`'s mesh count (one entry per mesh, NUD order; table 2 the VIS names -> mesh index). v3 appended 13 meshes
  (47) and left the moi at 34. The game's visibility track binding / mesh walks use a per-model mesh count, and a new
  mesh's byte starts as "follow the animation, shown" (docs/native-notes.md "Supamario's Ultra Instinct" v4), so a copy
  the animation can't reach is drawn. v4: `tools/moi.py` gives every copy its entries (table 1: its prefix, bone, index,
  VIS 1; table 2 its name; hash = CRC32 of the prefix: the game's hash function isn't known, flagged); round trips
  Mario's moi byte for byte.
- **The look by the meshes' own state (no override entries):** Mesh_Visibility **Persist 2** (new, exe_v46z_ui2): the
  game's setter writes the mesh's own set state (0 hidden, 1 shown, 2 follow the animation), no entry in the 32-entry
  table, kept until written again. The copies' tracks now FOLLOW (the eye copy = his eyes' keys, the full hair's copy
  shown all through, the overlays their faces; all hidden in the entry / win / lose / display motions), and his own
  state hides them while untransformed: Entry and Wait1 (Rebirth plays it) write his own look (cap / sideburns / full
  hair / eyes 2, copies 0); the transformation, its counter's end and Wait1 while transformed write the transformed
  look. No sideburn copy any more (Mario_HairUI dropped).
- **The cap (the user's request):** from the transformation's start (**frame 10**, XV2's gold start under the white
  body) Mario_Hathead (his only cap mesh) and Mario_Hair (the sideburns under it) hidden, his own full hair
  (Mario_Nohat) shown; at the burst (220) the silver full hair (Mario_NohatUI) / eyes / overlays follow their tracks
  and his own are hidden, until Entry / a respawn (TIMER_0 reset) gives his look back. The side taunt while transformed:
  no cap to take off (its own cap-off animation untransformed is untouched: no side-taunt script any more).
- **The mustache's colour:** its overlays get their own silver (`silver_face`: the face texture ..0B's strand shading
  on the hair's grey range x `FACE_TONE` 0.62, approximated) instead of v3's flat 0.8 (drawn almost white by the
  face's material).
- **Secret Sensation's owner chain** (cave slot 42): `[acc + 0x6C] + 0x34`, else LinkModule link 0, followed through
  weapons / items to a fighter (4 steps, loop guard); none: the stance plays out as before.
- **The transformation's scripts:** the lookup by files is right (motion 0x231 -> `0x9221C757` -> a non-empty
  script in each sorted .bin; `supamario_ui_test.py` checks it for all four slots). Prime suspect, not confirmed:
  Mario's native code special-cases statuses 0x157 / 0x158 (F.L.U.D.D.: its scripts are the pump article's), so the
  transformation / counter / dodge statuses would bind no body scripts. **Not fixed in v4:** the diag build's S4UM /
  S4UB lines settle it; the fix then is the transformation in another status (0x155 with a phase). Until then the
  transformed look still comes from Wait1 after the transformation.
- **The autododge:** re-read statically (slot 43's search, TIMER_1 / 2 4-frame signals, ui_tick / ui_can_dodge): no
  error found; the diag's S4U3 lines (status, TIMER_0 as read, attacker found, uncounterable, result) say where it
  stops.
- **The aura:** hue 262 (blue-violet), saturation x 0.45 (`AURA_HUE` / `AURA_SAT`). Distortion: Smash 4's format has
  refraction emitters (shader_type 1, a distortion-map texture: the fireball's shimmer; docs/custom-effects.md), so an
  XV2-style heat haze can be added by cloning one into the aura sets: **not done in v4**, nor the splotchiness study
  (particle size / alpha / emission against the clip).
- Diag lines: docs/native-notes.md "Supamario's Ultra Instinct" v4.
- Tests: `supamario_ui_cave_test.py` **187 ok** (v4: the owner chain's 10 cases, Persist 2's setter / pass-through, the
  diag lines S4U2 / S4U5 / S4U3 / S4UV / S4UM / S4UB); `supamario_ui_test.py` **342 ok** (v4: the moi invariant,
  the follow tracks, the cap / silver scripts (Persist 2 only), the mustache texture, the script lookup per slot);
  `supamario_timeskip_test` 198 ok, `supamario_godfist_test` 566 ok, `supamario_sledgehammer_test` 141 ok,
  `supamario_corebreaker_test` 104 ok, `supamario_fakedeath_test` 47 ok, `msc_stack_check` 0 problems (the MSC
  unchanged since v3); `import_call_check` 0 on both exes; `fighter_texture_ids.py check` 0 errors.
- **Not done in the first build:** the Sm4shCommand rebuild, the 0x157 status move, the aura's distortion (all in
  the second build below).

### v4, second build (exe `exe_v46z_ui3`, Sm4shCommand v1.7.18)
- **The status move.** The transformation / its counter and the autododge are now MODES of status **0x155** (the down
  special's: Fake Death's / Sledgehammer's / Secret Sensation's, whose body scripts run in game), not Mario's
  F.L.U.D.D. statuses 0x157 / 0x158. TIMER_4 (`UI_V_SS`, `0x0F000324`) holds the mode: `ui_tick` writes 7 (the
  transformation) / 5 (the dodge) right before asking the status; `func_2252` (pre) / `func_2265` (main) / `func_2282`
  (end) dispatch on `ui_mode()` (above 5.5: the transformation, above 3.5: the dodge; Secret Sensation writes 3, Fake
  Death / Sledgehammer 0); each mode's loop renews it, its end writes 0. `ui_tick` asks neither while flag
  `0x2000004d` is on (`func_79` turns a special status into 0x83 / 0x10 then). The 0x157 / 0x158 functions are
  Mario's again (unreachable). The cave (`supamario_ui_cave.py`): slot 42 in 0x155 takes a fighter's attack only
  (XV2's CounterMelee) when TIMER_4 > 5.5, else anything an opponent owns; slot 43 in 0x155 returns 0 when TIMER_4 >
  5.5 (fully invincible) or the counter flag is on (`ui_isform`, TIMER_4 through get_float).
- **The evidence (exe, unicorn-checked by `supamario_ui_test.py --exe`):** the motion change's "use the linked
  object's scripts" byte (`+0x2E`, which makes `0x027606C0` clear every body agent when there is no link 0) is a
  per-MOTION flag, not a status one: `0x02C5E5EC` passes `MotionModule vt +0x1F4` (`0x02C639E0`: the motion table
  entry's halfword +6 bit 0x8) as vt +0x4C's r7. Kind 0x41's table (Mario's) has it only on 0x7C-0x87, 0x96-0xC2 and
  0x1BE-0x20F (catches / throws / thrown), none of SpecialLwLight / Heavy, SpecialAirLwLight / Heavy, EscapeN; the run-
  time build (`0x02EA9008`) only sets byte +4 and bit 0x80 from vl group 1. Mario's status-keyed code: `0x02F169D0`
  (his status-change callback, vtable `0x101393C4 + 0x14`): leaving 0x152 / 0x157-0x159 (Kirby, kind 8: 0x1EB-0x1EE)
  clears flag `0x20000078`, removes article 0 (the pump) and calls `[acc + 0xA0]` vt +0x1E4(0x1A) (an effect kind,
  the vanilla MSC's `EffectModule(0x2727, 0x1a)`); entering them does nothing; `0x02F11C0C` (the pump's placement)
  acts in 0x152 / 0x157-0x159; the pump weapon's own code reads its owner's 0x157 / 0x158. **None of it clears or
  rebinds body scripts**, so the v3 silence is NOT explained by the native code read; the move takes those statuses
  out of the question (the game runs nothing of its own on 0x155 for kind 0x41: unicorn-checked), and the diag
  build's S4UM / S4UB / S4U4 lines settle what is left. Open: the cause of v3's silent scripts, if 0x155 is silent too.
- **The autododge (problem 2):** nothing else depended on 0x158 (the cave never tested it for the dodge; `ui_tick`'s
  guard is now "not in 0x155's dodge mode"). Diag: **S4U4** `st t1 t2 mode left`, the 8 frames after slot 43 wrote a
  dodge signal (the script module's per-frame late update, hooked at its call `0x02E4133C`: his status, TIMER_1 / 2 /
  4 as float bits), and S4UM now carries the status (`[[r27 + 4] + 0x7264] + 0x48`).
- **The aura (the user: "splotchy and purple instead of a slightly blue purple glow with distortion").** What made it
  splotchy (`ptcl.emitter_params` of the converted sets): every emitter emits ONE particle every 9-39 frames (1-4 big
  sprites alive, each popping in and out), and the big ones are alpha-blended smoke (blend 0: the 64x64 cloud and
  16x32 flame textures, 3.4-5.5 units at the call's 1.75): a few opaque purple patches. `smooth_aura()`: intervals /
  `AURA_DENSITY` 6 (1-7 frames), alpha x the same ratio (the same coverage, spread over many overlapping sprites), the
  smoke ones x `AURA_ADD_ALPHA` 0.3 more, every emitter **additive** (shader 11 is drawn with blend 1 by 32 other
  emitters of the file); saturation x `AURA_SAT` 0.8 (x 0.45 came out white once additive), hue 262. **The heat haze**
  (`haze()`): Mario's fireball's refraction emitter (`PtcMarioFireball` `refraction1`, shader 7: the distortion pass,
  its 64x64 distortion map; Supamario's Kamehameha charge set carries copies, KameShimmer) appended as `UiHaze` to the hip / head /
  feet sets (the ones the MSC keeps on him): sizes 5.0 / 3.6 / 3.0, volume 2.0 / 1.2 / 1.0, life 30, one every 2
  frames, rising 0.03 a frame, strength x 0.45. Render: `tools/supamario_ui_aura_render.py BEFORE AFTER OUT.png`
  (fx_render's Smash 4 side for the colour; fx_render doesn't draw the distortion pass, so the haze's particles warp a
  checker behind: about 3 px at 12 px a unit). Against the user's XV2 clip (a thin blue-white / violet rim glow, a
  heat shimmer): ours is a soft glow round the hips / feet / head, no rim along the limbs (no XV2-style per-limb
  emitters are called: the MSC keeps three sets); approximated, for the user to judge.
- **Sm4shCommand v1.7.18:** Mesh_Visibility's help / example 8 (Persist 2) in Events.cfg (the caves' `--labels`
  regenerated `param_labels.json`, then `apply_param_labels.py`); NvTest 288 ok (every Mesh_Visibility example
  compiles and round-trips), 1 known failure (effect.bin's else-if chains, upstream).
- Tests: `supamario_ui_cave_test.py` **204 ok** (v5: the modes in slots 42 / 43, the old statuses nothing special, the
  diag S4U4 / S4UM's status); `supamario_ui_test.py --exe` **363 ok** (v5: the 0x155 modes (pre / main / loop / end,
  the mode renewed and cleared, flag 0x2000004d), the game's own link-kind decision for every UI motion, the pump's
  placement and the status-change callback never acting on 0x155 (and acting on 0x157 / 0x158: controls), the aura's
  intervals / blend, the haze emitters); `supamario_timeskip_test` 198, `supamario_godfist_test` 566,
  `supamario_sledgehammer_test` 141, `supamario_corebreaker_test` 104, `supamario_fakedeath_test` 47 ok;
  `msc_stack_check` 0 of 2483; `import_call_check` 0 on both exes; `fighter_texture_ids.py check` 0 errors; both exes
  reproduced byte for byte from the cave script.
